Motivation: 现有3D占用预测方法依赖域内标注和精确传感器先验，可扩展性和跨域泛化能力有限；而视觉几何基础模型虽泛化能力强，但缺乏度量预测、杂乱场景几何补全及城市场景适配等关键能力。

Method: 提出三个贡献：(i) 首个通用化3D占用框架；(ii) Segmentation Forcing（分割强制）技术，在提升占用质量的同时支持掩码级预测；(iii) 新视角渲染管线，推断新视角几何以实现测试时视角增强的几何补全。

Result: 在3D占用预测任务上超越所有视觉几何基线方法，并在两个城市占用预测数据集的三种输入设置下与域内自监督方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: OccAny 证明了无需域内标注和传感器标定即可实现通用化城市3D占用预测的可行性，为跨域开放场景的3D感知提供了新范式。

Abstract: Relying on in-domain annotations and precise sensor-rig priors, existing 3D occupancy prediction methods are limited in both scalability and out-of-domain generalization. While recent visual geometry foundation models exhibit strong generalization capabilities, they were mainly designed for general purposes and lack one or more key ingredients required for urban occupancy prediction, namely metric prediction, geometry completion in cluttered scenes and adaptation to urban scenarios. We address this gap and present OccAny, the first unconstrained urban 3D occupancy model capable of operating on out-of-domain uncalibrated scenes to predict and complete metric occupancy coupled with segmentation features. OccAny is versatile and can predict occupancy from sequential, monocular, or surround-view images. Our contributions are three-fold: (i) we propose the first generalized 3D occupancy framework with (ii) Segmentation Forcing that improves occupancy quality while enabling mask-level prediction, and (iii) a Novel View Rendering pipeline that infers novel-view geometry to enable test-time view augmentation for geometry completion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OccAny outperforms all visual geometry baselines on 3D occupancy prediction task, while remaining competitive with in-domain self-supervised methods across three input settings on two established urban occupancy prediction datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/valeoai/OccAny .

</details>


### [107] [BSViT: A Burst Spiking Vision Transformer for Expressive and Efficient Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23165)
*Hongxiang Peng,Dewei Bai,Hong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BSViT提出了一种双通道爆发脉冲自注意力机制（DBSSA），通过爆发脉冲编码增强表示能力，并采用补丁邻接掩蔽策略实现结构化稀疏，在静态和事件视觉基准上优于现有脉冲Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脉冲视觉Transformer（S-ViTs）受限于二进制脉冲编码的信息容量不足和全局自注意力带来的密集token交互问题。

Method: 提出DBSSA机制：查询用二进制脉冲编码，键用爆发脉冲编码以增强表示能力；值通路采用双兴奋/抑制二进制通道实现符号化调制；引入补丁邻接掩蔽策略限制注意力到局部邻域，实现结构化稀疏；系统集成爆发脉冲编码以提升脉冲级表示能力。

Result: 在静态和事件视觉基准上，BSViT在准确率上持续优于现有脉冲Transformer，同时保持竞争性的能效。

Conclusion: BSViT通过爆发脉冲编码和局部注意力掩蔽，有效解决了二进制脉冲信息容量受限和全局密集交互问题，实现了精度与能效的平衡。

Abstract: Spiking Vision Transformers (S-ViTs) offer a promising framework for energy-efficient visual learning. However, existing designs remain limited by two fundamental issues: the restricted information capacity of binary spike coding and the dense token interactions introduced by global self-attention. To address these challenges, this work proposes BSViT, a burst spiking-driven Vision Transformer featuring a Dual-Channel Burst Spiking Self-Attention (DBSSA) mechanism. DBSSA encodes queries with binary spikes and keys with burst spikes to enhance representational capacity. The value pathway adopts dual excitatory and inhibitory binary channels, enabling signed modulation and richer spike interactions. Importantly, the entire attention operation preserves addition-only computation, ensuring compatibility with energy-efficient neuromorphic hardware. To further reduce spike activity and incorporate spatial priors, a patch adjacency masking strategy is introduced to restrict attention to local neighborhoods, resulting in structure-aware sparsity and reduced computational overhead. In addition, burst spike coding is systematically integrated across the network to increase spike-level representational capacity beyond conventional binary spiking. Extensive experiments on both static and event-based vision benchmarks demonstrate that BSViT consistently outperforms existing spiking Transformers in accuracy while maintaining competitive energy efficiency.

</details>


### [108] [VISTA: Video Interaction Spatio-Temporal Analysis Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01391)
*Alejandro Aparcedo,Akash Kumar,Aaryan Garg,Dalton Pham,Wen-Kai Chen,Anirudh Bharadwaj,Aman Chadha,Yogesh Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA是一个面向开放场景、多实体多动作时空理解的开集视频交互时空分析基准，包含约1.2万视频-查询对，系统评估了11种前沿VLM，揭示了传统指标无法捕捉的时空偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准主要评估简单单动作视频、封闭属性集和受限实体类型，无法捕捉真实世界视频理解中多样实体间的自由多动作交互；同时缺乏系统性框架来分析模型在互补时空轴上的失败模式。

Method: VISTA将视频分解为可解释的实体、关联动作和关系动态，支持多轴诊断和关系、空间、时间理解的统一评估；整合多个数据集到统一的交互感知分类体系，构建约1.2万精心策划的视频-查询对。

Result: 系统评估了11种最先进VLM，基于分类体系分解聚合性能，揭示了传统指标掩盖的不足和显著的时空偏差。

Conclusion: VISTA是首个大规模、交互感知的VLM时空理解诊断基准，通过细粒度分类学驱动的诊断分析，为模型设计、预训练策略和评估协议提供指导。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for Vision-Language Models (VLMs) primarily evaluate spatio-temporal understanding on simple single-action videos, closed attribute sets and restricted entity types, failing to capture the freeform, multi-action interactions between diverse entities which characterize real-world video understanding. Furthermore, the lack of a systematic framework for analyzing model failures across complementary spatio-temporal axes hinders comprehensive evaluation. To address these gaps, we introduce VISTA, a Video Interaction Spatio-Temporal Analysis benchmark designed for open-set, multi-entity and multi-action spatio-temporal understanding in VLMs. VISTA decomposes videos into interpretable entities, their associated actions, and relational dynamics, enabling multi-axis diagnostics and unified assessment of relational, spatial, and temporal understanding. Our benchmark integrates multiple datasets into a single interaction-aware taxonomy and comprises ~12K curated video-query pairs spanning diverse scenes and complexities. We systematically evaluate 11 state-of-the-art VLMs on VISTA, and break down aggregate performance across our taxonomy to reveal shortcomings and pronounced spatio-temporal biases obscured by traditional metrics. By providing detailed, taxonomy-driven diagnostics on a challenging dataset, VISTA offers a nuanced framework to guide advances in model design, pretraining strategies, and evaluation protocols. Overall, VISTA is the first, large-scale, interaction-aware diagnostic benchmark for spatio-temporal understanding in VLMs.

</details>


### [109] [GEASS: Gated Evidence-Adaptive Selective Caption Trust for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01733)
*Zeshang Li,Shuoyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现直接给VLM附加自生成描述不仅不能减少幻觉，反而会降低准确率（如Qwen2.5-VL-3B在HallusionBench上下降近10个点）。通过构建GD-Probe诊断集，发现描述效用是逐查询（per-query）属性：同一描述对全局问题有益、对细节问题有害，机制是描述文本与图像竞争注意力。据此提出GEASS，一种无训练、仅logit级别、自适应门控模块，通过置信度门控、熵加权和证据提升阈值三个机制，在四个VLM和两个基准上优于标准推理和对比解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs存在生成不存在对象的幻觉问题。现有工作尝试将模型自生成的描述作为辅助证据来缓解幻觉，但这种方法可能反而降低准确率，其背后的原因和条件尚未被充分理解。

Method: 1. 构建GD-Probe诊断集，通过配对全局和细节问题分离问题类型的影响；2. 发现描述效用取决于查询类型，机制是描述文本与图像竞争注意力；3. 提出GEASS模块：基于logit层面的置信度门控、熵加权和证据提升阈值，无需训练或注意力访问。

Result: 在四个VLM和POPE、HallusionBench两个基准上，GEASS在单一固定设置下优于标准推理和对比解码方法，仅需两次前向传播且无需额外参数。

Conclusion: 描述效用是逐查询属性而非固定的，GEASS通过自适应门控机制有效利用这一特性，在无训练条件下提升VLMs的幻觉缓解性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) hallucinate objects that are not present, and a growing line of work tries to curb this by feeding the model its own generated caption as auxiliary evidence -- assuming that a caption, once available, is something to consume. We show this fails: naively appending a caption can lower accuracy rather than raise it, dropping Qwen2.5-VL-3B$^\dagger$ on HallusionBench by nearly ten points. To understand why, we build \textbf{GD-Probe}, a diagnostic set that pairs a global and a detail question on the same image, so that any difference in caption effect is attributable to the question alone. Caption utility proves to be a \emph{per-query} property: the same caption helps global questions and harms detail ones, through a single mechanism -- an embedded caption competes with the image for attention and pulls the model's evidence onto its own text -- whose sign is set by whether the caption \emph{covers} the queried content. Crucially, this regime is readable from quantities the decoder already emits, with no attention access or grounding. We turn this into \textbf{GEASS} (Gated Evidence-Adaptive Selective Caption Trust), a training-free, logit-level module that decides per query how much of the caption to trust, gating it by the clean path's confidence, weighting it by the entropy reduction it induces, and raising the evidence bar when the two pathways disagree. Across four VLMs and two benchmarks (POPE and HallusionBench), GEASS improves over both vanilla inference and contrastive decoding under a single fixed setting, adding only two forward passes and no parameters.

</details>


### [110] [GeoWorld-VLM: Geometry from World Models for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16713)
*Renjie Gu,Kaichen Zhou,Yan Luo,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoWorld-VLM通过从冻结的相机条件视频世界模型中提取几何结构知识，仅微调图像编码器和多模态投影器，将后投影图像特征与世界模型中间表示对齐，从而提升VLM的空间关系推理能力，在What'sUp和VSR基准上均提升约4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在语义识别上表现强劲，但在基本空间关系（如左、上、后、之间）推理上脆弱，原因是视觉通路在特征提取过程中压缩或丢弃了关键3D结构线索，导致语言模型接收到的图像表示不足以支持可靠的空间判断。

Method: 提出GeoWorld-VLM蒸馏框架，利用冻结的相机条件视频世界模型作为教师，将静态视觉输入转换为合成多视角空间信号；仅微调VLM的图像编码器和多模态投影器，使投影后的图像特征与世界模型的中间表示对齐；同时结合空间答案监督、师生特征对齐和原始VLM保留锚点进行训练。

Result: 在两种不同VLM架构上均观察到一致改进，在What'sUp和VSR基准上性能提升约4%，表明世界模型引导的视觉对齐能跨模型结构和空间推理数据集泛化。

Conclusion: GeoWorld-VLM通过增强视觉通路而非修改语言模型来提升空间推理能力，在保持原始模型语言能力的同时实现了有效的空间关系改进，且方法具有跨架构的通用性。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) achieve strong semantic recognition, yet remain brittle on elementary spatial relations such as left of, on, behind, and between. One cause of this failure arises before language reasoning begins: the visual pathway may compress or discard critical 3D structural cues during feature extraction, so the language model receives image representations that are already insufficient for reliable spatial judgment. We introduce GeoWorld-VLM, a VLM-side distillation framework that transfers geometric structure from frozen camera-conditioned video world models into VLMs. GeoWorld-VLM fine-tunes only the image encoder and multimodal projector, aligning post-projector image features with intermediate world-model representations while leaving the main backbone frozen. Given images, a prompt, and a sampled camera trajectory, the world-model teacher converts static visual input into a synthetic multi-view spatial signal. Training combines spatial answer supervision, teacher-student feature alignment, and a preservation anchor to the original VLM. Since the language model remains frozen, GeoWorld-VLM preserves the original model's linguistic capabilities while attributing spatial improvements to the enhanced visual pathway. To evaluate the effectiveness and generality of the proposed method, we apply GeoWorld-VLM to two distinct VLM architectures and observe consistent improvements across both backbones. GeoWorld-VLM improves performance by approximately 4 percent on both the What'sUp and VSR benchmarks, suggesting that world-model-guided visual alignment generalizes across model structures and spatial reasoning datasets.

</details>


### [111] [Appearance-Invariant Detection of Suggestive Motion via Laban Movement Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24488)
*Jaehoon Ahn,Jeonghan Kong,Moon-Ryul Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Laban运动分析（LMA）描述符的仅运动分类流程，从SMPL骨骼轨迹检测暗示性和露骨动作，在17小时视频数据集上达到68%（逻辑回归）和70%（随机森林）的SFW/NSFW二分类准确率，性能与基于外观无关视频的学习模型相当。轨迹迂曲度（间接性）在暗示性动作中达到峰值，可作为功能性向暗示性运动转变的可解释标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线多人3D虚拟环境中的内容审核日益自动化，但现有检测聚焦于图像、视频和音频，忽略了暗示性动作这一盲区。

Method: 基于SMPL骨骼轨迹，提取61维Laban运动分析描述符特征，使用逻辑回归和随机森林进行分类；同时提出轨迹迂曲度（路径长度与净位移之比）作为可解释指标。

Result: 在包含日常、艺术、暗示性和露骨动作的17+小时视频数据集上，逻辑回归和随机森林分别达到68%和70%的SFW/NSFW二分准确率，性能与基于外观无关视频的学习模型相当。轨迹迂曲度在暗示性动作中达到峰值。

Conclusion: 基于Laban的运动学描述符提供了一种轻量级、可解释的暗示性动作检测方法，每个决策可分解为有命名的理论依据特征。由于仅依赖姿态轨迹，审核可直接在虚拟环境的化身姿态上运行，无需外观数据。

Abstract: Content moderation in online multiplayer 3D virtual environments is increasingly automated, yet detection has focused on images, video, and audio, leaving suggestive motion a blind spot. We present a motion-only classification pipeline that detects suggestive and explicit movement from SMPL skeleton trajectories using Laban Movement Analysis (LMA) descriptors. On a dataset spanning everyday, artistic, suggestive, and explicit movement (17+ hours of video), a logistic regression trained on 61-feature LMA descriptors reaches 68% binary SFW/NSFW accuracy (70% random forest) under a leak-free evaluation protocol. At this level, our descriptor performs comparably to a learned video model trained on the same motion re-rendered as appearance-free video, a gray figure with no clothing, skin, or scene. The indirectness (tortuosity) of each joint's trajectory, measured as the ratio of the joint's path length to its net displacement, peaks at the suggestive tier, showing that the Direct-to-Indirect polarity of Laban's Space factor provides an interpretable marker of the shift from functional to suggestive motion. Ultimately, Laban-based kinematic descriptors offer a lightweight, interpretable approach to suggestive-motion detection: every decision decomposes into named, theory-grounded features. Because the classifier operates on pose trajectories alone, moderation can run directly on avatar poses in virtual environments, with no appearance data.

</details>


### [112] [Spatially Grounded Concept Bottleneck Models via Part-Factorized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04364)
*Dhanesh Ramachandram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种部分因子化的概念瓶颈模型（Part-Factorized CBM），通过冻结DINOv3视觉Transformer、前景门控、部件查询和可学习二维高斯先验，强制每个概念头只关注其名称对应的身体部位。在CUB-200-2011上，该方法在保持分类精度（88.85% vs 88.95%）的同时，将指向准确率提升16个百分点（52.6% vs 36.4%），并可在无需每幅图像标注的情况下达到88.6% top-1和约70%指向准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准概念瓶颈模型（CBM）的概念头可以自由关注图像中的任意位置，导致以某身体区域命名的概念头可能被其他区域的证据所满足，从而破坏了模型的可审计性和可解释性。

Method: 方法基于冻结的DINOv3 ViT，包含三个组件：(1) 可学习前景门控，抑制背景补丁；(2) 部件查询交叉关注补丁特征，312个CUB属性通过固定概念到部件映射，仅从对应部件token读取；(3) 可学习二维高斯先验（对数空间加性注入注意力logits），打破部件查询的排列对称性，均值初始化为数据集平均关键点位置，无需每幅图像的关键点监督。

Result: 在CUB-200-2011上，空间先验模型匹配全监督基线（88.85% vs 88.95% top-1），指向准确率提升16个百分点（52.6% vs 36.4%）。用PCA前景目标替代边界框监督并组合高斯先验，无需每幅图像监督，达到88.6% top-1和约70%指向准确率。0.5%训练集（约27张图像）足以初始化先验而无性能损失。无任何空间先验时，指向准确率崩溃至2.9%。

Conclusion: 部分因子化CBM通过构造性约束概念头的空间注意力，在不牺牲分类性能的前提下显著提升了模型的定位能力和可解释性，且只需极少量监督即可实现。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models (CBMs) predict a layer of human-named attributes before predicting a class, which makes their decisions auditable. On fine-grained recognition tasks the concept heads are usually free to attend anywhere in the image, so a head named for one body region can be satisfied by evidence on another. This work studies a part-factorized CBM that removes that freedom by construction. The method has three components built on a frozen DINOv3 vision transformer. A learned foreground gate, trained on DINOv3 patch features, suppresses background patches inside the part attention. A set of part queries cross-attends to patch features and each of the 312 CUB attributes is routed, through a fixed concept-to-part map, to read only from the part token its name implies. A learnable two-dimensional Gaussian prior, injected additively in log space into the attention logits, breaks the permutation symmetry among part queries; its means are initialized from the dataset-average keypoint location of each part, which requires no per-image keypoint supervision at training or test time. On CUB-200-2011 the spatial-prior model matches a fully supervised baseline (88.85% versus 88.95% top-1) while raising pointing accuracy by 16 points (52.6% versus 36.4%). Replacing bounding-box supervision with a PCA foreground target and combining it with the Gaussian prior removes all per-image supervision and reaches 88.6% top-1 at about 70% pointing accuracy. A keypoint-fraction sweep shows that 0.5% of the training set (about 27 images) suffices to initialize the prior with no measurable loss. Removing part identity entirely is the harder case: without any spatial prior, pointing accuracy collapses to $2.9\%$.

</details>


### [113] [Where, What, Why, and Importance: Structured Defect Grounding for Text-to-Image Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06113)
*Huaisong Zhang,Hao Yu,Yuxuan Zhang,Jiahe Wang,Xinrui Chen,Haoxiang Cao,Feng Lu,Wendong Zhang,Changqian Yu,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化缺陷定位（SDG）框架，将文本到图像（T2I）模型的诊断问题建模为结构化集合预测，通过（位置、类型、原因、重要性）四元组表示每个缺陷，并配套构建了SDG-30K数据集和SDG-Eval评估协议，以及一个从诊断到对齐的框架（利用VLM作为检测器，BoxFlow-GRPO将缺陷集合转换为空间奖励用于扩散模型对齐），实验证明该方法在结构化缺陷定位上优于领先的商业VLM，并能持续提升T2I对齐质量和局部图像精化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型生成的图像虽越来越逼真，但仍存在局部、细微且结构复杂的失败模式。现有密集反馈方法（如热力图）将诊断视为像素域回归，难以定位数量可变的缺陷并将语义原因绑定到每个具体缺陷上，存在表示瓶颈。

Method: 提出结构化缺陷定位（SDG），将T2I诊断建模为结构化集合预测，每个缺陷表示为（位置、类型、原因、重要性）四元组；构建SDG-30K数据集（30K图像，框级标注，覆盖4种现代生成器）和SDG-Eval评估协议；提出诊断到对齐框架：VLM作为SDG检测器，BoxFlow-GRPO将预测的缺陷集合转换为盒导出、重要性加权的空间奖励用于扩散模型对齐。

Result: SDG检测器在结构化缺陷定位上优于领先的商业VLM；SDG引导的奖励持续提升T2I对齐质量并支持局部图像精化。

Conclusion: SDG建立了一个统一的、实例级的接口，用于诊断、评估和增强现代生成模型。

Abstract: Despite generating increasingly photorealistic images, text-to-image (T2I) models still exhibit localized, subtle, and structurally complex failures. Diagnosing these failures requires instance-level feedback that answers where a defect occurs, what type it is, why it is defective, and its importance to overall image quality. While recent dense-feedback methods move beyond scalar supervision, their heatmap-centric representations still formulate diagnosis as pixel-field regression, making it difficult to localize variable-cardinality defects and bind semantic reasons to individual failures. To address this representation bottleneck, we propose Structured Defect Grounding (SDG), which casts T2I diagnosis as structured set prediction by modeling each defect as a (location, type, reason, importance) tuple. To make this formulation trainable and measurable, we introduce SDG-30K, a 30K-image dataset with box-grounded annotations across four modern T2I generators, together with a dedicated evaluation protocol, SDG-Eval. Building on this structured representation, we further present a diagnosis-to-alignment framework in which a Vision-Language Model (VLM) serves as the SDG detector, and BoxFlow-GRPO converts predicted defect sets into box-derived, importance-weighted spatial rewards for diffusion model alignment. Extensive experiments show that our SDG detector outperforms leading proprietary VLMs on structured defect grounding, while SDG-guided rewards consistently improve T2I alignment and support localized image refinement. These results establish SDG as a unified, instance-level interface for diagnosing, evaluating, and enhancing modern generative models.

</details>


### [114] [Skill-3D: Evolving Scene-Aware Skills for Agentic 3D Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07436)
*Haoyuan Li,Zhengdong Hu,Jun Wang,Hehe Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill-3D框架，通过自进化的场景感知技能（Scene-Aware Skills）解决3D空间推理中MLLM代理工具使用不充分和偏好偏差的问题。该方法将成功工具使用轨迹存入场景记忆并蒸馏为可重用技能，失败轨迹作为教训附加，形成记忆与技能库共同进化的循环。实验表明Skill-3D显著提升工具利用率（VSI-Bench上从39%到78%），并使Qwen3-VL-8B性能提升60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM代理在3D空间推理中工具使用策略单一，对所有场景采用统一工具使用方式，导致工具误用和偏好偏差，代理范式相比非代理策略仅有边际收益。

Method: Skill-3D包含三个核心组件：1) Scene Memory，记录代理工具使用轨迹；2) 场景感知技能蒸馏，将相似场景的成功轨迹聚合为可重用技能，失败轨迹作为教训附加；3) 自进化循环，当相似场景再次出现时注入对应技能指导代理，新轨迹的成功/失败进一步优化技能库和记忆。

Result: VSI-Bench上工具利用率从39%提升至78%；Gemini-3-Flash在MMSI-Bench上提升67%；Qwen3-VL-8B经代理后训练后在VSI-Bench上提升60%。

Conclusion: Skill-3D通过场景感知的自进化技能学习，有效克服了3D空间推理中代理工具使用的均匀策略问题，实现了正确且充分的工具利用，验证了场景自适应的工具选择策略对3D推理任务的有效性。

Abstract: This paper explores agentic 3D spatial understanding, i.e., MLLM agents performing 3D reasoning through tool use. Existing methods often misuse tools and exhibit biased tool preferences under 3D scenarios, leaving the agentic paradigm with only marginal gains over non-agentic strategies. We reveal that 3D spatial reasoning tasks are heterogeneous across scenes, while these agents apply a uniform tool-use strategy to all scenes rather than selecting tools according to the specific scene and task. To address this, we propose Skill-3D, a framework that learns self-evolving scene-aware skills. Specifically, Skill-3D identifies the task scene and records the agent's tool-use trajectory into a Scene Memory, where successful trajectories from similar scenes are aggregated and distilled into a reusable scene-aware skill, with failed ones attached to the skill as lessons. During training, once a similar scene recurs, the corresponding skill is injected to guide the agent, producing new trajectories whose successes and failures further refine the skill, forming a loop in which the memory and the skill library co-evolve. Experiments show that Skill-3D substantially improves tool utilization in 3D spatial reasoning (from 39% to 78% on VSI-Bench), driving the agent toward correct and sufficient tool use. For instance, it improves Gemini-3-Flash by 67% on MMSI-Bench. Furthermore, we conduct agentic post-training over skill-guided trajectories, which boosts Qwen3-VL-8B by 60% on VSI-Bench.

</details>


### [115] [CACR:Reinforcing Temporal Answer Grounding in Instructional Video via Candidate-Aware Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08436)
*Muge Qi,Rong Fu,Pengbin Feng,Xianda Li,Yu Cai,Yifu Guo,Shizhe Zhang,Simon James Fong,Lei Ma,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出候选感知因果推理（CACR）框架，用于解决教学视频中的时间答案定位（TAGV）任务。该方法通过视觉-语言预训练的候选选择算法（VBCS）生成K个候选片段，再结合拒绝奖励机制的时序逻辑推理模块和GRPO优化实现鲁棒推理，在六个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对无关内容敏感且视觉推理能力不足，难以处理语义复杂的问题和未修剪视频与短片段之间的长度不匹配问题。

Method: 1) Visual-Language Pre-training based Candidate Selection (VBCS) 算法高效生成K个候选片段；2) 时序逻辑推理模块，结合拒绝奖励机制；3) 使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 进行优化。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，以mIoU为指标达到最先进的性能。

Conclusion: CACR框架为长视频中基于推理的检索提供了新视角，有效解决了现有方法对无关内容敏感和视觉推理能力不足的问题。

Abstract: The task of temporal answer grounding in instructional video (TAGV), which aims to locate precise video segments that respond to natural language queries, is increasingly important for direct video answer retrieval. This task remains challenging due to the need to comprehend semantically complex questions and to address the significant length mismatch between untrimmed videos and short target moments. Existing methods often suffer from sensitivity to irrelevant content or insufficient visual reasoning capabilities. To tackle these limitations, we propose a Candidate-Aware Causal Reasoning (CACR) framework. Our approach first employs a Visual-Language Pre-training based Candidate Selection (VBCS) algorithm to efficiently generate K candidate segments, then applies a temporal logic reasoning module enhanced by a rejection reward mechanism and optimized via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for robust inference. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in terms of mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU), providing a new perspective for reasoning-based retrieval in long videos.

</details>


### [116] [CineDance: Towards Next-Generation Multi-Shot Long-Form Cinematic Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09639)
*Yuheng Chen,Teng Hu,Yuji Wang,Qingdong He,Zhucun Xue,Qianyu Zhou,Jason Li,Lizhuang Ma,Jiangning Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CineDance-1M数据集（大规模、多镜头、长视频、文本-音频-视频联合生成数据集），通过三阶段策展流程（多样化来源清洗、电影理论叙事解析、分层双模态标注）提升视频生成质量，并配套CineBench评估基准和CineDance基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频生成模型中，商业系统能生成电影级叙事，但开源模型受限于高质量训练数据的稀缺性，亟需高质量多镜头长视频数据集来弥合差距。

Method: 提出三阶段策展流程：i) 多样化数据来源与综合清洗；ii) 基于电影理论的叙事解析（将视频切分为平均24.2个连续镜头）；iii) 分级双模态（音频+视频）描述生成。构建CineBench评估体系，包含多样化提示集和六维人类对齐指标。基于LTX-2.3改进为CineDance模型。

Result: CineDance-1M数据集平均时长92.8秒，平均24.2个连续镜头；CineDance模型在单模态质量、音视频对齐、主体与环境一致性方面表现优异，验证了策展策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将为多镜头长视频的联合音视频生成研究提供坚实基础，有望加速该领域的后续研究进展。

Abstract: The fidelity and structural diversity of training datasets fundamentally determine the capabilities of video generation models. While commercial systems showremarkableabilitytogeneratecinematicnarratives, the progress of open-source models remains limited by the scarcity of high-quality training data. To bridge this gap, we introduce CineDance-1M, a large-scale, open research Text-to-Audio-Video (T2AV) dataset designed specifically for multi-shot, long-form joint audio-video generation. Averaging 92.8 seconds and 24.2 continuous shots per video, it provides configurable, structured annotations for both audio and video modalities. This exceptional quality is achieved through a rigorous three-stage curation pipeline: i) diverse sourcing and comprehensive cleansing, ii) film-theory-inspired narrative parsing, and iii) hierarchical dual-modal captioning. For a comprehensive assessment, we propose CineBench, featuring a diverse prompt suite and a six-dimensional, human-aligned metric system tailored for complex narrative audio-video evaluation. Furthermore, we adapt LTX-2.3 into CineDance, which demonstrates exceptional single-modality quality alongside precise audio-video alignment and robust subject and environment consistency, effectively validating our curation strategy and the high quality of CineDance-1M. We anticipate that this work will serve as a solid foundation for accelerating future research in multi-shot, long-form joint audio-video generation. Our project page is available at https://aliothchen.github.io/projects/CineDance/.

</details>


### [117] [An Improved Generative Adversarial Network for Micro-Resistivity Imaging Logging Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10200)
*Ahmed Faizul Haque,S. M. Riaz Rahman Antu,Saif Ahmed,Asadullah Hil Galib,Souvik Pramanik,Mohammad Ashrafuzzaman Khan,Mohammad Abdul Qayum,Mohsin Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的GAN成像测井图像修复方法，通过FCN生成网络、深度可分离卷积残差块、Inception模块、多尺度特征提取模块和空间注意力残差块，结合全局与局部判别网络，实现微电阻率成像测井图像缺失区域的高质量修复，平均结构相似度达0.903，较同类方法提升约0.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决微电阻率成像测井图像部分缺失的问题，现有方法在语义结构连贯性和纹理细节修复方面效果不佳，需要一种新的深度学习方法确保后续测井图像解释工作的顺利推进。

Method: 以FCN作为生成网络基础架构；添加深度可分离卷积残差块以学习和保留更有效的像素与语义信息；引入Inception模块增加多尺度感受野并减少网络参数；加入多尺度特征提取模块和空间注意力残差块，结合通道注意力机制；设计全局判别网络与局部判别网络，通过对抗生成过程逐步提升修复部分与整体图像的内容和语义结构一致性。

Result: 测试集五组不同大小缺失区域的成像测井图像平均结构相似性度量（SSIM）为0.903，较其他同类方法提升约0.3。

Conclusion: 该方法可用于微电阻率成像测井图像修复，在语义结构连贯性和纹理细节方面有显著改善，为成像测井图像后续解释提供了新的深度学习方法。

Abstract: An improved GAN-based imaging logging image restoration method is presented in this paper for solving the problem of partially missing micro-resistivity imaging logging images. The method uses FCN as the generative network infrastructure and adds a depth-separable convolutional residual block to learn and retain more effective pixel and semantic information; an Inception module is added to increase the multi-scale perceptual field of the network and reduce the number of parameters in the network; and a multi-scale feature extraction module and a spatial attention residual block are added to combine the channel attention. The multi-scale module adds a multi-scale feature extraction module and a spatial attention residual block, which combine the channel attention mechanism and the residual block to achieve multi-scale feature extraction. The global discriminative network and the local discriminative network are designed to gradually improve the content and semantic structure coherence between the restored parts and the whole image by playing off each other and the generative network. According to the experimental results, the average structural similarity measure of the five sets of imaged logging images with different sizes of missing regions in the test set is 0.903, which is an improvement of about 0.3 compared with other similar methods. It is shown that the method in this study can be used for the restoration of micro-resistivity imaging log images with good improvement in semantic structural coherence and texture details, thus providing a new deep learning method to ensure the smooth advancement of the subsequent interpretation of micro-resistivity imaging log images.

</details>


### [118] [MultiToP: Learning to Patch Visual Tokens to Mitigate Hallucinations in Video Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11792)
*Yuansheng Gao,Wenbin Xing,Jiahao Yuan,Kaiwen Zhou,Han Bao,Zonghui Wang,Wenzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MultiToP提出了一种多模态上下文感知的视觉标记修补框架，通过选择性替换不可靠的视觉标记来缓解视频大语言模型中的幻觉问题，在Vript-HAL上F1提升50.60%，在ActivityNet-QA上准确率提升18.58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大语言模型在视频理解中表现优异，但仍存在幻觉问题——生成的响应无法被输入视频忠实支持。

Method: 提出MultiToP框架，包含轻量级视觉标记修补器（Visual Token Patcher），通过预测标记级替换分布并选择性用动态全局补丁标记替换不可靠视觉标记；同时提出信息引导的排序校准（information-guided rank calibration），利用答案条件化的帧级信息线索指导标记替换，结合真实答案监督和稀疏性正则化实现局部视觉证据精炼。

Result: 在Vript-HAL上Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct的F1分数提升50.60%；在ActivityNet-QA上Video-LLaVA-7B的准确率提升18.58%，且推理开销可忽略。

Conclusion: MultiToP能有效减少视频大语言模型的幻觉，同时保持通用视频理解能力，且无需修改原始模型。

Abstract: Video Large Multimodal Models have achieved remarkable progress in video understanding, yet they remain prone to hallucinations, where generated responses are not faithfully supported by the input video. In this paper, we propose MultiToP, a multimodal-context-aware visual token patching framework that mitigates hallucinations by refining unreliable visual tokens before language generation. MultiToP introduces a lightweight Visual Token Patcher to predict token-level replacement distributions and selectively substitute unreliable visual tokens with a dynamic global patch token. To train the patcher effectively, we further propose information-guided rank calibration, which uses answer-conditioned frame-level information cues derived from the backbone to guide token replacement. Combined with ground-truth answer supervision and sparsity regularization, MultiToP enables localized visual evidence refinement without modifying the original model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MultiToP effectively reduces hallucinations on Vript-HAL with negligible inference overhead, improving the F1 scores of Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct by 50.60% over the vanilla model. Meanwhile, MultiToP preserves general video understanding ability, yielding an 18.58% relative accuracy gain on ActivityNet-QA for Video-LLaVA-7B.

</details>


### [119] [Wild3R: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting from Unconstrained Sparse Photo Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11894)
*Yuto Furutani,Takashi Otonari,Kaede Shiohara,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wild3R提出了一种前馈式3D高斯泼溅方法，用于处理无约束稀疏照片集合，通过构建包含多样化光照和瞬态物体的WildCity数据集，学习跨视角外观一致性并移除瞬态内容，在无需逐场景优化的情况下取得了与优化方法竞争的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3DGS方法难以处理真实世界照片集合中多样化的光照条件和瞬态物体，主要原因在于缺乏包含多视角、多种光照和瞬态变化的大规模训练数据。

Method: 构建了WildCity数据集（200个场景、170种光照条件、337,500张图像），并基于该数据集训练模型，学习以参考视图为条件的跨视角外观一致性，同时移除瞬态内容。

Result: 在真实照片集合上，Wild3R优于现有前馈方法，并取得了与基于逐场景优化的方法相竞争的结果。

Conclusion: 通过大规模多样化数据集和外观一致性学习，前馈式3DGS可以有效处理无约束场景中的光照变化和瞬态物体，缩小与优化方法的差距。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) removes the need for time-consuming per-scene optimization required by traditional 3DGS. However, existing feed-forward approaches struggle with real-world photo collections that include diverse lighting conditions and transient objects. In this paper, we present Wild3R, a feed-forward approach for unconstrained sparse photo collections. The main bottleneck is the lack of training data that provides multiple viewpoints, a variety of illuminations, and transient variations necessary for learning robust scene representations. To address this, we introduce the WildCity dataset, which comprises 200 scenes, 170 lighting conditions, and transient objects, resulting in 337,500 images in total. By leveraging the dataset, our model learns appearance consistency across viewpoints conditioned on reference views, while removing transient content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing feed-forward approaches and achieves results competitive with prior per-scene optimization-based methods.

</details>


### [120] [VOID: Defeating Unauthorized Mimicry in Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12263)
*Chunlin Qiu,Ang Li,Tianxiao Huang,Ruilin Gan,Yunjie Ge,Shenyi Zhang,Huayi Duan,Lingchen Zhao,Chao Shen,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有防御依赖欺骗性扰动来阻止潜在扩散模型（LDM）的未经授权模仿，但模型自身的恢复机制会消除这些扰动，导致身份信息重新浮现。VOID通过操纵LDM的内在随机性，采用两种新方式扰动扩散管道：放大潜在编码错误以破坏图像语义结构，以及抵消目标引导信号以抑制模型恢复能力，从而实现语义破坏，有效阻止未经授权的模仿。实验表明，VOID在24种防御方法中表现最优，平均FID从113提升至365，比最强现有防御提升223%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗未经授权模仿的防御方法依赖欺骗性扰动，但潜在扩散模型的固有恢复机制会消除这些细微扰动，导致个体身份在生成图像中重新出现，因此需要一种能够克服这一缺陷的新型防御框架。

Method: VOID通过操纵LDM的内在随机性，在扩散管道中引入两种扰动：1) 放大潜在编码错误以破坏图像的语义结构；2) 抵消目标引导信号以抑制模型的恢复能力，从而实现语义破坏，同时将扰动限制在人类不可感知的图像区域。

Result: 在24种最新防御方法、10种模仿攻击和5个数据集上的综合评估显示，VOID实现了前所未有的保护能力：平均FID从113提升至365，比最强现有防御提升223%。

Conclusion: VOID能够在不牺牲视觉效用的前提下，有效阻止潜在扩散模型对个体的未经授权模仿，安全性增益显著。

Abstract: While Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) have revolutionized visual synthesis, they are increasingly exploited for unauthorized mimicry of individuals. Existing defenses inject deceptive perturbations to steer the generated images toward irrelevant targets. However, this approach hinges on an ungrounded assumption: subtle perturbations can maintain their deceptive efficacy throughout an LDM's extensive generation process. In reality, the model's innate restoration mechanism will remove such perturbations and cause individual identities to re-emerge in the images generated.
  We propose VOID, a defense framework that overcomes this conundrum by manipulating an LDM's intrinsic stochasticity. VOID perturbs the diffusion pipeline in two novel ways: 1) amplifying the latent encoding errors to shatter an image's semantic structure, and 2) counteracting the target guidance signals to suppress the model's restoration capabilities. This results in a semantic corruption that thwarts any unauthorized mimicry. Notably, the security gain does not come at the price of visual utility, as VOID simultaneously manages to confine perturbations to human-imperceptible regions of protected images. Our comprehensive evaluation of 24 state-of-the-art defenses against 10 mimicry attacks on 5 datasets demonstrates VOID's unprecedented protection power: it increases the average Frechet Inception Distance (FID) from 113 to 365, a 223% improvement over the strongest defense to date.

</details>


### [121] [DepthMaster: Unified Monocular Depth Estimation for Perspective and Panoramic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12368)
*Pengfei Wang,Shihao Wang,Liyi Chen,Zhiyuan Ma,Guowen Zhang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DepthMaster是一个统一的度量深度估计框架，通过将全景图分解为重叠透视补丁、引入对应一致性损失（CCL）和虚拟投影相机几何先验，实现了对窄视场透视图和360°全景图的通用度量深度估计，在13个零样本数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法针对特定相机类型设计，无法统一处理窄视场透视和360°全景图的度量深度估计，主要面临两个挑战：透视相机与全景相机之间的几何差异问题，以及带有度量标注的全景训练数据稀缺问题。

Method: 1) 将全景图像分解为重叠透视补丁，统一输入为标准透视表示；2) 提出对应一致性损失（CCL）实现补丁无缝拼接；3) 注入虚拟投影相机作为几何先验，无需专用网络学习球面畸变，保持主干与标准Transformer设计兼容。

Result: 仅使用一个全景数据集混合训练，在13个多样化数据集上实现零样本SOTA性能，不仅超越通用方法，还超过了透视和全景领域领先的专用模型。

Conclusion: 通过将全景问题转化为透视问题、利用虚拟投影相机几何先验和对应一致性损失，有效解决了几何差异和数据稀缺问题，实现了跨相机类型的统一度量深度估计。

Abstract: While monocular depth estimation has achieved significant progress, achieving generalized metric depth estimation for both narrow field-of-view (FoV) perspectives and $360^\circ$ panoramas remains an unsolved challenge. Existing methods are often tailored to specific camera types and struggle to produce accurate metric depth that generalizes across diverse settings. This limitation stems from two key challenges: the inherent geometric discrepancy between perspective and panoramic cameras, and the scarcity of panoramic training data with metric annotations. In this work, we introduce DepthMaster, a unified metric depth estimation framework. Rather than employing specialized networks to learn spherical distortions, we reformulate the problem by decomposing panoramic images into overlapping perspective patches. Crucially, distinct from prior projection-based methods that rely on ad-hoc architectural modifications to handle boundaries, we introduce a novel Correspondence Consistency Loss (CCL) and inject virtual projection cameras as geometric priors, allowing us to seamlessly stitch the patches while avoiding specialized operators and keeping the backbone largely compatible with standard Transformer designs. This strategy also resolves the geometric differences by unifying all inputs into a canonical perspective representation, and effectively circumvents data scarcity by directly unlocking powerful metric priors from vast perspective datasets. Trained on a mixed dataset that contains only one panorama dataset, DepthMaster achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on 13 diverse datasets, outperforming not only universal methods but also leading specialist models in both perspective and panoramic domains.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将token级幻觉检测重新定义为最快变化检测问题，并建立马尔可夫模型获得理论下限。实验表明因果循环标注器（CUSUM变体）检测延迟为11-13 tokens，显著优于线性基线（31 tokens），并通过信息率最优性定理解释了与理论下限（1.3 tokens）之间存在数量级差距的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token级幻觉检测器以AUC等分类指标评估，但流式监控场景更关注检测延迟（从幻觉发生到告警的token数）。分类指标掩盖了延迟结构，需要新的评估框架。

Method: 1. 将幻觉检测形式化为最快变化检测问题；2. 基于RAGTruth验证一阶马尔可夫模型；3. 使用Lorden下界推导理论最小延迟；4. 将因果循环标注器解释为CUSUM算法（带学习增量）；5. 通过受控分解分析优势来源；6. 应用Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优性定理解释性能差距。

Result: 1. 理论下界：在虚警率0.01时约1.3 tokens；2. 因果循环标注器检测延迟11-13 tokens；3. 线性per-token基线为31 tokens；4. 优势主要来自更好的per-token评分（而非时间累积）；5. 学习评分仅实现特征携带散度的1/4.5，剩余差距来自有限视界效应。

Conclusion: 分类指标（如AUC）掩盖了延迟结构，序贯分析使延迟可测量。因果循环标注器性能优于线性基线，但与理论下限仍有数量级差距，主要受限于评分质量而非累积机制。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

</details>


### [123] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出玻尔兹曼注意力机制，通过伊辛模型引入可学习的成对耦合项，显式建模注意力头中位置间的相互作用，在字符级语言建模和括号匹配任务上超越标准softmax注意力，并展示了量子退火进行训练的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制仅通过单个查询-键相似度计算相关性，无法显式建模注意力决策之间的协同或竞争关系，缺乏对注意力内部位置间相互作用的参数化表达能力。

Method: 将注意力模式建模为伊辛模型（能量基模型），在标准数据相关的局部场基础上加入可学习的成对耦合项，通过玻尔兹曼分布计算注意力权重，支持精确计算与量子退火采样两种训练方式。

Result: 在字符级语言建模和合成括号匹配任务中，玻尔兹曼注意力持续优于标准softmax注意力，优势随序列长度增加而加大；四路消融实验证实改进源于可学习成对耦合。

Conclusion: 显式位置间相互作用为基于注意力的序列建模提供了有原则的增强，且伊辛公式化为量子计算采样策略开辟了自然路径。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

</details>


### [124] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReCal框架，通过层次化奖励分解和分布感知优化策略，解决基于强化学习的LLM路由中奖励信号模糊和优化偏差问题，在7个数据集上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RL的LLM路由方法在异构任务下面临两个关键问题：(1) 多目标（如正确性、格式行为）被聚合为单一标量奖励，导致信用分配模糊和优化冲突；(2) 不同实例的奖励信号差异大，简单样本的高奖励引入优化偏差，使模型偏向琐碎样本而非信息性样本。

Method: 提出ReCal框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 层次化奖励分解机制，将奖励分解为多个分量并做分量级优势估计；(2) 分布感知优化策略，通过方差感知重加权和每数据集归一化来校准优化变异性。

Result: 在7个数据集上的实验表明，ReCal一致地提升了路由性能和训练稳定性，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过奖励分解和分布感知校准，有效解决了RL-based LLM路由中的信用分配和优化偏差问题，提升了路由质量和训练稳定性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

</details>


### [125] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出T2SP方法，将时间序列转化为结构化符号程序表示，使预训练LLM无需微调即可高效进行时间序列推理，在编辑、标注和问答三项任务上优于原始数值序列表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列属于非文本模态，而LLM原生处理文本/代码，存在模态不匹配问题。现有方法（序列化数值或微调）将提取时序结构的负担直接放在LLM上，导致长序列性能下降和计算开销大。

Method: T2SP是一种确定性、无需训练的方法，将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和显著事件，并以结构化符号程序的形式表达，使其与LLM原生训练所基于的文本和代码模态对齐。

Result: 在编辑、标注和问答三项推理任务上，相比原始字符串表示，T2SP一致提升了性能，减少了推理时间，并降低了失败率。

Conclusion: T2SP通过将时序结构提取从模型转移到表示本身，为时间序列与LLM之间提供了有效的接口，使现成的LLM能够利用其原有推理能力进行时间序列理解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

</details>


### [126] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），通过基于查询点与支持点之间的平方距离，解决了Christoffel函数在高维数据中矩阵求逆的指数级复杂度问题，在ADBench基准上超越14个SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Christoffel函数方法在异常检测中具有坚实的数学基础和计算高效性，但其矩阵求逆的规模随数据维度呈指数增长，即使对中等维度数据集也难以处理。本文旨在解决这一维度可扩展性瓶颈，同时保留该方法的理论优势。

Method: 提出UCF（Univariate Christoffel Function），基于查询点与支持点之间的平方距离构建单变量形式的Christoffel函数，避免了高维矩阵求逆的指数级复杂度。

Result: 在ADBench基准上的大量实验表明，UCF在平均精度（Average Precision）指标上一致优于14个最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: UCF解决了Christoffel函数的可扩展性瓶颈，为异常检测工具箱增添了一种鲁棒、理论扎实且普遍适用的方法，同时保留了关键的on-off支持二象性行为和准确的支持形状捕获能力。

Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

</details>


### [127] [Extracting Governing Equations from Latent Dynamics via Multi-View Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13260)
*Paolo Muratore,Mackenzie Weygandt Mathis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DYSCO是一种多视图时间对比学习算法，能从带噪声的高维观测中联合恢复潜在轨迹和动力学方程，在仿射不确定范围内提供可识别性理论保证，并在多种动力系统（混沌、振荡、亚稳态）及噪声类型（高斯、泊松）下验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从带噪声的高维观测数据中识别潜在动力系统是表示学习、系统辨识和科学发现的核心问题，现有方法在处理非线性噪声观测时缺乏理论保证。

Method: 提出多视图时间对比学习算法DYSCO，利用同一底层过程的多个独立带噪声视图来分离信号与噪声；将动力学参数化在结构化函数基中，实现仿射规范下控制方程的符号恢复。

Result: 在混沌、振荡和亚稳态等多样动力学场景下，对高斯和泊松观测噪声均准确恢复了潜在轨迹和流场；泊松噪声场景尤其适用于神经记录数据。

Conclusion: DYSCO在带噪声非线性观测的现实场景中实现了强可识别性（仿射不确定范围内），扩展了先前的可识别性理论成果。

Abstract: Identifying latent dynamical systems from noisy, high-dimensional measurements is a central problem at the intersection of representation learning, system identification, and scientific discovery. We present DYSCO, a multi-view temporal contrastive learning algorithm that jointly recovers latent trajectories and the governing dynamics from such observations, by leveraging multiple independent noisy views of the same underlying process to disentangle signal from noise. By parameterizing the dynamics in a structured functional basis, our framework further enables symbolic recovery of the governing equations within an affine gauge. We offer theoretical guarantees for strong identification up to an affine indeterminacy, extending prior identifiability results to the realistic setting of noisy nonlinear observations. Empirically, we demonstrate accurate recovery of both latent trajectories and flow fields across a diverse set of dynamical regimes (e.g., chaotic, oscillatory, and metastable) under both Gaussian and Poisson observation noise, the latter being particularly relevant for neural recordings.

</details>


### [128] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“推测性回滚修正”（SRC）的分支级模仿学习框架，通过固定视野分支审查和回滚机制，在交互式网页代理训练中平衡专家干预时机，有效缓解早期错误累积和过度依赖专家策略的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于专家轨迹的交互式网页代理模仿学习中，专家干预时机的选择是关键挑战：延迟干预导致早期错误累积使页面状态不可恢复，过早或过度干预则使代理过度依赖专家策略，陷入单一刚性轨迹的局部最优。

Method: SRC采用固定视野分支审查：学生先执行一段短推测性分支，教师在局部进度中断时定位首个有害偏差；通过回滚保留有用前缀，成功的rollout经硬验证器过滤后存入轻量级质量多样性档案。收集的数据同时支持基于定位修正和验证通过轨迹的下一步监督微调。

Result: 在WebArena-Infinity上，SRC收集了977条验证通过轨迹和9,183个下一步动作示例；固定视野审查在恢复性与查询次数的权衡上优于步级审查，同时保留了验证通过的方案多样性。

Conclusion: SRC通过分支级审查和回滚机制，在专家干预时机上实现了更优的权衡，既避免了早期错误累积，又防止了过度依赖专家策略，同时保留了解决方案的多样性。

Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive. The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

</details>


### [129] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于条件的预测性维护（PdM）方法论，假设被监控部件的磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列，通过选取最近观测值并转换为表格格式，使用机器学习模型进行分类，在Scania Component X数据集上相比现有最先进方法降低了成本，并通过AutoML简化了建模过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车车队的预测性维护虽日益重要，但面临数据量大、故障检测复杂以及实现成本效益平衡困难等挑战，现有方法难以有效解决这些问题。

Method: 假设部件磨损状态为单调非递减时间序列，仅选取最近观测值并将其转换为表格格式，利用针对表格数据设计的机器学习分类模型，并通过AutoML简化建模过程。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上，该方法相比现有最先进（SOTA）方法降低了成本，同时简化了建模流程。

Conclusion: 所提出的条件性预测性维护方法论能够有效降低维护成本，且通过AutoML降低了建模复杂度，为卡车车队预测性维护提供了一种更简单、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

</details>


### [130] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示大语言模型在W4A4KV4量化中，激活值的剧烈波动呈现跨层相位式变化模式（出现与消失），导致静态变换平滑方法失效。据此提出DynamicPTQ策略，通过动态识别量化敏感层并仅对这些层使用8位激活精度，在保持4位权值/KV缓存的同时，提升困惑度和零样本QA性能，并实现1.05-1.07倍吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化方法（如正交旋转、仿射缩放）主要依赖静态变换平滑来缓解极端激活值问题，但忽略了残差流中跨层的动态变化。本文发现极端激活值呈相位式出现和消失，导致残差变化剧烈，使得静态方法无法完全解决动态量化不稳定性。

Method: 提出DynamicPTQ动态后训练量化策略：(1) 引入Jump Ratio和Historical Feature SNR来刻画激活的相位式行为；(2) 根据残差流动态识别量化敏感层；(3) 仅对这些敏感层使用8位激活精度，其余保持W4A4KV4量化；(4) 可直接集成到QuaRot、SpinQuant、FlatQuant等基线方法中。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上，DynamicPTQ在W4A4KV4量化下持续提升困惑度和零样本QA性能，同时实现1.05-1.07倍吞吐量提升，内存开销适中。

Conclusion: 动态相位感知的混合精度量化策略能有效解决残差流跨层动态变化引起的量化不稳定问题，为鲁棒的低比特大模型推理提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision. A key difficulty lies in massive activations, whose extreme values dominate the activation range and amplify quantization errors. State-of-the-art methods mainly mitigate massive activations through transformation-based smoothing, such as orthogonal rotations and affine scaling, but overlook the cross-layer dynamics of the residual stream. In this paper, we show that massive activations emerge and disappear in a phase-wise pattern across network depth, triggering large residual changes. These changes cause newly injected layer-wise updates to dominate the 4-bit quantization scale and weaken historical residual information. To characterize this behavior, we introduce Jump Ratio and Historical Feature SNR. This suggests that static transformation-based smoothing cannot fully resolve dynamic quantization instability caused by cross-layer residual changes. Based on this analysis, we propose DynamicPTQ, a Dynamic Post-Training Quantization policy for phase-aware mixed-precision activation quantization. DynamicPTQ identifies quantization-sensitive layers from residual-stream dynamics and assigns 8-bit activation precision only to these layers, while keeping weights, KV caches, and other activations in 4-bit precision. It can be directly integrated with strong PTQ baselines such as QuaRot, SpinQuant, and FlatQuant. Experiments on LLaMA-2 and LLaMA-3 show that DynamicPTQ consistently improves perplexity and zero-shot QA performance under W4A4KV4 quantization, while achieving 1.05 to 1.07 times throughput improvement with modest memory overhead. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust low-bit LLM inference.

</details>


### [131] [A Stationary (and Therefore Compatible) Representation is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12488)
*Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Simone Ricci,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示具有兼容性，并提出通过交叉熵损失与对比损失的凸组合来捕捉高阶依赖，从而实现模型更新时的无缝检索服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决模型更新时特征表示不兼容的问题，使得新旧模型学到的特征可以互换使用，避免在模型更新后需要重新处理所有已存储的特征（如图库图像）。

Method: 1) 理论上证明d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示满足兼容性定义；2) 提出将交叉熵损失与对比损失进行凸组合训练，以捕捉表示间的高阶依赖关系；3) 证明该组合在兼容性约束下等价于纯交叉熵学习。

Result: 实验在模型顺序微调和模型替换场景下，平稳表示实现了无需重新处理图库图像的不间断检索服务，同时提升了性能，达到了最先进水平。

Conclusion: d-Simplex固定分类器结合交叉熵与对比损失的凸组合，能够学习兼具兼容性和高阶依赖的表示，适用于持续学习和模型更新的实际场景。

Abstract: Learning compatible representations aims to learn feature representations that can be used interchangeably over time whenever a model undergoes updates. In this paper, we demonstrate that stationary representations learned by d-Simplex fixed classifiers imply compatibility as in its formal definition. This result establishes a foundation for future works and can be directly exploited in practical learning scenarios. We address the challenge of learning compatibility using $d$-Simplex fixed classifiers when the model is sequentially fine-tuned. Learning according to a d-Simplex fixed classifier with the cross-entropy loss aligns feature distributions at the first-order statistics. Consequently, it may not fully capture higher-order dependencies in the representation between model updates. To address this issue, we demonstrate that training the model using a $d$-Simplex fixed classifier through a convex combination of the cross-entropy loss and a contrastive loss not only captures higher-order dependencies, but is also equivalent to learning with the cross-entropy under the compatibility constraints. We confirm our findings with extensive experiments also considering a new scenario where a pre-trained model is sequentially fine-tuned and occasionally replaced with an improved model. We show that stationary representations enable uninterrupted retrieval services (without reprocessing gallery images) while improving performance during model updates and replacements, achieving state-of-the-art. Code at https://github.com/miccunifi/iamcl2r.

</details>


### [132] [Robustness Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks with Abstraction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12490)
*Li-Jen Lin,Chih-Duo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向循环神经网络（RNN）的抽象-细化验证框架，通过SHAP引导的时间步选择策略对激活函数区间进行划分，在控制组合成本的同时显著提升认证局部鲁棒性的成功率和紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN的局部鲁棒性验证面临挑战：非线性松弛引入的近似误差会通过循环连接随时间累积传播，导致线性界传播方法过于保守，尤其在预激活区间跨越零点时难以认证实际鲁棒的输入。

Method: 提出抽象-细化框架：1) 对跨越零点的预激活区间进行划分，使ReLU精确化，tanh/sigmoid获得更紧的线性包络；2) 引入SHAP指导的时间步选择策略，按隐藏状态对验证目标的贡献排序，仅细化最关键的时间步。

Result: 在CIFAR10和MNIST笔画基准上，相比纯抽象基线方法，验证成功率和鲁棒性边界紧致性均取得一致提升，同时揭示了ReLU与tanh模型之间的运行时权衡。

Conclusion: 所提抽象-细化框架能有效缓解RNN验证中的累积误差问题，SHAP引导的选择策略在控制计算成本的同时提升验证性能，但不同激活函数下的运行时特性存在差异。

Abstract: Certified local robustness verification for recurrent neural networks (RNNs) is challenging because approximation errors introduced by nonlinear relaxations can propagate through recurrent connections and accumulate over time. As a result, scalable linear bound propagation methods often become overly conservative and fail to certify inputs that are in fact robust, especially when many pre-activation intervals cross zero. We propose an abstraction-refinement framework for RNN verification that partitions such intervals to remove the dominant relaxation error: on each refined branch, ReLU becomes exact, and smooth activations such as tanh and sigmoid admit substantially tighter linear envelopes. To control the combinatorial cost of splitting in long sequences, we introduce a SHAP-guided timestep selection strategy that ranks hidden states by their contribution to the verification objective and refines only the most critical timesteps in temporal order. Experiments on CIFAR10 and MNIST stroke benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in verification success and robustness-margin tightness over abstraction-only baselines, while exposing clear runtime trade-offs between ReLU and tanh models.

</details>


### [133] [The Urysohn Machine: A Metric-Topological Model of Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.14143)
*Xin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Urysohn Machine，一种以度量分离、前沿结构和收缩为计算状态的分类计算模型。其核心是Urysohn Triple（支撑区域、目标划分和可重用度量库中的分类器），通过构造性Urysohn实现定理构建二分梯度的嵌套多面体分离器，并建立前沿的链级演算。论文证明了平摊分离定理，引入对比分离算子，并给出动态Urysohn梯度的四个保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类计算模型主要基于符号描述，隐藏了几何结构。作者希望建立一个将度量分离、前沿结构和收缩显式作为计算状态的形式化框架，以揭示分类复杂度、平摊推理和组合复用的度量-拓扑本质。

Method: 1) 定义Urysohn Triple作为基本对象；2) 提出构造性Urysohn实现定理，用二分梯度的嵌套多面体区域构建分离器；3) 建立前沿的链级演算（前沿是链，壳层边界由前沿差给出）；4) 定义两种复杂度度量：决策边界宽度和Urysohn宽度；5) 证明平摊分离定理；6) 引入对比分离算子，其图割泛函一致估计决策边界宽度，拉普拉斯谱认证类分量结构和导率；7) 分析动态Urysohn梯度并证明四个保证。

Result: 平摊分离定理：在显式边界足迹假设下，逼近宽度为w的边界到精度ε所需简单基三元的数量与边界宽度成正比，与分辨率成反比。对比分离算子的图割泛函能一致估计决策边界宽度。动态Urysohn梯度满足：商坍缩下的可分离性、承诺前沿的稳定性、收缩下的有界容量、商距离下的可扩展性。

Conclusion: Urysohn Machine为分类复杂度、平摊推理和组合复用提供了一个度量-拓扑形式化框架，在保持经典可计算性的同时，揭示了被纯符号描述隐藏的几何结构。

Abstract: We introduce the Urysohn Machine, an effective model of classification-oriented computation in which metric separation, frontier structure, and contraction are explicit parts of the computational state. Its basic object is a \emph{Urysohn Triple}: a support region, a target partition, and a separating classifier stored in a reusable Metric Library. The topological foundation is a constructive Urysohn Realization theorem for finite simplicial settings. It builds separators from dyadic ladders of nested polyhedral regions and equips their frontiers with a chain-level calculus: frontiers are cycles, and shells between levels have boundaries given by differences of frontiers. This construction yields two related complexity measures: decision-boundary width, the geometric measure of a single classifier's boundary, and Urysohn width, the total frontier mass represented by a library or realization. We prove an Amortized Separation Theorem showing that approximating a boundary of width to accuracy requires a number of simple basis triples proportional to boundary width and inversely proportional to resolution, under explicit boundary-footprint assumptions. We also introduce a contrastive separation operator whose graph-cut functional consistently estimates decision-boundary width from sampled metric data, while its Laplacian spectrum certifies class-component structure and conductance. Finally, we analyze the dynamic Urysohn ladder and prove four guarantees: separability under quotient collapse, stability of committed frontiers, bounded capacity under contraction, and scalability with quotient distance. Together, these results give a metric-topological account of classification complexity, amortized inference, and compositional reuse that preserves classical computability while exposing geometric structure hidden by purely symbolic descriptions.

</details>


### [134] [Net-Ev$^2$: A Generative Simulator for Network Event Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12494)
*Guangyu Wang,Zhaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Net-Ev$^2$，一种联合事件语义与网络拓扑结构的生成式模拟器，用于模拟扰动事件在网络中的传播演化。框架包括结构引导的掩码预训练和拓扑感知的扩散过程，采用U-Net风格图上下采样。同时引入多模态基准Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M和新拓扑评估指标JL-MMD，实验表明达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法同时建模事件的结构化属性和非结构化语义，也无法捕捉拓扑结构以模拟网络事件演化。因此需要一种能联合事件线索并保持网络拓扑的生成式模拟器。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 结构引导的掩码预训练；(2) 拓扑感知的扩散过程，在去噪时通过类U-Net的图下采样和上采样实现。推理时仅需自然语言事件输入即可生成模拟。

Result: 在四个大规模路网数据集上达到SOTA性能，展现出强泛化能力。同时发布了包含对齐事件和网络流量数据的多模态基准Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M及拓扑保真度评估指标JL-MMD。

Conclusion: Net-Ev$^2$有效解决了网络事件演化模拟中事件语义与拓扑结构联合建模的难题，在多个路网场景下验证了其优越性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Reducing real-world trial and error has long been a central goal of decision making, and generative simulators advance this goal by modeling the evolution of future states. An even more challenging yet meaningful task is simulating how disturbance events (e.g., accidents) propagate their impacts across real-world networks. The existing approaches fall short of modeling both structured attributes and unstructured semantics of events, and capturing topological structures in simulating network event evolution. Therefore, we are motivated to propose Net-Ev$^2$ ($\underline{\textbf{Net}}$work $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$ent $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$olution), a novel generative simulator that jointly leverages event cues while preserving network topology in simulations. Specifically, the framework consists of two stages, namely structure-guided masked pre-training and topology-aware diffusion process, which is achieved by U-Net-like graph downsampling and upsampling during denoising. At inference time, Net-Ev$^2$ can generate simulations using natural-language event input only, with greater flexibility for practical usage. Furthermore, we introduce Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M, a multimodal benchmark of aligned event and network traffic data across four large-scale road networks, as well as a new topology-aware metric, namely JL-MMD, to evaluate topological fidelity in generated network dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization ability of Net-Ev$^2$. Code is made available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/Net-Ev-2.

</details>


### [135] [$μ$VLA: On Recurrent Memory for Partially Observable Manipulation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12497)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Daniil Zelezetsky,Aydar Bulatov,Artem Pshenitsyn,Yuri Kuratov,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出μVLA，通过在强预训练VLA骨干中仅添加少量可学习记忆token并利用截断反向传播时间（TBPTT）进行端到端训练，实现了对递归机制在部分可观测条件下的隔离研究。实验表明，在MIKASA-Robo上，最强设置将平均成功率从0.42提升至0.84；在LIBERO上达到96.2%，证明在全可观测下无退化。研究标定了最小化骨干递归的能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强VLA模型同时引入递归、检索、压缩、辅助目标、层次记忆或任务特定架构变更，导致递归本身的贡献被混淆。本文旨在通过受控隔离实验，纯粹地研究递归在强预训练VLA骨干中的作用。

Method: 在Transformer中引入少量可学习记忆token，这些token跨时间步传递并通过自注意力更新；采用截断反向传播时间（TBPTT）进行端到端训练，无辅助损失和架构变更。实例化为μVLA系列，参数化为记忆宽度m、TBPTT长度K和记忆更新规则（跨步梯度或分离EMA），使递归成为唯一变量。

Result: 在MIKASA-Robo上，最强设置下训练任务平均成功率从0.42提升至0.84，保留集任务达到0.23（基线0.07）；需不同记忆结构的任务性能接近基线。LIBERO上最强递归变体平均成功率达96.2%，全可观测下无退化。

Conclusion: 标定了最小化骨干递归的能力边界：识别了递归足以应对的任务场景以及需要额外记忆结构的场景，为VLA模型在部分可观测条件下的设计提供了校准参考。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions from the current observation, an assumption that fails under partial observability, where decisions depend on information no longer visible. Existing memory-augmented VLAs simultaneously introduce recurrence, retrieval, compression modules, auxiliary objectives, hierarchical memory, or task-specific architectural changes, so the contribution of recurrence itself remains entangled with surrounding machinery. We present a controlled isolation study of recurrence in a strong pretrained VLA backbone. Our formulation augments the transformer with a small set of learnable memory tokens carried across timesteps and updated through self-attention, trained end to end with truncated backpropagation through time, with no auxiliary losses and no architectural changes. We instantiate this as $μ$VLA, a family of OpenVLA-OFT variants parameterized by memory width m, TBPTT length K, and the memory update rule (cross-step gradients or a detached EMA), so that recurrence is the only varying factor. On MIKASA-Robo, $μ$VLA improves average success rate on five training tasks from 0.42 to 0.84 at the strongest setting and reaches 0.23 on held-out tasks with the same memory structure versus 0.07 for the memoryless baseline. On tasks requiring different memory structure, performance remains near baseline. On LIBERO, the strongest recurrent variant achieves 96.2% average success, indicating no regression under full observability. We interpret these results as a calibration of the capability envelope of minimal in-backbone recurrence, identifying the regime in which it is sufficient and the regime where additional memory structure is required. Demos and videos can be found in https://avanturist322.github.io/mu-vla/.

</details>


### [136] [Improving Crash Frequency Prediction from Simulated Traffic Conflicts Using Machine Learning Based Microsimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12500)
*Xian Liu,Carlo G. Prato,Gustav Markkula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于机器学习的驾驶行为模型在交通微观仿真中用于碰撞预测的效果，结果显示ML模型生成的冲突能准确预测真实碰撞频率，且无需地点特定标定，而传统规则模型无法实现有意义的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于微观仿真的安全研究采用简化规则模型，虽能合理再现交通流，但难以生成真实的冲突动态，限制了碰撞预测精度。机器学习行为模型通过学习大规模轨迹数据，有望提升仿真真实性和碰撞预测能力。

Method: 对英国利兹市五个实际信号交叉口分别使用标准规则模型和先进ML模型进行交通微观仿真；采用二维碰撞时间指标识别仿真冲突；利用极值理论预测碰撞频率。

Result: ML模型生成的冲突预测的碰撞频率与真实碰撞数据一致，而规则模型无法产生有意义的预测；直接使用ML模型生成的仿真碰撞来预测真实碰撞频率效果较差。

Conclusion: ML行为模型在无需地点特定标定的情况下，通过仿真冲突进行碰撞预测具有显著优势，但当前ML模型虽能真实再现冲突，尚无法生成真实的碰撞；研究为基于ML的交通微观仿真指明了未来方向。

Abstract: Traffic microsimulation combined with surrogate safety measures has increasingly been used as a proactive alternative to historical crash data for predicting crash frequency for current or planned road infrastructure designs. However, existing microsimulation-based safety studies have adopted simplified rule-based behaviour models, which reproduce traffic flow reasonably well but often fail to generate realistic conflict dynamics, limiting crash prediction accuracy. Recent advances in machine learning (ML)-based behaviour models offer a promising opportunity to potentially improve microsimulation realism and crash frequency predictions by learning human driving behaviour directly from large-scale trajectory datasets. To investigate this possibility, traffic microsimulation was conducted for five real-world signalised intersections in Leeds, UK, using both a standard rule-based model and a state-of-the-art ML model. Simulated vehicle trajectories were analysed using a two-dimensional Time-to-Collision metric to identify simulated conflicts, which were then modelled using Extreme Value Theory to predict crash frequency. Results show that conflicts from the ML model yielded crash predictions in line with the real-world crash data, whereas the rule-based model did not permit meaningful predictions, presumably due to a lack of model calibration to the specific simulated intersections. Directly using ML-generated simulated crashes to predict real-world crash frequency also yielded poor results, suggesting that while current ML models can realistically reproduce conflicts, they are not yet able to generate realistic crashes. Overall, the findings demonstrate that ML-based behaviour models are promising for improving crash prediction from simulated conflicts, without a need for location-specific model calibration, and suggest clear future directions for ML-based traffic microsimulation.

</details>


### [137] [Policy-driven Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy QoT Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12501)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal QoT框架，将统计保证的QoT估计与运营决策策略相结合，在域偏移下实现可靠的光路可行性预测，在开放数据集上将准确率从92%提升至99.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光路可行性预测方法在域偏移（domain shift）场景下可靠性不足，需要一种能提供统计保证且可泛化的预测框架。

Method: 提出Conformal QoT框架，该框架结合了保形预测（conformal prediction）提供的统计保证QoT估计与策略驱动的运营决策策略，实现域偏移下的鲁棒预测。

Result: 在开放数据集上，准确率从92%提升至99.6%。

Conclusion: Conformal QoT框架能够有效提升域偏移场景下光路可行性预测的可靠性，显著提升预测准确率。

Abstract: We propose Conformal QoT, a policy-driven framework that combines statistically guaranteed QoT estimation with operational decision policies, enabling reliable lightpath-feasibility predictions under domain shift and improving accuracy from 92\% to 99.6\% on open datasets.

</details>


### [138] [Dolph2Vec: Self-Supervised Representations of Dolphin Vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12503)
*Chiara Semenzin,Faadil Mustun,Roberto Dessi,Pierre Orhan,Alexis Emanuelli,Yair Lakretz,Gonzalo de Polavieja,German Sumbre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文收集并发布了五只已知海豚超过五年的纵向录音数据集，基于Wav2Vec2.0架构提出物种特异性自监督学习模型Dolph2Vec，在签名哨声分类和哨声检测任务上显著优于通用基线模型，且学习到的嵌入和码本结构能够捕捉与海豚哨声类别对应的可解释声学单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督学习模型在生物声学领域追求跨物种的广泛泛化，未能针对个体通信系统的细粒度结构进行优化，缺乏对特定物种通信系统的深入分析能力。

Method: 收集五只已知海豚在半自然海洋环境中超过五年的纵向录音数据；基于Wav2Vec2.0架构进行领域适配，训练物种特异性的自监督学习模型Dolph2Vec。

Result: Dolph2Vec在签名哨声分类和哨声检测两个生物相关任务上均显著优于通用基线模型；学习到的嵌入和码本结构能够捕捉与海豚哨声类别对应的可解释声学单元，甚至可能揭示亚哨声结构。

Conclusion: 自监督学习不仅可以作为高性能模型，还可以作为探索动物交流研究假设的科学工具，实现细粒度的通信模式分析。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has opened new opportunities in bioacoustics by enabling scalable modeling of animal vocalizations without the need for expensive manual annotation. However, current SSL models in this domain prioritize broad generalization across species and are not optimized for uncovering the fine-grained structure of individual communication systems. In this work, we collect and release a novel dataset of over five years of longitudinal recordings, from five known dolphins in a semi-naturalistic marine environment, an unprecedented resource for studying dolphin communication. We adapt the Wav2Vec2.0 Baevski et al. (2020) architecture to this domain and introduce Dolph2Vec, the first large-scale, species-specific SSL model trained exclusively on this data. We benchmark our model on two biologically relevant tasks: signature whistle classification and whistle detection. Dolph2Vec significantly outperforms general-purpose baselines in both tasks. Beyond performance, we show that learned embeddings and codebook structure capture interpretable acoustic units aligned with dolphin whistle categories and possibly sub-whistle structure, enabling fine-grained analysis of communication patterns. Our findings demonstrate how SSL can serve as both a model and a scientific tool to explore hypotheses in animal communication research.

</details>


### [139] [Boosting Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12505)
*Pengwei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DPOP，通过在DPO基础上增加对参考模型贪婪响应的门控惩罚，利用被忽略的参考模型信号来改进离线偏好优化，在AlpacaEval 2.0上超越DPO、SimPO和AlphaDPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO类方法仅使用数据集中的chosen和rejected响应，忽略了参考模型自身对同一提示生成的响应这一有用信号。

Method: 在DPO损失基础上增加一个门控惩罚项，当当前策略对偏好响应的似然仍低于拒绝响应时激活惩罚，惩罚目标为参考模型的贪婪响应。

Result: 在AlpacaEval 2.0上，DPOP在Llama-3-8b-it和Gemma-2-9b-it上分别比基线提升5.3%和4.4%的长度控制胜率。

Conclusion: DPOP通过引入参考模型贪婪响应的门控惩罚有效提升了偏好优化效果，且SimNPO风格的长度归一化惩罚优于NPO和token级unlikelihood。

Abstract: Offline preference optimization has become a practical substitute for reinforcement learning from human feedback, but pairwise objectives such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and its variants use only the chosen and rejected responses stored in a static dataset. This leaves a useful signal unused: the response that the reference model itself would generate for the same prompt. We propose Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization (DPOP), a simple extension of DPO that augments the base preference loss with a gated penalty on reference-greedy responses. DPOP activates this penalty only when the current policy still assigns a lower likelihood to the preferred response than to the rejected response. On AlpacaEval 2.0, DPOP improves length-controlled win rate over DPO, SimPO, and AlphaDPO on both Llama-3-8b-it and Gemma-2-9b-it, achieving relative gains of 5.3\% and 4.4\% over baselines on the two models, respectively. Ablations further show that a SimNPO-style length-normalized penalty is stronger than NPO and token-level unlikelihood in this setting.

</details>


### [140] [Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation: Post-Training Without Rubric Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12507)
*MohammadHossein Rezaei,Anas Mahmoud,Zihao Wang,Utkarsh Tyagi,Advait Gosai,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Aakash Sabharwal,Bing Liu,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无验证器的Rubric引导自蒸馏方法RGSD，通过将基于rubric的条件化策略蒸馏到无条件学生策略中，实现逐token的密集学习信号，替代了传统基于LLM验证器的稀疏轨迹奖励，在医疗和科学领域的多个模型上取得了与基于判别的GRPO相当的rubric满意度，同时显著降低了训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于rubric的训练方法依赖LLM验证器对轨迹打分，存在训练开销大、受验证器偏差影响、反馈稀疏等问题，亟需一种无验证器的高效替代方案。

Method: 将基础策略在rubric条件下的分布作为教师，蒸馏到无条件的student策略中，实现逐token的密集学习信号，完全移除LLM验证器。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B)和Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B)模型上，RGSD在医疗和科学领域达到与基于判别的GRPO相当的rubric满意度，每个prompt仅需一次on-policy rollout且无需训练时验证器调用。

Conclusion: 原始rubric比自生成参考响应提供更强的教师信号；在验证器成本或可靠性成为瓶颈时，RGSD可作为无验证器的互补方案。

Abstract: Rubrics have emerged as an alternative to RLVR in open-ended domains where a single ground-truth final answer is not available. Existing rubric-based training methods rely on an LLM verifier that scores each rollout against rubrics. This introduces substantial training-time overhead, exposes optimization to verifier-specific biases, and reduces rubric feedback to a sparse end-of-trajectory signal. We propose Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD), a verifier-free training method in which the base policy, conditioned on the rubric, serves as the teacher for the unconditioned student. RGSD distills the rubric-conditioned teacher distribution into the student token-by-token, replacing sparse trajectory-level rewards with dense per-token learning signals and removing the LLM judge from the training loop entirely. Across Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B) and Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B) models on medical and science domains, RGSD achieves rubric satisfaction comparable to judge-based GRPO while using one on-policy rollout per prompt and no training-time verifier calls. Ablations show that raw rubrics provide a stronger teacher enrichment signal than self-generated reference responses, while a stronger GRPO judge can outperform RGSD in some settings, positioning RGSD as a complementary verifier-free alternative when verifier cost or reliability is the bottleneck.

</details>


### [141] [Crossing the Validation Crisis: Cross-Validation Reduces Benchmarking Variance Surprisingly Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12552)
*Célestin Eve,Gaël Varoquaux,Thomas Moreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出机器学习领域因统计变异和测试样本有限导致性能评估不可靠的验证危机，提出通过交叉验证提升评估置信度，引入"样本增益"概念量化多折交叉验证对方差降低的效果，并设计动态早停策略以在资源与可靠性间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习领域存在验证危机：由于算法本身的随机性和有限的测试样本，性能估计不可靠，难以区分真实进步与统计噪声。

Method: 提出样本增益概念量化交叉验证折数对虚拟数据增量的等效作用；设计动态早停程序，利用前几折结果预测后续折是否能带来显著样本增益。

Result: 在合成数据、组织病理扫描和NLP微调等真实数据集上，多折交叉验证显著提升性能估计的可靠性和稳定性，且边际收益递减点晚于预期。

Conclusion: 强调充分挖掘可用样本的交叉验证潜力对于实现稳健可靠的基准测试具有重要价值。

Abstract: Modern machine learning progresses through empirical work, benchmarking new methods to evaluate relative performance.
