However, this coupling design becomes troublesome in MAGs, where structure-induced and modality-intrinsic semantics may contribute differently to downstream tasks. Structure-induced semantics promote relational consistency through smooth topological variation, whereas modality-intrinsic semantics often encode local, fine-grained distinctions that should not be uniformly smoothed or aligned. Therefore, the key challenge is to identify semantic roles before cross-modal fusion. To this end, we leverage graph-frequency variation as a prior, where low-frequency components capture topology-consistent semantics and high-frequency components preserve modality-specific semantics. Based on this intuition, we propose SMGFM, a spectral multimodal graph pretraining framework that decomposes each modality-specific node signal into graph-frequency bands and assigns band-level semantic roles before cross-modal interaction. Concretely, SMGFM constructs frequency-resolved modality tokens with scalable Chebyshev filters, estimates their coupling reliability through topology-conditioned routing, and performs band-modality interaction before fusion. Its frequency-routed objectives align smooth consensus routes while preserving modality-specific routes, mitigating spatial-domain entanglement and uniform cross-modal alignment. Extensive experiments conducted on the MAG datasets demonstrate that SMGFM achieves state-of-the-art performance across graph-level and modality-level tasks.

</details>


### [178] [Multi-Bitwidth Quantization for LLMs Using Additive Codebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12876)
*Liza Babaoglu,Shuangyi Chen,Ashish Khisti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Drop-by-Drop，一种多比特宽度的训练后量化框架，允许从单个模型在推理时动态控制LLM权重的精度，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM需要在不同资源受限的异构硬件上部署，自适应地管理性能与效率的权衡至关重要，但现有方法往往需要重新训练或无法灵活控制精度。

Method: 基于信息论和逐次细化理论，利用LLM权重服从高斯分布的特性，在加权均方误差失真度量下实现最优重构；通过Matryoshka式监督和加法码本结构，使有序子集能在每个精度级别生成准确的部分重建。

Result: 单个检查点支持多个比特宽度，显著降低存储和内存开销，在Qwen、LLaMA、Gemma、Mistral等主流架构上保持有竞争力的困惑度和准确率。

Conclusion: Drop-by-Drop实现了无需重训练的推理时精度控制，为LLM在异构硬件上的高效部署提供了灵活且实用的量化方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across heterogeneous hardware with varying resource constraints, the ability to adaptively manage the trade-off between performance and efficiency without retraining is critical. We propose Drop-by-Drop, a novel multi-bitwidth post-training quantization framework that enables inference-time precision control over LLM weights from a single trained model. Our method is theoretically grounded in information theory and successive refinement. We establish that LLM weights, which commonly follow a Gaussian distribution, can be optimally reconstructed with increasing fidelity as additional bits are incorporated, under a weighted mean squared error distortion motivated by LLM loss functions. To realize this in practice, Drop-by-Drop incorporates Matryoshka-style supervision into the loss function, exploiting the structure of additive codebooks. Drop-by-Drop produces a single model where ordered subsets of codebooks yield accurate partial reconstructions at each precision level. This approach significantly reduces storage and memory overhead by allowing a single checkpoint to serve multiple bitwidths, while maintaining competitive perplexity and accuracy across major architectures, such as Qwen, LLaMA, Gemma, and Mistral.

</details>


### [179] [LongSpike: Fractional Order Spiking State Space Models for Efficient Long Sequence Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12895)
*Xinrui He,Qiyu Kang,Xuhao Li,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongSpike 提出将分数阶状态空间模型（f-SSM）引入脉冲神经网络，通过分数阶微积分实现长记忆核的层次化整合，在长序列任务中超越现有SNN方法，同时保持稀疏突触计算的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SNN基于一阶ODE假设，存在"无记忆"瓶颈，难以捕获长序列任务中的复杂长程依赖关系。

Method: 将控制理论中的分数阶状态空间建模（f-SSM）扩展到脉冲域，通过分数阶微积分扩展传统整数阶SSM，实现具有长记忆核的层次化神经元动力学，并采用支持高效并行训练的状态空间公式化。

Result: 在Long Range Arena (LRA)、WikiText-103、Speech Commands等基准上，LongSpike在准确率上超越最先进SNN，同时保持稀疏突触计算。

Conclusion: 分数阶状态空间建模能有效突破一阶SNN的记忆瓶颈，为长序列脉冲计算提供新范式。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) are well-regarded for their biological plausibility and energy efficiency in processing sequential data. However, dominant SNN architectures typically rely on first-order Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) to govern neuronal state transitions. This first-order assumption imposes a "memoryless" bottleneck, limiting the model's capacity to capture the complex, long-range dependencies inherent in long-sequence tasks. In this work, we propose LongSpike, a novel SNN framework that integrates fractional-order State-Space Modeling, or f-SSM, from control theory into the spiking domain. By extending traditional integer-order SSMs to the fractional-calculus regime, LongSpike enables the hierarchical integration of neuronal dynamics with long-memory kernels. To mitigate the computational overhead and parallelization challenges typically associated with fractional operators, we leverage a state-space formulation that supports efficient, parallel training. Empirical evaluations on challenging benchmarks, including Long Range Arena (LRA), large-scale WikiText-103, and Speech Commands, demonstrate that LongSpike outperforms state-of-the-art SNNs in accuracy while preserving sparse synaptic computation. The code is available at https://github.com/xinruihe389-commits/LongSpike.

</details>


### [180] [PolicyGuard: Towards Test-time and Step-level Adversary Defense for Reinforcement Learning Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12896)
*Junfeng Guo Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PolicyGuard，一种基于高斯过程后验方差的测试时逐时间步后门防御方法，在7个RL游戏中对扰动攻击和对抗攻击的平均AUROC分别达到0.856和0.859。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后门防御方法存在局限性：需要访问模型内部参数、仅在模型或轨迹层面操作、或仅适用于特定攻击类型。为保障RL智能体的安全性，亟需一种更通用、细粒度的防御方法。

Method: 提出PolicyGuard，利用高斯过程(GP)后验方差进行检测，并引入伪轨迹(pseudo trajectories)适配机制，使得在测试时能够对单个时间步计算不确定性。

Result: 在7个RL游戏上的实验表明，PolicyGuard在大多数情况下达到SOTA检测性能，对扰动攻击平均AUROC为0.856，对对抗攻击平均AUROC为0.859。

Conclusion: PolicyGuard作为一种测试时逐时间步的后门防御方法，能够有效检测多种类型的RL后门攻击，且提供了GP后验方差有效性的理论依据。

Abstract: While real-world applications of reinforcement learning (RL) are becoming increasingly popular, the security of RL systems deserve more attention and exploration. In particular, recent work has revealed that RL agents are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where a victim agent behaves normally under standard conditions but executes malicious actions when a specific trigger is activated. Existing backdoor defenses for RL either require access to the agent's internal parameters, operate only at the model or trajectory level, or are limited to specific attack types. To ensure the security of RL agents, we propose \texttt{PolicyGuard}, a \textit{test-time step-level} backdoor defense which leverages Gaussian Process (GP) posterior variance and adapts pseudo trajectories to enable uncertainty computation for individual time step. Besides, we also provide theoretical foundations to explain the efficacy of GP posterior variance. Extensive experiments across seven RL games demonstrate that PolicyGuard achieves state-of-the-art detection performance in most cases, with average AUROC of 0.856 for perturbation-based attacks and 0.859 for adversary-agent attacks.

</details>


### [181] [Selecting Samples on Graphs: A Unified Dataset Pruning Framework for Lossless Training Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12913)
*Dongyue Wu,Zilin Guo,Xiaoyu Li,Jiajia Liu,Jingdong Chen,Nong Sang,Changxin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图模型的统一数据集剪枝框架，将数据集建模为加权图，通过求解最大权重团问题（MWCP）来同时捕捉样本的内在价值和外在多样性，在ImageNet-1k上以ResNet-50实现40%以上的训练时间缩减且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集剪枝方法通常仅依赖内在信号（独立评估样本质量）或外在信号（通过成对关系促进多样性），各自只捕捉了样本效用的一个方面，在不同剪枝比例或数据分布下缺乏鲁棒性。因此需要一种统一框架来综合两者。

Method: 将数据集建模为加权图，节点权重编码内在价值，边权重编码外在多样性；将数据集剪枝形式化为最大权重团问题（MWCP），并设计基于边际增益的贪心求解算法；在温和条件下给出了近似保证。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k上使用ResNet-50，将训练时间减少40%以上且不牺牲准确率；在多个基准上优于现有数据集剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的图统一框架能够同时利用样本内在和外在信号进行高效剪枝，具有理论近似保证和广泛的实用性，为数据集高效训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of modern training datasets has significantly increased computational cost, motivating dataset pruning~(DP) methods which retain only a subset of informative samples to reduce training cost.
  Existing pruning criteria typically rely on either intrinsic signals that assess samples independently or extrinsic signals that promote diversity via pairwise relations.
  While effective in their own specific regimes, each captures only one aspect of sample utility and lacks robustness across different pruning ratios or data distribution.
  In this work, we present a unified graph-based DP framework.
  By modeling the dataset as a weighted graph, where node weights encode intrinsic value and edge weights encode extrinsic value, DP can be cast as a Maximum Weight Clique Problem (MWCP).
  Although MWCP is NP-hard, its structure admits a principled greedy solution based on sample-wise marginal gains.
  Under a few mild conditions, we further prove that this unified objective enjoys a formal approximation guarantee, which applies to a broad family of importance metrics and provides practical design guidelines.
  Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms existing DP methods while substantially reducing training cost, reducing training time by over 40\% without sacrificing accuracy on ImageNet-1k with ResNet-50.

</details>


### [182] [Where Computation Lives Inside TabPFN: Causal Localisation of Attention Head Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12917)
*Atharva Gupta,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对表格基础模型TabPFN 2.5进行因果机制分析，发现其特征级注意力头存在明确的时间特化：一个主导注意力头的因果必要性比其余头高2-5倍，其主导层随任务复杂度移动，而其他头呈现对称的后期层分布。研究还发现对比激活引导在样本间迁移失败，归因于TabPFN的上下文学习机制依赖上下文相关的注意力编码任务结构，而非语言模型中可引导的稳定参数方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解表格基础模型TabPFN的内部计算机制，特别是其注意力头在层间的分工模式，以及探究推理时可操控性（steerability）的限制及其原因。

Method: 使用激活修补（activation patching）、消融实验（ablation）和注意力熵（attention entropy）分析，在两个合成回归数据集上对TabPFN 2.5的特征级注意力头进行因果机制分析。

Result: 发现一个主导注意力头的因果必要性在峰值层比其他头高2-5倍，且其主导层随任务复杂度变化；其余头呈现对称的后期层分布。注意力熵与修补实验提供了一致的证据。对比激活引导在样本间无法迁移。

Conclusion: TabPFN的上下文学习机制依赖于上下文相关的注意力编码任务结构，而非语言模型中那种稳定的参数方向，因此对比激活引导等操控方法难以跨样本迁移。

Abstract: We present the first causal mechanistic analysis of a tabular foundation model, investigating how TabPFN 2.5's feature wise attention heads distribute computation across layers. Using activation patching, ablation, and attention entropy across two synthetic regression datasets, we find clear temporal specialisation: one head's causal necessity dominates that of the others by 2 to 5 times at peak layer, with its dominant layer shifting across tasks of different complexity, while the remaining heads exhibit symmetric late layer profiles. Attention entropy and patching provide convergent evidence for the computationally active layers of the dominant head. We additionally investigate inference time steerability via contrastive activation steering, which fails to transfer across samples. We attribute this result to TabPFN's in context learning mechanism, which encodes task structure through context dependent attention rather than the stable parametric directions that make steering tractable in language models.

</details>


### [183] [LoRA-Muon: Spectral Steepest Descent on the Low-Rank Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12921)
*Franz Louis Cesista,Katherine Crowson,Cédric Simal,Stella Biderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-Muon优化器，将Muon优化器的谱最速下降规则应用于低秩微调场景，解决了LoRA在初始化选择、学习率跨秩迁移困难以及难以超越密集基线等问题。实验表明，LoRA-Muon在计算匹配条件下，低秩代理能恢复密集训练的最佳学习率，且内存效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽然显著降低了微调深度模型的计算和内存成本，但相比密集训练更难调优：对初始化敏感、最优学习率难以跨秩迁移、且常无法超越密集基线。现有优化器（如Spectron）存在任意因子缩放依赖问题，需要更稳定高效的低秩优化方案。

Method: 将Muon优化器的谱最速下降规则扩展到低秩设定，提出LoRA-Muon；配合分裂权重衰减规则（split weight-decay rule）。该方法不需要QR分解，也不存储二阶矩，计算更高效。

Result: 在TinyShakespeare实验中，rank-2的LoRA-Muon代理恢复密集训练的最佳学习率；rank-32的LoRA-Muon在种子平均扫描中达到低于密集基线的平均验证损失。其最优学习率可在秩、宽度、深度和因子缩放之间迁移。

Conclusion: LoRA-Muon是全秩Muon和Shampoo族优化器的良好低秩代理，在初始化稳定性、超参数迁移性和内存效率方面均优于现有LoRA优化方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) significantly reduces compute and memory costs for finetuning Deep Learning models but is often harder to tune than dense training: when using factor-wise optimizers such as AdamW, it is sensitive to initialization choices, its optimal learning rates transfer poorly across ranks, and it often fails to beat dense baselines. We derive LoRA-Muon by applying the Muon optimizer's spectral steepest-descent rule to the low-rank setting. Along with our split weight-decay rule, our main claim is that LoRA-Muon is a good low-rank proxy for full-rank Muon and Shampoo-family optimizers. Its optimal learning rates transfer across rank, width, depth, and factor-rescaling. In our compute-matched TinyShakespeare study, a rank-$2$ proxy recovers the dense best tested learning rate, and a rank-$32$ LoRA-Muon run attains lower mean validation loss than the dense baseline in the seed-averaged sweep. We further show that the Spectron optimizer depends on arbitrary factor scaling, so it would likely be a poor fit when finetuning starts from badly imbalanced factors, and that LoRA-RITE's simplified QR-coordinate core implements the same spectral update. LoRA-Muon computes that update without QR-decomposition and avoids storing second moments, making it more accelerator-friendly and memory-efficient.

</details>


### [184] [Order Is Not Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12923)
*Gareth Seneque,Lap-Hang Ho,Nafise Erfanian Saeedi,Jeffrey Molendijk,Tim Elson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“序不等于控制”的核心论点，并定义了基于接收器门控响应律的控制判别标准。通过生物（小鼠ALM、线虫、斑马鱼）、大语言模型、适配器和随机算子四类面板的实验证据，揭示了局域受纳控制的普遍存在性。LLM面板显示响应向量在四种材料条件下的预测准确率达72.8-73.7%，非零分量达84.3-84.8%；外部观察者对系统效应和靶标/先验家族的预测准确率分别为93.6%和91.7%。研究建立了介观控制层次的驱动-耗散响应系统框架，同时明确排除了预生成控制、隐层/逻辑因果充分性、生物-LLM坐标同一性及字面热力学量的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐、可解释性、引导和神经扰动研究关注的是“序诱导对象”，但将序等同于控制存在根本性问题。本文的动机在于严格区分“序”与“控制”，建立控制的操作性定义，并通过跨生物、LLM、适配器和随机算子的实证证据，验证局域受纳控制的存在性及响应律的普遍性。

Method: 定义以分母索引算子为核心的接收器门控响应律框架，将控制分解为材料状态、行动/驱动、浴和接收器状态到响应位移、汇、努力和盆地投影的映射。通过四个面板（生物物理响应算子、LLM生成输出、构型条件适配器、随机算子）进行实证验证，采用分量符号准确率、非零分量准确率、外部观察者预测准确率等指标量化控制性能。

Result: 生物面板提供了物理响应算子证据但排除了坐标同一性和控制器解释；LLM面板在四种材料条件下响应向量预测准确率72.8-73.7%，非零分量达84.3-84.8%；外部观察者预测系统效应准确率93.6%、靶标/先验家族准确率91.7%；构型条件适配器重塑易感性；随机算子面板区分了测量机会与可部署行动策略。

Conclusion: 序不等于控制，控制需满足接收器门控响应律和有限努力标准。跨面板证据支持局域受纳控制和可测随机响应算子的存在，但预生成控制、隐层因果充分性、生物-LLM坐标同一性和字面热力学量不在当前框架范围内。该工作为介观控制层次提供了驱动-耗散响应系统理论框架。

Abstract: AI alignment, interpretability, steering, and neural perturbation studies identify order-inducing objects. We argue that order is not control. Control requires a receiver-gated response law: a denominator-indexed operator mapping material state, action/drive, bath, and receiver state to response displacement, sinks, effort, and basin projection. We identify it across biological, LLM, adapter, and stochastic-operator panels. The laws are local: an intervention can be admitted, saturated, sign-changing, leaky, or overdriven depending on medium, bath, receiver state, action port, and comparator. Control is assigned when finite effort moves a target or outcome-readout class under the same denominator while damage, null/evasive, invalid format, overdrive, and unnecessary effort stay bounded. Mouse ALM, C. elegans, and zebrafish panels provide physical response-operator evidence while excluding coordinate identity and controller conclusions. LLM panels show generated-output response laws: across four material conditions, response vectors are predictable at 72.8-73.7% component-sign accuracy, rising to 84.3-84.8% on nonzero components; held-out observers predict system-effect and target/oracle families at 93.6% and 91.7% accuracy. Constitution-conditioned adapters reshape susceptibility as prepared media, and stochastic-operator panels separate measured opportunity from deployable action policies. This gives a driven-dissipative response-system account at the mesoscopic control level: drives act through prepared media, baths, and receivers, producing admitted movement, impedance, sinks, or overdrive. The evidence supports local admitted control and measurable stochastic response operators, while leaving deployable pre-generation control, hidden/logit causal sufficiency, biological-to-LLM coordinate identity, and literal thermodynamic quantities outside scope.

</details>


### [185] [Is Spurious Correlation Removal Always Learnable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12930)
*Yibo Zhou,Bo Li,Hai-Miao Hu,Hanzi Wang,Xiaokang Zhang,Ruifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了不变性学习中的计算-统计差距问题，证明即使在不变结构统计可识别的情况下，也存在计算障碍。在特定稀疏恢复原语假设下，存在可采样多环境实例使得指数搜索可学习但多项式算法不可行。同时推导了环境多样性参数γ控制下的极小极大风险率，并发现了标签偏移导致的相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示不变性学习中存在的计算障碍（即使统计可识别），并量化环境多样性与学习难度之间的关系。

Method: 理论证明：基于黑箱可采样监督稀疏恢复原语构造计算困难实例；统计风险分析：推导极小极大风险率，刻画相变条件；实验验证：合成和真实数据展示理论预测的差距与转变。

Result: 存在计算障碍：在特定原语假设下，k=1的实例指数搜索可学而多项式算法不可行；极小极大风险为Θ(k(d-k)/(n|ℰ|))；标签偏移下存在相变，n*∝k(d-k)/(|ℰ|γ²)，误差缩放比例与1/γ²相关。

Conclusion: 不变性学习存在固有的计算-统计差距，环境多样性是控制可识别性和学习难度的关键参数，相变现象进一步揭示了标签偏移对学习复杂度的影响。

Abstract: Invariant learning can fail even when the invariant structure is statistically identifiable. We show a conditional computational barrier: under a black-box samplable supervised sparse recovery primitive motivated by average-case sparse-recovery reductions, there exist \emph{samplable} multi-environment instances with a one-dimensional predictive invariant subspace ($k=1$) that are learnable with polynomial samples by exhaustive search, while any polynomial-time constant-accuracy recovery algorithm would contradict the primitive. We further quantify environment diversity by a separation parameter $γ$, which controls identifiability and the curvature of invariance objectives. Under sufficient diversity and local Gaussian regularity, the minimax risk is $\mathbb{E}[\dist(\hat{V},V_{\mathrm{inv}})^2]=Θ(k(d-k)/(n|\mathcal{E}|))$, and under label-induced shifts a phase transition occurs at $n^*\propto k(d-k)/(|\mathcal{E}|γ^2)$ with refined estimation error scaling proportional to $1/γ^2$. Synthetic and real datasets illustrate the predicted gaps and transitions and motivate simple diversity diagnostics.

</details>


### [186] [Circuit Synchronization Precedes Generalization: Causal Evidence from Fourier Structure in Grokking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12966)
*Achyuthan Sivasankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出频率同步度（FSD）指标，可在泛化突现（grokking）前500-3000步预测该现象，并通过实验证明其与权重衰减的倒数呈线性关系，揭示了grokking的正则化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已知变换器在模算术任务上会突现泛化能力（grokking）且与傅里叶电路有关，但其时间因果结构和可控性尚不明确，亟需一个无需先验知识的早期预测指标及对其机制的理解。

Method: 提出FSD指标（归一化置换检验度量）；在多种模加法配置（p=53,71,97,113,131）上验证其预测能力；通过分叉训练实验改变权重衰减λ，建立Δₜ ∝ 1/λ的线性关系；进行架构消融实验（纯注意力、纯MLP、单层模型）。

Result: FSD在grokking前500-3000步同步（平均领先1722步，符号检验p≈0.004），早于排除损失基线；Δₜ与1/λ呈严格单调线性关系（R²=1.00和0.99）；纯注意力模型有FSD前兆但纯MLP无，单层模型的FSD滞后。

Conclusion: 证实grokking的阶段性间隙是正则化现象，FSD是最早可用的预测指标，且该机制可被权重衰减定量控制。

Abstract: Grokking -- where a transformer on modular arithmetic suddenly transitions from near-chance to near-perfect validation accuracy -- is attributed to a Fourier circuit, but its timing, causal structure, and controllability remain poorly understood. We introduce the Frequency Synchronization Degree (FSD), a normalised, permutation-tested metric for Fourier circuit synchronisation requiring no prior circuit knowledge. Across nine modular addition configurations (primes p in {53, 71, 97, 113, 131}, three seeds), FSD synchronises 500-3,000 steps before grokking (mean lead +1,722 steps; all nine positive, sign-test p~0.004), and precedes a restricted-logit loss baseline (Nanda et al.'s excluded loss) in all nine cases, making it the earliest available predictor. We provide direct causal evidence that the inter-phase gap is a regularisation phenomenon: forking training at the FSD-ceiling step and varying weight decay lambda produces strictly monotone earlier grokking, with Delta_t proportional to 1/lambda. This law replicates across three primes (p in {53,97,131}; R^2=1.00 and R^2=0.99 for two clean cases), captured as Delta_t ~ C/lambda, consistent with (1/lambda)*log(||W_mem||/tau). Architecture ablations show an attention-only model groks with a strong FSD precursor; an MLP-only model never groks; a single-layer model's FSD lags, confirming the precursor is a multi-block circuit property.

</details>


### [187] [Predicting Cognitive Load from Speech and Interaction Dynamics in Dyadic Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12971)
*Tahiya Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自然协作对话中的认知负荷预测，通过分析53组双人在九项协作任务中的音频数据，提取声学、动态和交互特征，使用双头GRU编码器预测感知认知负荷。结果表明，对话交互信号（如话轮转换动态、参与不平衡）能有效预测时间压力、脑力劳动、努力程度和任务表现相关的认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往认知负荷估计算法多在受控实验室环境中研究，其在自然协作对话中的可靠性尚不明确，缺乏对真实场景下语音与交互动态能否预测认知负荷的理解。

Method: 收集53组双人在九项协作任务中的音频数据，提取静态声学特征、动态特征和交互特征，训练双头门控循环单元（GRU）编码器模型来预测认知负荷得分。

Result: 对话交互信号能有效预测时间压力、脑力工作、努力程度和任务表现相关的认知负荷。时间需求与话轮重叠、说话者切换等话轮转换动态相关；脑力需求与说话者之间的参与不平衡相关。

Conclusion: 任务结构和对话交互对在自然协作环境中建模认知负荷至关重要。

Abstract: Estimating cognitive load from speech has largely been studied in controlled laboratory settings, with limited understanding of its reliability in natural collaborative conversations. We investigate whether speech and interaction dynamics predict perceived cognitive load during dyadic conversations. We analyze audio from 53 dyads performing nine collaborative tasks and extract static acoustic, dynamic, and interaction features to train a two-head Gated Recurrent Unit encoder to predict cognitive load scores. Results show conversational interaction provides useful signals for predicting cognitive load related to time pressure, mental work, effort, and task performance. Temporal demand is associated with turn-taking dynamics such as overlap and speaker switch, while mental demand is linked to imbalanced participation between speakers. These findings highlight the importance of task structure and conversational interaction for modeling cognitive load in natural collaborative settings.

</details>


### [188] [EPM-JEPA: Operator-Side Experience Modulation in JEPA-Family World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12979)
*Vedant Pandya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究JEPA系列世界模型在分布偏移下如何将累积经验融入预测器，比较了操作侧注入（EI-JEPA）和操作侧调制（EPM-JEPA）两种机制。预注册实验（Moving MNIST，重力偏移）显示两者差异为4.74%，属于无效结果。但EPM-JEPA比无记忆基线提升1.90%，而EI-JEPA低于基线，表明权重级调制的优势。主要贡献是机制分析：D_shift轨迹反映三个独立动力学过程（缓冲区循环、EMA目标漂移、LoRA固有暂态），而非收敛到平衡态。这些发现催生了PEM-JEPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA系列世界模型使用静态预测器，其权重在测试时动力学与训练分布不同时无法自适应。本文旨在探索如何将累积经验有效融入JEPA预测器以应对分布偏移。

Method: 对比两种机制：(1) 操作侧注入（EI-JEPA）：将压缩经验表示作为残差添加到预测器隐藏状态；(2) 操作侧调制（EPM-JEPA）：通过LoRA将相同表示生成低秩权重增量。在Moving MNIST重力偏移场景下进行预注册比较实验。

Result: 预注册比较中，EPM-JEPA（D_shift=0.7848）与EI-JEPA（0.8238）差异4.74%，被判定为无效结果。但EPM-JEPA比无记忆基线（0.8000）提升1.90%，而EI-JEPA低于基线。机制分析发现D_shift轨迹由三个独立动力学过程组成：缓冲区循环、EMA目标漂移和LoRA固有暂态（+0.021），而非收敛到平衡态。

Conclusion: 权重级调制（EPM-JEPA）对分布偏移下提升性能有效，而隐藏状态注入（EI-JEPA）无效。LoRA暂态现象表明存在动力学峰值限制，因此提出物理启发的PEM-JEPA作为后续方案。

Abstract: JEPA-family world models use a static predictor whose weights do not adapt when test-time dynamics diverge from training. We compare two mechanisms for incorporating accumulated experience into a JEPA predictor under distribution shift: operand-side injection, where a compressed experience representation is added as a residual to the predictor's hidden state (EI-JEPA), and operator-side modulation, where the same representation generates low-rank weight deltas via LoRA applied to the predictor's weights (EPM-JEPA). On a pre-registered comparison (Moving MNIST, gravity shift), EPM-JEPA (D_shift^{n=50} = 0.7848 +/- 0.0078, three seeds) differs from EI-JEPA (0.8238) by delta = 4.74% - Outcome C: a null result - by our stated criterion, a valid outcome. As a secondary, non-pre-registered observation, EPM-JEPA improves 1.90% over a no-memory baseline (0.8000), consistently across seeds, while EI-JEPA underperforms the baseline, indicating the benefit is specific to weight-level modulation. Our primary contribution is a mechanism analysis: the D_shift^{n=50} trajectory reflects three independent dynamical processes - buffer cycling, EMA target drift, and an intrinsic LoRA settling transient of +0.021 - rather than convergence to equilibrium. These findings motivate PEM-JEPA, a physics-grounded successor addressing this dynamical-peak limitation.

</details>


### [189] [Exposure Bias as Epistemic Underidentification in Recursive Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12990)
*Riku Green,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Telmo M Silva Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明递归多步预测不仅是分布偏移问题，在部分可观测或状态截断条件下更是 epistemic underidentification 问题。通过引入诱导状态 Z 和溯源变量 P，将诱导状态误差分解为三项，并实验验证了闭环预测中诱导状态机制、局部校正任务以及溯源感知校正的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将递归多步预测问题仅视为分布偏移（训练时使用观测历史，推理时使用自身预测），作者认为这种框架不完整，需要揭示更深层的认知识别问题。

Method: 理论证明递归 rollout 在部分可观测或状态截断下存在 epistemic underidentification；引入诱导状态 Z 和溯源变量 P 进行形式化；推导出诱导状态误差的三项分解；设计二元溯源编码进行溯源感知校正实验。

Result: 实证表明：rollout 会进入不同的诱导状态区间；固定诱导状态可定义局部校正任务；闭环增益不仅来自局部适应，还来自诱导状态的变化；溯源感知校正可在特定条件下提升性能。

Conclusion: 应将暴露偏差重新理解为自诱导认知不确定性下的推理问题，而非单纯的分布偏移。

Abstract: Recursive multi-step forecasting is usually framed as distribution shift: models are trained on observed histories but deployed on their own predictions. We show this framing is incomplete by proving that, under partial observability or state truncation, recursive rollout is also an epistemic underidentification problem. Even with deterministic latent dynamics, one-step Bayes supervision identifies behavior only on observed contexts and need not identify the deployed recursive predictor once rollout queries self-generated induced states whose correct local targets are not determined by numeric state alone. We formalize this with induced states $Z$ and provenance variables $P$, and derive a decomposition of induced-state error into teacher-forcing/rollout mismatch, representation--class approximation, and provenance information gaps. Empirically, we show that rollout enters a distinct induced-state regime, that fixed induced states define a distinct local corrective task, and that closed-loop gains arise not only from local adaptation but also from changing the induced states visited during rollout. Using a simple binary provenance encoding, provenance-aware correction can further improve performance, though gains are conditional rather than uniform. These results recast exposure bias as reasoning under self-induced epistemic uncertainty.

</details>


### [190] [DeepJEB++: Foundation Model-Driven Large-Scale 3D Engineering Dataset via 2D Latent Space Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12994)
*Soyoung Yoo,Leekyo Jeong,Jinsu Ra,Dongeon Lee,Sunwoong Yang,Hyogu Jeong,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeepJEB++是一个基于基础模型的数据增强框架，通过2D潜空间扩散、3D网格重建和自动化仿真标注，将不足400个种子设计扩展为15,360个带仿真标签的3D支架数据集（40倍扩展），并在单GPU上完成各阶段计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的工程设计中缺乏大规模同时包含几何与物理性能标签的3D数据集，现有3D数据增强方法难以保持微妙多样的几何变化，且自动化仿真标注过程因边界条件随生成几何变化而难以实现。

Method: 方法分为三个阶段：(1) 微调预训练2D潜扩散模型，通过多视图渲染和潜空间插值合成新视图，并用VLM质量过滤器保留可制造设计；(2) 通过领域自适应生成基础模型将验证后的图像提升为3D网格；(3) 自动管道识别每个网格上的载荷和螺栓接口，并分配有限元标签（质量、应力、位移）。

Result: 从不足400个种子设计出发，DeepJEB++生成15,360个带仿真标签的3D支架，实现40倍扩展，且每个阶段仅需单GPU。从可制造性、标签保真度（对比SimJEB真值）和分布一致性三个维度评估了增强质量。

Conclusion: DeepJEB++通过2D潜空间增强策略有效解决了3D工程数据稀缺问题，实现了大规模自动化仿真标注，数据集将公开发布以支持可复现的工程AI研究。

Abstract: Data-driven engineering design is constrained by the lack of large-scale 3D datasets that pair geometry with physics-based performance labels. In particular, existing 3D data augmentation techniques have limitations in preserving subtle and diverse geometric variations, and it remains difficult to automate the subsequent simulation-labeling process, where boundary conditions vary depending on the generated geometry. We present DeepJEB++, a foundation-model-driven data-augmentation framework that expands a small seed set of jet engine brackets into a large, simulation-labeled 3D dataset under constrained resources. Our key idea is to augment in the data-rich 2D latent space, then transfer to 3D. In Stage 1, we fine-tune a pretrained 2D latent diffusion model on multi-view renders and synthesize novel views by latent interpolation, retaining manufacturable designs through a vision-language-model (VLM) quality filter. In Stage 2, the validated images are lifted to 3D meshes by a domain-adapted generative foundation model. In Stage 3, an automated pipeline recognizes the load and bolt interfaces on each mesh and assigns finite-element labels -- mass, stress, and displacement -- without manual intervention. We assess augmentation quality along three intrinsic axes: manufacturability, label fidelity against the SimJEB ground truth, and distributional consistency. Starting from fewer than 400 seed designs, DeepJEB++ yields 15,360 simulation-labeled 3D brackets -- a 40x expansion -- using a single GPU per stage. The dataset will be made publicly available to support reproducible engineering-AI research.

</details>


### [191] [Reliability of Probabilistic Emulation of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12997)
*Sam F. Greenbury,Radka Jersakova,Paolo Conti,Marjan Famili,Christopher Iliffe Sprague,Edwin Brown,Jason D. McEwen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了生成模型（扩散/流匹配）与CRPS训练集成方法在2D时空物理系统概率预测中的可靠性，发现CRPS集成方法在不确定性校准、推理速度上更优，生成模型仅在环境空间训练时覆盖度相当但推理延迟大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两种主流概率预测方法（生成模型与CRPS训练集成）虽表现良好，但其不确定性可靠性尚未被系统评估，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在匹配模型大小和计算预算的条件下，构建评估框架，通过检查预测区间的经验覆盖率和准确度，对比两种方法在单步预测和自回归 rollout 中的表现，并考虑计算效率。

Result: CRPS训练集成在单步和自回归预测中均实现更可靠的不确定性校准，推理速度显著更快；生成模型在环境空间训练时覆盖度与CRPS集成相当但推理延迟大，在潜空间训练时覆盖度较差；两者预测精度均较好。

Conclusion: CRPS训练集成在不确定性可靠性和推理效率上优于生成模型，研究发布AutoCast和AutoSim工具包以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Two dominant approaches have emerged for generating probabilistic forecasts of physical systems: generative models, such as diffusion or flow matching; and ensembles of deterministic models with stochasticity injected, trained using the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) loss. While both approaches have demonstrated strong predictive accuracy, the reliability of their uncertainties has not been systematically assessed. We address this gap by developing a framework to evaluate both approaches across diverse 2D spatiotemporal physical systems, under matched model size and computational budget. We assess the reliability of probabilistic emulation by inspecting the empirical coverage of predictive intervals, while also considering accuracy and computational efficiency metrics. CRPS-trained ensembles typically achieve more reliable uncertainties on both single-step prediction and autoregressive rollouts, demonstrating better coverage than the standard alternative of training generative models in a latent space. Moreover, the CRPS approach offers significantly faster inference. When generative models are trained in ambient rather than a compressed latent space, which is often infeasible for high-dimensional problems, they exhibit comparable coverage to CRPS-trained ensembles, though with substantially larger inference latency. In contrast, when CRPS-trained ensembles are trained in latent space they do not show a marked degradation in coverage with respect to ambient space. Both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles demonstrate good predictive accuracy. To facilitate future research and application, we release AutoCast, a modular framework implementing both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles, alongside AutoSim, a flexible dataset generation package for rapid prototyping.

</details>


### [192] [scLLM-DSC: LLM-Knowledge Enhanced Cross-Modal Deep Structural Clustering for Single-Cell RNA Sequencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13007)
*Ping Xu,Pengjiang Li,Tian Du,Zaitian Wang,Jiawei Gu,Ziyue Qiao,Pengfei Wang,Yuanchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出scLLM-DSC框架，通过融合LLM语义知识与图拓扑结构，实现跨模态对比对齐的scRNA-seq细胞聚类，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有scRNA-seq聚类方法仅挖掘数值统计模式，忽略基因编码的生物学功能语义，而LLM的语义能力因生成式预训练与判别式任务的结构不匹配而难以直接应用。

Method: 构建双视图框架：知识驱动语义视图（NCBI基因先验+Cell2Sentence上下文嵌入）和结构感知拓扑视图（图引导编码器），并通过跨模态对比对齐机制将生物学语义与转录组特征在统一潜空间中对齐。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，scLLM-DSC在聚类精度上显著优于11个SOTA基线方法。

Conclusion: 融合LLM语义知识与图结构信息的跨模态对比学习范式能够有效提升scRNA-seq细胞聚类的表现，克服了传统数据驱动方法的语义缺失问题。

Abstract: Clustering is fundamental to scRNA-seq analysis, serving as a cornerstone for identifying cell populations and resolving tissue heterogeneity. However, existing methods focus on mining numerical statistical patterns, suffering from semantic agnosticism by neglecting the intrinsic biological functions encoded by genes. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer promising semantic capabilities, their direct adaptation to cell clustering is hindered by the structural mismatch between generative pre-training objectives and discriminative downstream tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose scLLM-DSC, a novel LLM-Knowledge Enhanced Cross-Modal Deep Structural Clustering framework. Diverging from data-driven paradigms, scLLM-DSC establishes a semantically-grounded representation by synergizing two views: a Knowledge-Driven Semantic View derived from NCBI gene priors and contextualized Cell2Sentence embeddings, and a Structure-Aware Topological View extracted via a graph-guided encoder. Crucially, we introduce a cross-modal contrastive alignment mechanism to enforce consistency between biological semantics and transcriptomic features within a unified latent space. Extensive benchmarks demonstrate that scLLM-DSC significantly outperforms eleven state-of-the-art baselines in clustering accuracy.

</details>


### [193] [CausalMoE: A Billion-Scale Multimodal Foundation Model for Granger Causal Discovery with Pattern-Routed Heterogeneous Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13024)
*Bo Liu,Di Dai,Jingwei Liu,Jiarui Jin,Xiaocheng Fang,Guangkun Nie,Hongyan Li,Shenda Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausalMoE是一个亿级规模的多模态Granger因果基础模型，通过模式路由混合专家架构解耦时变机制，结合因果感知自注意力和LLM/VLM先验，在监督和少样本场景下均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经Granger因果发现方法采用"一刀切"范式，无法捕获真实时间序列中的分布偏移和动态机制变化，导致表征纠缠和虚假因果图。

Method: 1) 模式路由混合专家架构，动态识别潜在时间模式并路由到领域专家；2) 因果感知自注意力机制，通过近端优化生成稀疏Granger因果图；3) 首次集成LLM和VLM，对齐数值信号与文本/视觉先验。

Result: 在全监督基准上达到新SOTA，并在传统方法失效的少样本场景下有效泛化。

Conclusion: 显式建模patch级异质性并融合多模态先验是提升因果发现鲁棒性和泛化性的有效途径。

Abstract: Granger Causal Discovery (GCD) is fundamental for analyzing temporal dependencies in complex systems. However, existing neural GCD methods predominantly rely on a "one-size-fits-all" paradigm, struggling to capture distribution shifts and dynamic regime changes inherent in real-world time series. This often leads to entangled representations and spurious causal graphs. In this paper, we propose CausalMoE, a billion-scale multimodal Granger causal foundation model that explicitly models patch-level heterogeneity. CausalMoE introduces a Pattern-Routed Mixture of Heterogeneous Experts, which dynamically identifies latent temporal patterns and routes patches to specialized domain experts, effectively decoupling regime-specific mechanisms from shared dynamics. To ensure interpretable graph recovery, we design a Causality-Aware Self-Attention mechanism operating across variables, yielding sparse Granger causal graphs via proximal optimization. Furthermore, CausalMoE is the first to integrate LLMs and VLMs to align numerical signals with textual and visual priors, regularizing causal estimation in complex scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CausalMoE establishes a new state-of-the-art on fully supervised benchmarks, while effectively generalizing to few-shot settings where traditional methods fail.

</details>


### [194] [TWLA: Achieving Ternary Weights and Low-Bit Activations for LLMs via Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13054)
*Zhixiong Zhao,Zukang Xu,Zhixuan Chen,Xing Hu,Zhe Jiang,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TWLA是一个后训练量化框架，实现了1.58位权重量化和4位激活量化，通过E2M-ATQ、KOTMS和ILA-AMP三个组件解决了激活分布重尾问题，在保持高精度的同时显著加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三元量化方法难以处理激活值的重尾分布，因此只能将激活保持在高精度，这从根本上限制了端到端推理加速。TWLA旨在解决这一限制，实现低比特权重量化与低比特激活量化的兼容。

Method: TWLA包含三个组件：(1) E2M-ATQ：通过从欧几里得初始化到流形重定位的两阶段优化，最小化权重量化下的层输出误差；(2) KOTMS：利用Kronecker结构正交旋转将权重重塑为适合三元量化的三峰分布，同时抑制激活异常值；(3) ILA-AMP：引入层间二阶交互代价进行比特分配，联合优化由共享正交变换引起的激活量化增益的层间差异，防止弱层引发级联效应。

Result: 实验表明，TWLA在W1.58A4设置下保持高精度，同时实现了显著的推理加速。

Conclusion: TWLA通过后训练量化框架成功实现了极低比特权重量化与低比特激活量化的兼容，有效解决了激活重尾分布问题，为LLM的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit exceptional general language processing capabilities, but their memory and compute costs hinder deployment. Ternarization has emerged as a promising compression technique, offering significant reductions in model size and inference complexity. However, existing methods struggle with heavy-tailed activation distributions and therefore keep activations in high precision, fundamentally limiting end-to-end inference acceleration. To overcome this limitation, we propose TWLA, a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that achieves 1.58-bit weight compression and 4-bit activation quantization while maintaining high accuracy. TWLA comprises three components: (1) Euclidean-to-Manifold Asymmetric Ternary Quantizer (E2M-ATQ) minimizes layer-output error under weight ternarization via a two-stage optimization from Euclidean initialization to manifold relocation; (2) Kronecker Orthogonal Tri-Modal Shaping (KOTMS) applies a Kronecker-structured orthogonal rotation to reshape weights into ternary-friendly tri-modal distributions, while the shared rotation statistically suppresses activation outliers; and (3) Inter-Layer Aware Activation Mixed Precision (ILA-AMP) explicitly introduces adjacent-layer second-order interaction costs in bit allocation and jointly optimizes for the layer-wise disparity of activation quantization gains induced by the shared orthogonal transform, preventing cascades triggered by a few weak layers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TWLA maintains high accuracy under W1.58A4, while delivering significant inference acceleration. The code is available at https://github.com/Kishon-zzx/TWLA.

</details>


### [195] [A green solvent screening tool for emerging materials via uncertainty aware, transformer enhanced transfer learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13060)
*Ioannis Kouroudis,Simon Ternes,Zhaosu Gu,Gohar Ali Siddiqui,Marina Ustinova,Angelo Lembo,Alessio Gagliardi,Aldo Di Carlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用预训练基础模型迁移学习，在极少量数据条件下高精度预测溶解度关键参数（Hansen参数、介电常数、Gutmann给体/受体数），并集成不确定性量化，实现了溶解度描述符数量级的数据扩充，最终部署了可用于绿色溶剂筛选与排序的易用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴技术（有机/混合光伏、电池、催化）对溶剂需求激增，亟需用绿色溶剂替代传统溶剂，但溶解度关键参数的实验数据极为有限，严重制约了机器学习模型的效能。

Method: 将预训练的基础模型（基于QM9目标）迁移至溶解度预测任务，仅需极少数据；集成不确定性量化模块以评估预测置信度。

Result: 在Hansen溶解度参数和介电常数（数据充足）上取得良好基线性能；在Gutmann给体/受体数（数据极度稀缺）上实现高模型性能；将溶解度描述符数据扩充了数个数量级；成功发现已知绿色溶剂替代品并提出了新候选。

Conclusion: 迁移学习结合不确定性量化可在数据稀缺条件下高效预测溶解度参数，部署的易用工具可有效支持高通量实验室的绿色溶剂筛选与替代。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of solubility remains a central challenge across materials science and sustainable chemistry. In particular due to emerging technologies like organic and hybrid photovoltaics, batteries, and catalysis, solvent usage is expected to increase significantly within the coming years. Therefore, substituting solvents with greener alternatives is vital. This is where machine learning can have substantial impact. However, the limited data on critical parameters of solubility significantly constraints machine learning efficacy. In this work, we transfer a pre-trained foundational model on QM9 targets to our application with minimal data requirements. Additionally, the pipeline integrates uncertainty quantification, allowing the user to gauge the confidence of the predictions. As baseline, we succeed in predicting the Hansen solubility parameters and Dielectric Constant for which extensive databases exist. Importantly, we achieve high model performance on additional targets, such as Gutmann Donor and Acceptor numbers, where the available data is extremely limited. Overall, we augment data on solubility descriptors by orders of magnitude with high quality predictions. For effective dissemination, we deploy easy-to-use, easily integrateable with high throughput labs, customizable tool for ranking and screening possible solvent substitutes. Finally, we rediscovered known green solvent alternatives and proposed new candidates proving its relevance for finding eco-friendly solvents.

</details>


### [196] [Limits of spectral learning under noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13067)
*Sabin Roman,Ljupco Todorovski,Saso Dzeroski,Marta Sales-Pardo,Roger Guimera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带噪声监督回归中稀疏谱表示的学习问题，发现噪声会导致系数向量发生可预测的漂移，并推导出无噪声与有噪声系数向量重叠度的闭式表达式，揭示了由单一内在噪声尺度控制的普适退化曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从带噪声数据中学习函数关系是科学推理的核心问题，但谱方法在噪声下系数估计的稳定性尚不明确，亟需系统理解。

Method: 研究采用稀疏谱表示，在多个基（Fourier、Legendre、Bessel、Haar）和多维设置下对带加性标签噪声的监督回归进行分析。通过对经验特征几何进行白化处理，推导出噪声与无噪声系数向量重叠度的闭式表达式。

Result: 噪声会在学习到的系数向量中引入可预测的漂移，其幅度取决于有效活跃谱模式的数量；存在一个基本噪声阈值，超过该阈值系数估计将变得不稳定。

Conclusion: 谱学习存在一个内在噪声阈值，限制了从带噪数据中恢复函数结构的可能性，结果在多种基下得到数值验证。

Abstract: Learning functional relationships from noisy data is a central problem in scientific inference. Spectral methods approximate unknown functions by expanding them in a basis and estimating the corresponding coefficients from data, but the stability of these coefficients under noise remains poorly understood. Here we study supervised regression with additive label noise using sparse spectral representations across multiple bases and dimensions. We show that noise induces a predictable drift in the learned coefficient vector whose magnitude depends on the effective number of active spectral modes. After whitening the empirical feature geometry, we derive a closed-form expression for the overlap between noisy and noiseless coefficient vectors, revealing a universal degradation curve governed by a single intrinsic noise scale. Numerical experiments across Fourier, Legendre, Bessel, and Haar bases confirm the theoretical prediction. The results demonstrate that spectral learning exhibits a fundamental noise threshold beyond which coefficient estimates become unstable, placing intrinsic limits on recovering functional structure from noisy data.

</details>


### [197] [Emotional regulation improves deep learning-based image classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13081)
*Riccardo Emanuele Landi,João M. F. Rodrigues,Marta Chinnici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出情感调节（Emotional Regulation）框架，通过人工主观体验建模情感，将其融入深度学习。在图像分类任务中，对ResNet和ViT架构在四个情感数据集上进行预训练，以CIFAR-10/100为基准测试，结果显示该方法优于现有情感增强深度学习模型，达到新SOTA，证明了主观情感对机器学习优化的积极作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感增强深度学习方法仅依赖客观神经生理因素，忽视了情感的主观性。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过引入人工主观体验来建模情感，从而提升深度学习模型的泛化性能。

Method: 提出Emotional Regulation框架，核心包括：(1) 基于情感刺激的预训练；(2) 在下游任务优化中平衡非情感响应与情感影响响应。在ResNet和ViT架构上，使用四个情感数据集进行预训练，以CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100作为目标基准进行图像分类实验。

Result: 所提方法在ResNet和ViT骨干网络上均取得性能提升，超越了现有情感增强深度学习相关方法，在基于CIFAR的大规模视觉数据集图像分类任务上达到新的SOTA。

Conclusion: 情感调节框架通过人工主观体验定义情感增强深度学习是一种有前景的方法，证实了情感状态对机器学习任务优化的积极影响，鼓励进一步探索情感启发式架构。

Abstract: Emotion significantly influences cognition, enhancing memory and learning under certain conditions. Drawing on this principle, emotion-augmented deep learning investigates how affective states can improve neural network architectures and learning paradigms, achieving better generalization than non-emotional models. However, existing methods often rely solely on objective neurophysiological factors, neglecting the role of subjectivity in emotion. To bridge this gap, the present study introduces Emotional Regulation, a novel framework for modeling emotion in deep learning through artificial subjective experience. The method employs pre-training based on affective stimuli, balancing non-emotional and emotionally-influenced responses in downstream task optimization. Extensive experimentation was conducted in image classification, pre-training ResNet and ViT architectures on four emotional datasets, using CIFAR-10 and -100 as target benchmarks. Results reveal improvements over the aforementioned backbones, providing evidence of Emotional Regulation as a promising method for defining emotion-augmented deep learning through artificial subjective experience. Furthermore, the proposed approach overcomes the related work in image classification based on CIFAR, revealing Emotional Regulation as the new state-of-the-art in emotion-augmented deep learning for large-scale vision datasets. The study also enforces evidence of the impact of affective states in improving machine learning tasks' optimization, encouraging further investigation on emotion-inspired architectures.

</details>


### [198] [Authority, Truth, and Citation Bias: A Large-Scale Multi-Domain Benchmark for Studying Epistemic Susceptibility in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13104)
*Aryan Khurana,Aravind Ramana RN,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuthorityBench基准，通过22万+提示的平衡因子设计实验，发现引用(无论真实与否)会显著增加大模型的幻觉率，其中伪造引用伴随真实声明的场景增幅最大(3-22个百分点)，法律领域相对鲁棒，而场所声望和作者人口统计学影响可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究忽略了引用存在本身(独立于事实内容)对模型认知行为的影响，缺乏系统性评估引用权威信号如何改变大模型认知行为的基准。

Method: 构建220,564条提示的多领域基准，采用2x2因子设计(声明真实性×引用真实性)，覆盖4个领域(通用知识、科学、法律、医学)，控制40个提示模板、4个场所声望等级和国家编码的作者姓名数据集，在7个模型上评估12个结构化研究问题。

Result: 引用存在一致性地提高幻觉率；伪造引用伴随真实声明时增幅最大(3-22个百分点)，通用知识领域达到35-77%；法律领域相对鲁棒；场所声望和作者人口统计影响可忽略。

Conclusion: 引用权威信号会系统性地诱导大模型产生更多幻觉，即使引用是伪造的，这一发现对检索增强生成(RAG)系统的可靠性设计具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in citation-augmented settings, yet the effect of citation presence on model behavior independent of factual content remains poorly understood. We introduce AuthorityBench, a 220,564-prompt multi-domain benchmark that isolates how citation-based authority signals influence epistemic behavior in LLMs. The benchmark uses a fully balanced 2x2 factorial design crossing claim veracity with citation veracity, the first to do so, across four domains (general knowledge, science, law, and medicine), with controlled variation over 40 prompt templates, four venue prestige tiers, and a country-coded author name dataset. Evaluating seven models on 12 structured research questions, we find that citation presence, whether real or fabricated, consistently increases hallucination rates relative to a no-citation baseline. The effect is strongest when fabricated citations accompany true claims, raising hallucination rates by 3 to 22 percentage points and reaching 35 to 77% in the general knowledge domain, while legal claims are comparatively robust and venue prestige and author demographics show negligible impact. All datasets and evaluation code are available at: https://github.com/floating-reeds/AuthorityBench

</details>


### [199] [Disparate Impact in Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13105)
*Paul Andrey,Michaël Perrot,Batiste Le Bars,Marc Tommasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了合成数据生成（SDG）中的差异影响公平性概念，指出真正的非差异影响应使合成分布与真实分布一致，而非纠正真实分布中的偏差。文章分析了SDG因表达能力、采样误差和差分隐私估计误差导致对群体产生差异影响的原因，并提出了一种分组学习SDG模型的策略来改善效用及其公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平SDG工作通过纠正观测分布中的不当偏差来定义公平，这实际上重新定义了SDG为学习一个不同于真实数据的分布。作者认为非差异影响应体现在合成分布与真实分布一致，因此需要重新审视并分析SDG为何可能无法达到这一目标。

Method: 论文从理论上分析了SDG产生差异影响的三个来源：1）SDG方法表达能力相对于数据分布复杂度的限制；2）群体比例导致的采样误差；3）差分隐私机制引入的估计误差。通过人工和真实数据实验，以概率图模型为例说明差异影响现象，并提出分组学习SDG模型的缓解策略。

Result: 实验证明了SDG方法确实会因上述三种误差导致对敏感群体产生差异影响。分组学习SDG模型策略能够在多种设定下同时提升整体效用和效用均等性。

Conclusion: 公平SDG不应定义为纠正真实分布中的偏差，而应追求合成分布与真实分布的一致性。SDG的差异影响源于技术局限而非数据本身的偏差，分组学习策略是一种有效的缓解方案。

Abstract: We revisit the fairness notion of disparate impact for synthetic data generation (SDG), that assesses whether the utility of generated records is the same across sensitive groups. Our approach departs from existing work on fair SDG, that address the problem of correcting for undue biases in the observed distribution, hence redefining SDG as learning a distribution that is not that of the real data. By contrast, non-disparate impact is notably achieved when the synthetic and real distributions are the same. We expose reasons why SDG may fail to reach that solution and discuss why approximation and estimation errors occur and can be disparate across groups. We notably look into the expressive power of SDG methods relative to distribution complexity, sampling errors due to group proportions, and estimation errors induced by differential privacy mechanisms. We illustrate cases of disparate impact on both artificial and real-world data, focusing on SDG methods that rely on probabilistic graphical models. We also introduce a strategy of learning group-wise SDG models and illustrate how it can improve both the overall utility and its parity in many settings.

</details>


### [200] [Demystifying Hidden-State Recurrence: Switchable Latent Reasoning with On-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13106)
*Jiayu Yang,Chao Chen,Shengen Wu,Yinhong Liu,Yuxuan Fan,Lujundong Li,Songning Lai,Chengwei Qin,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SWITCH框架，通过离散边界标记<swi>和</swi>将隐式链式推理与标准策略强化学习(GRPO)兼容，并支持因果机制分析。实验证明该方法优于现有隐状态循环推理方法，并通过边界标记揭示了隐式推理的三个机制性发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式链式推理（latent chain-of-thought）虽然通过连续隐状态循环压缩推理过程，但难以用标准在线强化学习（on-policy RL）优化，且无法进行因果机制解释。需要一种既能兼容RL训练又支持可解释性分析的隐式推理框架。

Method: 提出SWITCH框架：1) 在模型输入和输出端分别插入离散边界标记<swi>（进入隐式模式）和</swi>（退出隐式模式），使GRPO策略比率在每个决策点明确定义；2) 采用可见到隐式的课程学习（visible-to-latent curriculum）训练；3) 设计Switch-GRPO目标函数，通过循环隐式计算传播梯度。

Result: SWITCH在相同规模下持续优于现有的隐状态循环推理方法。通过边界标记的机制分析揭示：1) <swi>是高度局域化的学习性切换策略而非风格伪影；2) 隐式步骤执行问题特定的因果重要计算；3) 该计算集中在入口处的单个隐状态转换上。

Conclusion: 隐状态循环推理（latent reasoning）既可以兼容RL训练，也支持直接的因果机制分析，包括分析RL如何从内部改进模型本身。

Abstract: Latent chain-of-thought compresses reasoning by replacing visible reasoning traces with continuous hidden-state recurrence, but existing formulations are difficult to optimize with standard on-policy reinforcement learning (RL) and hard to interpret causally. Our key insight is that a single pair of explicit boundary tokens can address both issues at once: discrete entry and exit anchors make the latent block compatible with standard on-policy RL, and the same anchors offer a natural foothold for mechanistic analysis. Motivated by this, we propose SWITCH, a switchable latent reasoning framework. The model emits <swi> to enter latent mode and </swi> to exit. Because the boundaries are ordinary discrete tokens, the GRPO policy ratio is well-defined at every decision point. The same anchors also expose the latent steps to direct probing and causal intervention. We train the model with a visible-to-latent curriculum and a Switch-GRPO objective that propagates gradients through recurrent latent computation. SWITCH consistently outperforms prior hidden-state-recurrence latent reasoning approaches at similar scale. Mechanistic analysis through the boundary tokens further reveals three findings: (i) <swi> is a sharply localised, learned switching policy rather than a stylistic artefact; (ii) the latent step it opens performs problem-specific, causally important computation rather than acting as an inert placeholder; and (iii) that computation is concentrated at a single hidden-state transition on entry. Together, these results show that hidden-state-recurrence latent reasoning is both RL-trainable and open to direct mechanistic analysis, including of how on-policy RL itself improves the model from the inside.

</details>


### [201] [DiffCoord: Differentiable Coordination for Distributed Multi-Agent Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01630)
*Bingheng Wang,Yichao Gao,Tianchen Sun,Shanker Ajay,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffCoord框架，通过端到端可微分的元学习联合优化截断ADMM-DDP多智能体轨迹优化中的耦合参数，利用智能体共享神经网络实现异构团队规模的自适应，并在合作空中运输系统中验证了其高效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADMM与DDP结合用于分布式多智能体轨迹优化时，截断操作导致原本分离的协调质量和任务性能参数紧密耦合，且人工调参困难，缺乏自适应能力。

Method: 提出DiffCoord框架，使用智能体级神经网络生成截断ADMM-DDP管线的参数，同构智能体共享网络以实现规模可扩展；通过端到端微分实现元学习，并导出一个辅助的ADMM-LQR分布式梯度求解器，复用管线关键计算结果，实现高效并行化。

Result: 在合作空中运输系统中，DiffCoord能够重构四旋翼编队以实现6自由度负载安全操控；适应不同团队规模和负载动力学；相比SOTA轨迹梯度方法，每个智能体的梯度计算时间降低达70%。

Conclusion: DiffCoord通过元学习框架有效解决了截断ADMM-DDP中参数耦合与调参难题，在保持分布式可扩展性的同时显著提升了计算效率，适用于多智能体协同任务。

Abstract: Integrating the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) with Differential Dynamic Programming (DDP) provides a scalable framework for distributed multi-agent trajectory optimization. In practice, ADMM is typically truncated for computational efficiency, tightly coupling parameters that would otherwise separately govern coordination quality and task performance. In this paper, we propose Differentiable Coordination (DiffCoord), a unified framework that jointly meta-learns these coupled parameters for the truncated ADMM-DDP pipeline. These parameters are generated by agent-wise neural networks for task adaptation, and the same networks are shared among isomorphic agents to enable scalability to varying agent counts. We achieve efficient meta-learning by differentiating the ADMM-DDP pipeline end-to-end. Notably, this yields an auxiliary ADMM-LQR distributed gradient solver that computes and coordinates meta-gradients with respect to these parameters. This solver inherits the computational structure of the pipeline, enabling reuse of key computation results and efficient parallelization over agents and along trajectory horizons. We validate DiffCoord through numerical and physical experiments on a cooperative aerial transport system, where it reconfigures quadrotor formations for safe 6-DoF load manipulation in tight spaces. It adapts robustly to varying team sizes and load dynamics, while reducing per-agent gradient computation time by up to 70% compared with state-of-the-art trajectory-gradient methods.

</details>


### [202] [MP3: Multi-Period Pattern Pre-training for Spatio-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13119)
*Lilan Peng,Yandi Liu,Qingren Yao,Chongshou Li,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MP3的多周期模式预训练插件，用于解决城市时空数据中的"时间幻象"问题（即短窗口输入相似但未来趋势不同）。MP3通过多周期模式学习、全局空间建模和因果增强Transformer来区分时间幻象，可无缝集成到现有STGNN中，在五个数据集上平均降低MAE 4.7%、RMSE 5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STGNN无法有效识别时空数据中的"时间幻象"现象（短窗口输入相似但未来趋势不同，或反之），其根本原因在于短窗口输入存在周期观测不完整、全局空间异质性关联以及跨周期叠加因果性。

Method: 提出MP3插件，包含三个核心模块：(1) 多周期时间建模：利用边卷积识别不同多周期模式；(2) 多周期空间建模：采用瓶颈投影和全局记忆库捕获异质全局空间关系；(3) 跨周期模式交互：使用因果增强Transformer建模不同周期模式间的依赖关系。

Result: 在五个STGNN基线模型和五个真实数据集（含大规模数据集CA）上验证了有效性，MP3带来一致且稳健的性能提升，平均降低MAE 4.7%、RMSE 5.0%。

Conclusion: MP3作为一种即插即用的预训练插件，能有效区分时间幻象，具有优秀的可扩展性和强适应性，可显著提升现有STGNN的时空预测性能。

Abstract: Spatio-Temporal forecasting is crucial in diverse fields, such as transportation, climate, and energy. Urban spatio-temporal data exhibits temporal mirage: similar short-window inputs have divergent future trends, and vice versa. Existing spatio-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs) cannot effectively identify such mirages. We argue that the core reason lies in the short-window inputs that have incomplete period observation, heterogeneous global spatial correlation, and cross-period superposition causality. To bridge this gap, we develop a novel Multi- Period Pattern Pre-training (MP3), a plug-and-play pre-training plugin for distinguishing temporal mirages. MP3 presents two core innovations: (1) The multi-period pattern learning is designed to learn multi-period patterns from long time series. Specifically, multi-period temporal modeling leverages edge convolution to identify different multi-period patterns. Multi-period spatial modeling uses a bottleneck project and a global memory bank to capture heterogeneous global spatial relations efficiently. Cross-period pattern interaction employs a causality-enhanced Transformer to capture dependencies across different period patterns. (2) This plugin can seamlessly integrate into existing STGNN backbones to strengthen their forecasting performance. The experiment on five STGNN baselines across five real-world datasets (including a large-scale dataset CA) verify the effectiveness, superior scalability and strong adaptability of MP3, which brings consistent and robust performance improvements across all evaluated baselines. On average, MP3 reduces the MAE 4.7% and the RMSE 5.0%. The code can be available at https://github.com/YAN-outlook/MP3.

</details>


### [203] [Select and Improve: Understanding the Mechanics of Post-Training for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13125)
*Akshay Krishnamurthy,Audrey Huang,Nived Rajaraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过数学推理实验，揭示了强化学习后训练中能力获取的两个核心机制：策略选择（strategy selection）和策略改进（strategy improvement），并阐明了SFT数据和RL数据在激活这些机制中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽已广泛应用于推理和代码模型训练，但其背后的机制性理解仍然不足，作者希望从机制层面揭示RL后训练如何获取或增强能力。

Method: 基于Qwen-2.5-1.5B模型，在受控的数学推理实验中进行强化学习后训练，分析能力获取的底层过程。

Result: 发现两个核心机制：策略选择（strategy selection）和策略改进（strategy improvement）。SFT数据通过展示多样化的推理策略来激活策略选择机制；RL数据通过增加难度来激活策略改进机制。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了RL训练的机制性洞察，并为继续扩展推理能力提出了实用的干预建议。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has rapidly emerged as a key component in the training of reasoning and coding models, yet it remains poorly understood from a mechanistic perspective. We study how and through what underlying processes capabilities are acquired or enhanced via reinforcement learning post-training. Our analysis, based on controlled math reasoning experiments with Qwen-2.5-1.5B, reveals two core mechanisms: strategy selection and strategy improvement. Our results highlight the role of SFT data and reinforcement learning data in activating these mechanisms, in particular showing how supervising the model on diverse reasoning strategies can enable strategy selection and how increasing difficulty in reinforcement learning data can enable strategy improvement. Taken together, our results provide mechanistic insight into RL training and suggest practical interventions to continue scaling reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [204] [MiniPIC: Flexible Position-Independent Caching in <100LOC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13126)
*Nathan Ordonez,Thomas Parnell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MiniPIC通过无位置编码的KV缓存和用户控制缓存复用原语，在vLLM中实现了最小化、灵活且快速的PIC，仅需不到100行核心引擎修改即可支持多种位置无关缓存方法，在2WikiMultihopQA上预填吞吐量提升49%，缓存跨度首令牌延迟降低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强和智能体工作负载反复预填可预测的结构化输入（如文档和代码文件），但现有前缀缓存机制要求请求共享完全相同的前缀才能复用KV条目，而生产级推理服务器的位置无关缓存实现要么需要大量服务器代码修改，要么将KV状态保存在服务器外引入设备间传输开销。

Method: MiniPIC由两个核心组件构成：1) 无位置编码的KV缓存——在缓存中存储未旋转的K向量，在注意力计算时按请求逻辑位置对K tiles应用RoPE；2) 用户控制的缓存复用原语——三个令牌级原语（块对齐填充、跨度分隔符SSep、提示依赖PDep）修改哈希行为和块级因果注意力结构。

Result: 在2WikiMultihopQA上，结合交错调度，MiniPIC相比基准vLLM提升预填吞吐量49%，缓存跨度首令牌延迟降低最多两个数量级，保持未缓存跨度的线性预填扩展，仅引入5.7%最坏情况开销。

Conclusion: MiniPIC以极简设计实现了多种PIC方法的统一支持，证明了通过少量核心引擎修改即可在生产级推理服务器中高效集成位置无关缓存能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented and agentic workloads repeatedly prefill recurring predictable structured inputs (which we call "spans") such as documents and code files. Yet, prefix caching in engines such as vLLM cannot reuse their KV entries unless they share identical prefixes with another request, while Position-Independent Caching (PIC) implementations within production-grade inference servers typically either require substantial server code changes or keep KV state outside the server, incurring host-to-device transfer overhead. We present Minimalistic PIC (MiniPIC): a minimal, flexible and fast vLLM design built from two ingredients: positional-encoding-free KV cache and user-controlled cache-reuse primitives. MiniPIC stores unrotated K vectors in the KV cache, applies RoPE to K tiles inside attention using per-request logical positions, and exposes three user-facing and token-level primitives: block-aligned padding, span separator (SSep), and prompt depend (PDep), that modify hashing behavior and effective block-level causal attention structure. With fewer than 100 lines of core-engine changes plus a custom attention backend, these primitives are sufficient to realize multiple PIC methods, including Block-Attention, EPIC, and Prompt Cache, within the same running vLLM instance, while natively integrating with KV cache CPU offload implementations. On 2WikiMultihopQA, MiniPIC with interleaved scheduling improves prefill throughput by 49% over baseline vLLM, reduces cached-span time-to-first-token by up to two orders of magnitude, preserves the linear prefill scaling of uncached spans, and incurs only 5.7% worst-case overhead.

</details>


### [205] [WOMBET: World Model-Based Experience Transfer for Robust and Sample-efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08958)
*Mintae Kim,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WOMBET框架，通过在学习源任务世界模型时采用不确定性惩罚规划生成离线数据，再结合自适应采样的在线微调，实现从源任务到目标任务的高效经验迁移，显著提升样本效率与最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人强化学习受限于数据采集的高成本和风险，现有离线到在线RL方法通常假设固定数据集，缺乏如何生成可靠可迁移数据的机制。

Method: 在源任务中学习世界模型，采用不确定性惩罚规划生成离线数据，筛选高回报且低认知不确定性的轨迹；然后在目标任务中结合离线与在线数据自适应采样进行在线微调。

Result: 在连续控制基准任务上，WOMBET在样本效率和最终性能上均优于多个强基线方法。

Conclusion: 联合优化数据生成与迁移能够有效提升迁移学习效果，不确定性惩罚目标可为真实回报提供下界，并给出了有限样本误差分解的理论分析。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) in robotics is often limited by the cost and risk of data collection, motivating experience transfer from a source task to a target task. Offline-to-online RL leverages prior data but typically assumes a given fixed dataset and does not address how to generate reliable data for transfer. We propose World Model-Based Experience Transfer (WOMBET), a framework that jointly generates and utilizes prior data. WOMBET learns a world model in the source task and generates offline data via uncertainty-penalized planning, followed by filtering trajectories with high return and low epistemic uncertainty. It then performs online fine-tuning in the target task using adaptive sampling between offline and online data, enabling a stable transition from prior-driven initialization to task-specific adaptation. We show that the uncertainty-penalized objective provides a lower bound on the true return and derive a finite-sample error decomposition capturing distribution mismatch and approximation error. Empirically, WOMBET improves sample efficiency and final performance over strong baselines on continuous control benchmarks, demonstrating the benefit of jointly optimizing data generation and transfer.

</details>


### [206] [When Does Routing Become Interpretable? Causal Probes on Block Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13168)
*Aydin Javadov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Block Attention Residuals机制在可解释性中的作用，发现仅暴露路由权重不足以进行机械论解释：路由权重需参与训练才能形成结构化模式，且平均路由质量与因果重要性存在显著分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将原本隐含的信息流通过Block AttnRes中的可观察路由张量暴露出来后，是否足以支撑机械论层面的模型可解释性分析。

Method: 对比两个同等规模（0.6B）的Block AttnRes检查点：一个使用确定性近因偏差调度包装的Qwen3基线，另一个从头训练包含路由优化的Block AttnRes Qwen3。通过相同的路由消融干预实验进行分析。

Result: 包装基线的路由权重与内容无关且再现了调度的分析预测；训练模型展现出三种局部路由模式（嵌入源路径、当前状态路径、较早历史路径）；平均路由质量与因果重要性存在显著分离——最大质量切片不是最大因果贡献，且有一类源家族携带可观质量但无可检测的因果作用。

Conclusion: 架构层面暴露路由是必要但不充分的：结构化深度路由仅当路由参与训练时才出现，且描述性路由摘要应被视为待因果检验的候选假设，而非机制本身的证据。

Abstract: Block Attention Residuals (Block AttnRes) by replace fixed additive residuals with a learned softmax over earlier depth-source representations, surfacing cross-layer routing as an inspectable tensor in the forward pass. This is a tempting interpretability target: information flow normally inferred indirectly is now directly observable. We ask whether such exposure suffices for mechanistic interpretation. We probe two same-scale ($0.6$B) Block AttnRes checkpoints under identical routing-ablation interventions: a vanilla Qwen3 inference-wrapped through a deterministic recency-bias schedule that the codebase admits as a routing-equivalent loading path, and a Block AttnRes Qwen3 trained from scratch with routing as part of optimisation. The wrapped baseline's routing weights are content-independent and reproduce the schedule's analytic prediction. The trained AttnRes checkpoint instead exhibits three localised routing motifs: an embedding-source pathway through early-layer MLP, a current-state pathway through early-layer attention and MLP, and an older-history pathway through late-layer attention. Beyond this stratification, we find a sharp dissociation between average routing mass and causal importance: in both sublayers, the largest mass slice is not the largest causal contribution, and one source family carries appreciable mass with no detectable causal role under intervention. Architectural exposure of routing is therefore necessary but not sufficient for mechanistic interpretation: structured depth routing emerges only when routing has been part of training, and even then, descriptive routing summaries should be treated as candidate hypotheses to be tested by causal interventions, not as evidence of mechanism in their own right.

</details>


### [207] [Detecting Explanatory Insufficiency in Learned Representations: A Framework for Representational Vigilance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13172)
*Jacques Raynal,Pierre Slangen,Elsa Raynal,Jacques Margerit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VER（警惕性表征评估器）概念框架，用于监控学习表征的充分性。VER不提出新算法或损失函数，而是形式化一个诊断过程，通过识别、分析和解释持续存在的残差结构，将其作为表征解释不足的潜在指标。该框架区分表征不充分与普通预测误差、不确定性、噪声和分布偏移，并引入基于表征识别、解释域界定、残差结构检测、解释阻力和警惕信号发出的监控序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表征评估方法（如预测性能、鲁棒性、不确定性估计和泛化能力）可能无法捕捉到表征中持续存在的残差结构，这些结构虽然不影响操作成功，但可能暗示表征的解释性不足。因此需要一种新的诊断框架来补充现有评估方法。

Method: VER框架通过一个监控序列来运作：表征识别→解释域界定→残差结构检测→解释阻力评估→警惕信号发出。该框架将表征不充分性与普通预测误差、不确定性、噪声和分布偏移区分开来，并提出了通过表征警惕基准进行实证评估的路径。

Result: 本文未提供具体实验结果，而是概念性地提出了VER框架及其组成部分，包括监控序列的设计和实证评估路径的概述。

Conclusion: VER作为表征诊断学的贡献，旨在补充而非替代现有评估方法，将表征充分性作为明确的探究对象。未来方向包括通过表征警惕基准进行实证评估。

Abstract: Learned representations are central to modern machine learning and are commonly evaluated through predictive performance, robustness, uncertainty estimation, or generalization. However, a learned representation may remain operationally successful while progressively failing to organize persistent residual structures that are not fully captured by conventional evaluation metrics. This article introduces VER, the Vigilant Evaluator of Representations, a conceptual framework for monitoring representational adequacy in learned representations. VER does not propose a new learning algorithm, loss function, or model architecture. Instead, it formalizes a diagnostic process through which persistent residual structures may be identified, analyzed, and interpreted as potential indicators of explanatory insufficiency. The framework distinguishes representational inadequacy from ordinary prediction error, uncertainty, noise, and distribution shift. It introduces a monitoring sequence based on representation identification, explanatory-domain delimitation, residual-structure detection, explanatory-resistance evaluation, and vigilance signaling. VER is intended as a contribution to representation diagnostics in machine learning. Its objective is not to replace existing evaluation methods but to complement them by treating representational adequacy as an explicit object of inquiry. A path toward empirical evaluation through representational-vigilance benchmarks is also outlined.

</details>


### [208] [Getting Better at Working With You: Compiling User Corrections into Runtime Enforcement for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13174)
*Yujun Zhou,Kehan Guo,Haomin Zhuang,Xiangqi Wang,Yue Huang,Zhenwen Liang,Pin-Yu Chen,Tian Gao,Nuno Moniz,Nitesh V. Chawla,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，一种将用户对话中的修正信息编译为运行时检查规则的方法，以解决LLM智能体在多次会话中反复违背已纠正偏好行为的问题。实验表明，相比Mem0记忆系统（57.5%违规率），TRACE在ClawArena上将违规率从100.0%降至37.6%（分布内）和2.0%（分布外），在MemoryArena任务上从100.0%降至60.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体在交互过程中虽能通过记忆系统保存用户偏好，但这些偏好往往无法在跨会话中得到可靠遵守——一个会话中纠正过的行为在后续会话中仍可能再次出现。这种偏好访问与偏好遵守之间的差距导致用户需要在未来会话中重复纠正相同问题，降低了交互效率和使用体验。

Method: TRACE（Test-time Rule Acquisition and Compiled Enforcement）是一个即插即用的技能层流水线，包含三个步骤：（1）从用户真实的对话修正中挖掘纠正信息；（2）将这些信息重写为原子化的规则；（3）将规则编译为运行时检查，确保智能体在完成未来任务前必须通过这些检查。该方法无需开发者预写规则，规则完全来自用户自己的对话修正。

Result: 在ClawArena任务上，TRACE将保留的偏好违规率从100.0%降至37.6%（分布内）和2.0%（分布外）。在MemoryArena衍生任务上，将分布内违规率从100.0%降至60.5%，同时在任务通过率上达到或超过最强记忆基线。而对比Mem0记忆系统，仍有57.5%的适用偏好检查被违反。

Conclusion: 将用户修正编译为运行时强制执行规则，可以有效解决记忆系统单独无法可靠处理的重复摩擦问题，显著减少用户在未来会话中重复陈述相同修正的需求。代码已开源。

Abstract: Interactive LLM agents are becoming part of daily work, but they do not reliably become easier to work with over time: a correction remembered in one session may still be violated in the next. We study this gap between preference access and preference compliance. In tasks derived from anonymized real-user friction cases, Mem0 memory still leaves 57.5% of applicable preference checks violated. We introduce Test-time Rule Acquisition and Compiled Enforcement (TRACE), a drop-in skill-layer pipeline for coding-agent runtimes that mines user corrections, rewrites them as atomic rules, and compiles them into runtime checks that must pass before an agent completes future tasks. Unlike runtime checks written ahead of time by developers, TRACE skills come from the user's own chat corrections. We evaluate TRACE with simulated user-in-the-loop experiments on ClawArena coding-agent tasks and MemoryArena-derived memory-intensive tasks. On ClawArena, TRACE reduces held-out preference violation from 100.0% to 37.6% on in-distribution tasks and from 100.0% to 2.0% on out-of-distribution tasks. On MemoryArena-derived tasks, TRACE reduces in-distribution violation from 100.0% to 60.5% while matching or exceeding the strongest memory baseline on task pass. These results suggest that compiling corrections into runtime enforcement can address a repeated-friction failure mode that memory alone does not reliably solve, reducing the need for users to restate the same correction across future sessions. Experiment code is available at https://github.com/YujunZhou/TRACE_exp, and the deployable skill is available at https://github.com/YujunZhou/tellonce.

</details>


### [209] [Loss-Shift Transfer via Bayes Quotients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13178)
*Vasileios Sevetlidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与分布偏移正交的迁移学习失败模式——损失偏移（loss shift），即在固定数据分布下，不同损失函数需要不同的最优表示，并通过贝叶斯商（Bayes quotients）形式化证明了这一现象，在有限输出对数损失下给出了精确的定量关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迁移学习主要研究分布偏移（distribution shift）带来的问题，但作者发现即使数据分布固定，损失函数的变化也可能导致表示学习失败，从而识别出一个被忽视的失败模式。

Method: 引入贝叶斯商（Bayes quotients）对损失函数进行排序（粗化 vs. 细化），从定性上证明严格细化损失会导致源最小表示不充分；对于有限输出的对数损失，进一步给出精确的定量关系：超额风险等于表示所丢弃的条件信息量。

Result: 在受控实验、学习实验、合成图像和真实图像实验中都观察到了预测效应：分类等价的表示在固定数据分布下可能具有不同的最优对数损失性能。

Conclusion: 损失偏移是与分布偏移正交的迁移学习障碍，即便数据分布不变，仅因损失函数不同也需重新学习表示。

Abstract: Transfer learning is usually studied as a consequence of distribution shift. This paper identifies an orthogonal failure mode in which the data distribution is fixed and the loss changes. This setting is called \emph{loss shift}. A loss determines which information in \(X\) is Bayes-relevant, and two losses may therefore require different representations even under the same joint law \(P(X,Y)\). The idea is formalized using Bayes quotients, which allow losses to be ordered by refinement. In the Bayes-quotient formulation, strict refinement gives an immediate qualitative obstruction. A source-minimal representation for a coarser loss is insufficient for a strictly finer target loss. For finite-output log loss, this obstruction becomes an exact quantitative identity. The excess risk is the conditional information about \(Y\) discarded by the representation. Experiments in controlled, learned, synthetic-image, and real-image settings show the predicted effect, i.e., classification-equivalent representations can have different optimal log-loss performance under a fixed data distribution.

</details>


### [210] [Distributional Loss for Robust Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13223)
*Kathleen Anderson,Thomas Martinetz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的监督分类损失函数，通过将分类器输出建模为双峰高斯分布，替代传统的硬标签映射，从而隐式处理类别模糊性、缓解过拟合并学习更鲁棒的决策边界，在低数据场景下提升尤为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督分类强制每个输入样本映射到单一标签，忽略了类别间的模糊性和不确定性，容易导致过拟合和决策边界不鲁棒。作者希望在不依赖额外标签信息的前提下，设计一种更柔性的优化目标来缓解这些问题。

Method: 将分类器所有输出上的优化目标定义为双峰高斯分布，替代标准的硬标签（如one-hot）或软标签形式。通过这种分布建模，隐式地编码类别间的模糊关系，同时保持训练流程的最小改动。

Result: 在多个任务上取得了鲁棒性的一致提升，尤其在低数据（小样本）场景下增益更为显著。

Conclusion: 所提出的双峰高斯分布损失函数是一种简单有效的替代方案，能够在几乎不改变标准训练流程的前提下提升分类器的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel loss concept for supervised classification tasks. Rather than enforcing a direct mapping from each input sample to a single assigned label, we define an optimization objective over all classifier outputs as a bimodal Gaussian distribution. This softer target formulation implicitly captures class ambiguity, mitigates overfitting, and encourages the learning of more robust decision boundaries, all without requiring additional label information. Experimental results demonstrate consistent improvements in robustness, with particularly pronounced gains in low-data regimes, while requiring only minimal modifications to standard training pipelines.

</details>


### [211] [The Geometry of Phase Transitions in Generative Dynamics via Projection Caustics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13191)
*Ryosuke Sakamoto,Kotaro Sakamoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度解释了扩散模型和流匹配模型中样本生成过程中的相变行为，指出这些突变发生在投影焦散点附近，并提出了临界边界检测器（CBD）作为诊断工具，用于定位模态承诺、预测干预敏感窗口，并支持在几何敏感区域进行针对性控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流匹配模型在连续逆时间演化中常出现样本的剧烈定性变化（如模态承诺、语义替代坍塌），现有研究缺乏对这些相变行为的几何解释和系统诊断工具。

Method: 将去噪过程视为自由能 landscape 上的梯度下降，指出在投影焦散点（nearest-point projection onto data support 不再唯一）附近会出现尖锐转变。基于此提出临界边界检测器（CBD）作为分数方向不稳定性的实用诊断工具。

Result: 在玩具模型、标准扩散模型和潜在文本到图像扩散模型上，CBD 能够定位模态承诺、预测干预敏感窗口，并在几何敏感区域实现针对性控制。

Conclusion: 建立了数据几何与扩散生成动力学之间的联系，为理解和控制生成过程中的相变行为提供了理论框架和实用工具。

Abstract: Continuous-state generative samplers, including diffusion and flow-matching models, evolve through continuous reverse-time dynamics, yet their samples often undergo abrupt qualitative changes: trajectories commit to modes, semantic alternatives collapse, and small perturbations in narrow time windows can produce large downstream effects. This paper develops a geometric account of such phase-transition-like behaviour. We view denoising as gradient descent on a free energy landscape and show that sharp transitions arise near projection caustics, where the nearest-point projection onto the data support ceases to be unique. Motivated by this perspective, we introduce the Critical Boundary Detector (CBD), as practical diagnostics for score-direction instability. Across toy models, standard diffusion models, and latent text-to-image diffusion models, CBD localises mode commitment, predicts intervention-sensitive windows, and supports targeted control in geometrically sensitive regions. Our results connect geometry of data and dynamics of diffusion generation.

</details>


### [212] [Towards More General Control of Diffusion Models Using Jeffrey Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13240)
*Raphaël Razafindralambo,Rémy Sun,Frédéric Precioso,Jes Frellsen,Pierre-Alexandre Mattei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Jeffrey guidance，一个基于Jeffrey条件化规则的原则性框架，用于扩展扩散模型在采样时的控制能力，使其能处理标准引导方法无法表达的应用场景。通过将边际分布更新为预设目标，该框架在保持条件结构的同时最小化对联合分布的扰动。在CIFAR-10和FFHQ上使用Inception嵌入作为目标，显著降低了FID；在CelebA-HQ上应用于公平性任务，实现了属性间的独立性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在采样时通过引导（guidance）控制输出具有灵活性，但现有方法在复杂情况下（超出简单条件采样）的目标分布往往是隐式的，仅通过采样规则或启发式能量函数定义，缺乏一个原则性的框架来扩展其表达能力。

Method: 提出Jeffrey guidance框架，利用Jeffrey条件化规则（Jeffrey's rule of conditioning）将边际分布更新为指定的目标分布，在保持原始条件结构的同时最小化对联合分布的扰动，从而实现对扩散模型输出的更灵活控制。

Result: 1）以Inception嵌入作为目标分布时，在CIFAR-10和FFHQ数据集上显著降低了FID分数；2）在CelebA-HQ数据集上应用于公平性任务，成功更新无条件扩散模型以强制属性间的独立性。

Conclusion: Jeffrey guidance是一个原则性的框架，能够扩展扩散模型在采样时的控制能力，处理标准引导方法无法表达的应用，并在图像质量和公平性控制上取得了实质性改进。

Abstract: A key strength of diffusion models lies in their flexibility, since their outputs can be controlled at sampling time through guidance. However, beyond simple cases such as conditional sampling, the target distribution is often left implicit, defined only through a sampling rule or a heuristic energy function. To address this, we propose Jeffrey guidance, a principled framework that extends diffusion-model control to applications beyond what standard guidance can express. It leverages Jeffrey's rule of conditioning to update marginal distributions towards a prescribed target, preserving the conditional structure and minimally perturbing the joint distribution. We first demonstrate Jeffrey guidance by targeting a prescribed embedding distribution. With Inception embeddings as the target, this leads to substantial reductions in FID on both CIFAR-10 and FFHQ. We further apply Jeffrey guidance to fairness on CelebA-HQ, updating an unconditional diffusion model to enforce independence between attributes.

</details>


### [213] [WHAR Arena: Benchmarking the State of the Art in Efficient Wearable Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13194)
*Maximilian Burzer,Tobias King,Till Riedel,Michael Beigl,Tobias Röddiger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含30个数据集、17种架构和4760次训练的大规模标准化基准，揭示WHAR领域预测性能已趋近天花板，当前SOTA呈分布式而非单一架构主导；在考虑部署效率时，紧凑模型和随机森林构成Pareto前沿，而大模型高硬件成本未带来显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WHAR领域存在可比性危机：研究使用不一致的数据集、自定义数据处理和差异化的评估协议，导致SOTA声明缺乏可靠性，亟需标准化基准来客观评估不同方法的真实表现。

Method: 集成30个多样化数据集进行标准化处理，统一模型接口和跨受试者评估协议，在Android参考设备上同时评测17种架构的预测性能（macro-F1）与部署效率（延迟、峰值内存、模型大小），共执行4760次训练运行。

Result: CNN-HAR取得最高平均macro-F1，但顶级模型性能紧密聚集，表明架构已接近预测性能上限；部署效率维度上，紧凑神经网络（TinierHAR）和经典随机森林构成Pareto前沿，大型循环/混合模型付出高硬件成本却未获得相应性能增益。

Conclusion: WHAR预测性能已趋于饱和，未来研究应聚焦于优化部署效率和改进域适应能力，而非继续追求边际预测性能提升。

Abstract: Deep learning has become the dominant paradigm in Wearable Human Activity Recognition (WHAR), yet progress is obscured by a comparability crisis. Results are often reported using inconsistent datasets, custom data processing, and varying evaluation protocols, making state-of-the-art claims fragile. We address this with a large-scale, open-source benchmark that integrates 30 diverse datasets under standardized processing, unified model interfaces, and a shared cross-subject evaluation protocol. Evaluating 17 representative architectures across 4760 training runs, we jointly measure predictive performance alongside on-device latency, peak memory, and model size on an Android reference device. Our results reveal that the WHAR state of the art is distributed rather than dominated by a single architecture. While CNN-HAR achieves the highest mean macro-F1, top-performing models cluster tightly, indicating contemporary architectures have converged near a predictive performance ceiling. When accounting for deployment efficiency, compact neural models, such as TinierHAR, and classical Random Forests define the practically relevant Pareto frontier, whereas larger recurrent and hybrid models incur high hardware costs without corresponding performance gains. Consequently, while predictive performance has plateaued, substantial potential for future progress remains in optimizing deployment efficiency and improving adaptation to domain shifts. We release our full framework to support transparent reuse and extension.

</details>


### [214] [VideoMDM: Towards 3D Human Motion Generation From 2D Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13364)
*Amir Mann,Gal Michael Harari,Merav Keidar,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VideoMDM是一个无需3D标注、仅从单目视频的2D姿态中学习3D人体运动先验的扩散框架。通过2D重投影损失（深度加权）近似等效3D监督，在HumanML3D上几乎追上完全3D监督的MDM（FID 0.88 vs 0.54），在真实视频数据集Fit3D和NBA上生成的运动更受人类偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D人体运动扩散模型依赖昂贵的3D地面真值标注，限制了数据规模和泛化能力。本文旨在利用大量易获取的单目视频（仅含2D姿态标注）来学习3D运动先验，降低对3D数据的依赖。
