For action generation, a Hybrid Whole-Body Action Decoder decomposes arm and base into distinct subspaces and lets each action modality attend to its task-relevant visual context through sparse cross-attention, while causal temporal modeling captures intra-timestep coordination and inter-timestep dependencies. Experiments on the ManiSkill-HAB simulation benchmark demonstrate that GeoHAT achieves a 79.3% mean success rate, surpassing the strongest baseline by 23.7%. Furthermore, real-world experiments on diverse tasks also confirm consistent improvements over all baselines.

</details>


### [398] [GIVE: Grounding Human Gestures in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13435)
*Pengfei Liu,Gen Li,Junqiao Fan,Boyu Ma,Jindou Jia,Yang Xiao,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GIVE是一种通过视觉和语义双路径增强预训练VLA模型的手势理解能力的方法，无需修改模型架构，在真实HRI实验中目标识别准确率提升40%，任务成功率提升80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将机器人操作视为纯文本驱动任务，忽略了手势在HRI中的重要作用，导致语言指令模糊时意图接地不准确、操作不可靠。

Method: GIVE通过两条互补路径引入手势信息：视觉路径（在手部叠加骨骼和指尖射线以显式物体接地）和语义路径（生成手势和任务指令的高层描述以实现鲁棒意图接地），联合利用视觉和语义引导来增强VLA策略。

Result: 真实HRI实验中，GIVE目标物体识别准确率提升40%，总体任务成功率提升80%，且对未见空间布局和不同参与者表现出强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: GIVE通过视觉-语义双路径增强策略有效弥补了VLA模型在手势理解方面的不足，为多模态人机交互中的意图接地和可靠操作提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Human communication is inherently multimodal, where language is often accompanied by non-verbal cues such as gestures to convey intentions. However, current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models treat robotic manipulation as a pure text-driven task, overlooking the important role of gestures in Human-Robot Interaction (HRI). This often leads to inaccurate intent grounding and unreliable manipulation when language instructions are ambiguous or underspecified. To address this challenge, we propose GIVE (Gesture Intent via Visual-Semantic Enhancement), an effective approach that enhances pre-trained VLA models with human gesture understanding without architectural modifications. Specifically, GIVE incorporates gesture information through two complementary pathways: a visual pathway that overlays hand skeletons and fingertip rays onto robot observations for explicit object grounding, and a semantic pathway that generates high-level descriptions of human gestures and task instructions for robust intent grounding. By jointly leveraging visual and semantic guidance, GIVE enables VLA policies to better associate gestures with manipulation behaviors and adapt to dynamic interaction intents. In real-world HRI experiments, GIVE substantially outperforms the baseline, improving target object recognition accuracy by 40% and overall task success rate by 80%, while demonstrating strong robustness and generalization to unseen spatial layouts and diverse participants.

</details>


### [399] [NavWAM: A Navigation World Action Model for Goal-Conditioned Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13494)
*Daichi Azuma,Taiki Miyanishi,Koya Sakamoto,Shuhei Kurita,Yaonan Zhu,Petr Khrapchenkov,Motoaki Kawanabe,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NavWAM，一种扩散变换器策略，通过学习联合预测未来观测、目标进展值和动作块，将导航世界模型预测直接转化为可执行动作，无需外部规划器，在图像目标导航任务中优于基于规划的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航世界模型虽然能提供视觉预测，但仅作为预测模块，需要外部规划器将预测的未来转化为闭环控制，导致预测与控制分离，效率低下。

Method: NavWAM采用扩散变换器策略，将未来观测、目标进展值和动作块编码到共享潜在序列中进行联合学习；通过仿真预训练和真实机器人适配实现；直接输出可执行动作，无需CEM式动作搜索。

Result: 在离线基准测试和真实机器人闭环部署中，NavWAM在图像目标导航任务上优于基于规划的世界模型基线和使用默认策略模式（无CEM动作搜索）的对比方法。

Conclusion: 通过联合预测与动作-价值学习，NavWAM使视觉预测直接可用于机器人控制，消除了预测与规划之间的鸿沟，为部分可观测下的目标条件视觉导航提供了一种更高效的端到端方案。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned visual navigation requires a robot to act under partial observability by anticipating how its motion will change the future egocentric view and whether that change brings it closer to the goal. Navigation world models provide such visual foresight, but they remain prediction modules that require an external planner to convert predicted futures into closed-loop control. We propose Navigation World Action Model (NavWAM), a diffusion-transformer policy that turns navigation world-model prediction into executable action by representing future observations, goal-progress values, and action chunks in a shared latent sequence. By learning future prediction jointly with the action and value targets that determine closed-loop behavior, NavWAM makes visual foresight directly usable for robot control. We build NavWAM through simulation pretraining and real-robot adaptation, and evaluate it on image-goal navigation against planning-based world models and a representative direct navigation policy. Across offline benchmarks and closed-loop real-robot deployment, NavWAM improves over planning-based world-model baselines in our evaluations while using the default policy mode without CEM-style action search. Project page: https://dachii-azm.github.io/navwam/

</details>


### [400] [SPARC: Reliable Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13497)
*Nils Blank,Paul Mattes,Maximilian Xiling Li,Jakub Suliga,Thomas Roth,Moritz Reuss,Pankhuri Vanjani,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARC是一个风险感知框架，利用机器人演示中的时空结构自动生成带有可靠性校准的结构化空间标注，在保留更多有用样本的同时显著减少噪声标签；在1.7k人工标注数据上，定位精度显著优于纯检测基线，高精度操作点保留样本量提升3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化标注管线虽可大规模生成结构化空间标注（如边界框、物体轨迹、操作阶段标签），但检测器置信度与标注正确性之间校准不良，迫使研究者在接受噪声标签或丢弃有用样本之间做出权衡，缺乏可靠的质量信号。

Method: SPARC利用机器人任务固有的时空结构生成可靠性信号，对每个标注赋予可靠性分数，实现噪声标签的过滤与有用样本的保留。同时引入IA-Bench基准测试，用于衡量模型在机器人演示中定位交互物体位置的准确性。

Result: 在涵盖多种实体与场景的1.7k人工标注演示上，SPARC相比纯检测基线在定位精度上显著提升，高精度操作点保留样本量达3倍。微调后的模型在物体定位和指向基准上达到同类规模模型SOTA，在更广泛的空间推理套件上保持竞争力，且在杂乱的真实场景中训练的机器人策略优于基线。

Conclusion: SPARC通过可靠性校准的结构化空间标注有效解决了自动化标注中的噪声问题，在无需人工验证或人工标注训练数据的情况下，显著提升了物体定位和机器人策略性能，展示了时空结构信息在标注质量信号生成中的价值。

Abstract: This work introduces Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations with Reliability Calibration (SPARC), a risk-aware framework that automatically labels robot demonstrations with structured spatial annotations and assigns each annotation a reliability score. Structured spatial annotations, such as bounding boxes, object trajectories, and manipulation phase labels, benefit a broad range of robotics applications from training grounded robot policies and embodied foundation models to motion planning and hierarchical task composition. Existing automated pipelines generate such annotations at scale but provide no reliable quality signal: detector confidence is poorly calibrated for annotation correctness, forcing a choice between accepting noisy labels or discarding useful samples. In contrast to existing automated pipelines, SPARC leverages the spatio-temporal structure inherent to robot tasks to generate a reliability signal, reducing noisy labels and retaining more useful samples. We further introduce Interaction-Aware Bench (IA-Bench), a benchmark that measures model accuracy in grounding the locations of interacted objects in robot demonstrations. On 1.7k human-annotated demonstrations spanning diverse embodiments and scenarios, SPARC significantly outperforms detection-only baselines in localization accuracy while retaining three times more samples at high-precision operating points. Our experiments demonstrate that models finetuned on our annotations achieve state-of-the-art results on object-grounding and pointing benchmarks among similarly sized models, while remaining competitive on broader spatial-reasoning suites without manually verified or annotated training data. Furthermore, policies trained on SPARC-generated annotations outperform baselines in cluttered, visually ambiguous real-world scenes. Code, data, and models are available at intuitive-robots.github.io/sparc-labeling.

</details>


### [401] [MCR-Bionic Hand: Anatomical Structural Priors for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13601)
*Haosen Yang,Guowu Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出结构智能概念，将人手灵巧性分为结构先验生成和肌肉介导调节两个层面，并基于此开发了MCR-Bionic仿生手，通过1:1肌肉骨骼仿生设计实现低维状态生成与精细接触后调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统灵巧手将灵巧性归因于高维主动控制系统（自由度、驱动、算法），但忽略了人手物理结构本身编码的灵巧性。本文旨在揭示并利用手部解剖结构（骨骼、韧带、肌腱、内在肌）所蕴含的"结构智能"，将其作为控制的一部分。

Method: 提出结构智能的双层框架：(1)结构先验生成——利用腕至指的腱固定效应、FDS/FDP走线、背侧伸肌帽将低维姿态输入转换为默认抓取构型和PIP-DIP协调；(2)肌肉介导调节——外在肌、蚓状肌、骨间肌调控MCP姿态、远端稳定性、指尖力路径和接触状态。基于此设计1:1肌肉骨骼仿生手MCR-Bionic，集成了两排八骨腕关节、跨腕肌腱、解剖屈肌走线、掌板/侧副韧带约束、背侧伸肌帽和内在肌通路。

Result: 功能演示和几何力学模型表明：腕姿态诱导多关节预塑形，伸肌帽将PIP姿态映射为耦合的DIP响应，内在肌通路调节远端稳定性和指尖动作方向。硬币旋转、笔传递、手背翻硬币、方块操作等接触密集型任务展示了MCR-Bionic实现低维状态生成与精细接触后调节的结合能力。

Conclusion: 解剖仿生学的价值不在于视觉相似性，而在于识别人类手部结构中承担部分控制功能的关键结构，为灵巧手设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic hands are usually formulated as high dimensional active control systems governed by degrees of freedom, actuation, and algorithms. Human hand dexterity, however, is partly encoded in the physical architecture of bones, ligaments, tendons, aponeuroses, and intrinsic muscles. This work describes that contribution as two linked forms of structural intelligence: structural prior generation, in which wrist to finger tenodesis, FDS/FDP routing, and the dorsal extensor hood transform low dimensional posture inputs into default grasp configurations and PIP to DIP coordination; and muscle mediated modulation, in which extrinsic muscles, lumbricals, and interossei regulate MCP posture, distal stability, fingertip force paths, and contact states around that default state.
  Based on this framework, MCR-Bionic Hand is developed as a 1:1 musculoskeletal biomimetic hand integrating a two row eight bone wrist, cross wrist tendons, anatomical flexor routing, volar plate and collateral ligament constraints, the dorsal extensor hood, and intrinsic muscle pathways within one body. Functional demonstrations and geometric mechanical models show that wrist posture induces multi joint pre shaping, the extensor hood maps PIP posture to a coupled DIP response, and intrinsic plus pathways modulate distal stability and fingertip action direction after grasp formation. Contact rich tasks, including coin rotation, pen transfer, dorsal coin flipping, and cube manipulation, show that MCR-Bionic links low dimensional state generation with fine post contact modulation. These results suggest that anatomical biomimetics is valuable not for visual similarity, but for identifying human hand structures that perform part of control.

</details>


### [402] [$\texttt{WEAVER}$, Better, Faster, Longer: An Effective World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13672)
*Arnav Kumar Jain,Yilin Wu,Jesse Farebrother,Gokul Swamy,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WEAVER，一种多视角世界模型架构，通过流匹配损失预测未来潜变量和奖励值，同时在保真度、一致性和效率三个维度上取得最优表现，在机器人操作任务上实现策略评估（ρ=0.870）、策略改进（成功率提升38%）和测试时规划（成功率提升14%，速度提升5-10倍）的显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在机器人领域潜力巨大（策略评估、改进、测试时规划），但现有方法难以同时满足保真度、长程一致性和效率三个关键需求，尤其在复杂长程动态操作任务上表现不佳。

Method: WEAVER是一种多视角世界模型架构，采用流匹配损失（flow-matching loss）来预测未来潜变量和奖励值。论文提炼了模型架构、记忆机制和预测目标三个维度的关键设计决策。

Result: 在机器人硬件上：策略评估与真实成功率的相关系数ρ=0.870；在π0.5基础模型上策略改进提升38%成功率；测试时规划提升14%成功率且速度较先前WM快5-10倍；在分布外场景上优于先前方法。

Conclusion: WEAVER证明了通过合理的设计选择，世界模型可以同时在保真度、一致性和效率上取得突破，有效解锁机器人长程动态操作任务中的多种下游能力。

Abstract: The potential impacts of world models (WMs, i.e., learned simulators) on robotics are far-reaching -- policy evaluation, policy improvement, and test-time planning -- all with limited real-world interaction. To unlock these downstream capabilities, a WM needs to jointly satisfy three desiderata: $\textit{(i)}$ fidelity (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that correlate with reality), $\textit{(ii)}$ consistency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that are coherent over long horizons), and $\textit{(iii)}$ efficiency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories quickly). We propose $\texttt{WEAVER}$ (World Estimation Across Views for Embodied Reasoning): a WM architecture that simultaneously achieves all three desiderata, providing state-of-the-art results on robotic manipulation tasks. $\texttt{WEAVER}$ is a multi-view WM trained to predict future latents and reward values via a flow-matching loss. We distill the key design decisions across model architecture, memory, and prediction objectives required to unlock the kinds of long-horizon dynamic manipulation tasks that have confounded prior world modeling approaches. We apply $\texttt{WEAVER}$ in robotic hardware, demonstrating its effectiveness at policy evaluation ($ρ$=0.870 correlation with real-world success rate), policy improvement (real-world success rate improvement of $38\%$ on top of the $π_{0.5}$ robot foundation model), and test-time planning (real-world success rate improvement of $14\%$ with a $5-10\times$ speedup over prior WMs). $\texttt{WEAVER}$ also demonstrates better performance than prior WMs when evaluated on out-of-distribution scenarios. Code, models, and videos at: https://arnavkj1995.github.io/WEAVER/ .

</details>


### [403] [Improving Robotic Generalist Policies via Flow Reversal Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13675)
*Andy Tang,William Chen,Andrew Wagenmaker,Chelsea Finn,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Flow Reversal Steering (FRS)方法，通过将次优动作逆向映射到流匹配通用策略的潜在噪声空间，再导向高质量动作模式，从而改进机器人操控任务的零样本控制、行为克隆蒸馏和强化学习引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略虽能从多样化数据中学习丰富行为先验，但当直接命令策略执行新任务时往往失败，需要一种方法从策略的先验知识中推断并调用合适的动作模式。

Method: FRS方法：针对流匹配通用策略，将次优但"合理"的动作通过逆向流策略得到其潜在噪声，再将其映射到邻近的通用策略高质量动作模式上。

Result: 在模拟和真实操控任务中，FRS成功将人类或VLM的粗粒度语义引导转化为优质机器人动作；通过行为克隆训练辅助策略输出噪声，在不到1分钟训练内实现高达95%的绝对任务成功率提升；使强化学习能在标准RL无法改进的任务上取得提升。

Conclusion: FRS为通用机器人策略提供了一种有效的方法来利用其行为先验，支持零样本控制提升、快速行为克隆蒸馏以及强化学习引导，显著扩展了通用策略在实际机器人操控中的应用能力。

Abstract: Generalist policies can learn a wide range of skills from diverse robot datasets. In order to solve or improve on challenging news tasks, we need a way to infer and invoke the appropriate actions from the policy's rich behavioral prior, especially when directly commanding the policy fails. We focus on flow matching generalists and propose Flow Reversal Steering (FRS): a method that takes suboptimal but ``reasonable'' actions, finds their latent noises by passing them through the flow policy in reverse, and maps them to nearby generalist action modes. We evaluate FRS across many simulated and real-world manipulation settings. First, FRS can turn coarse semantic guidance from humans or vision-language models (VLMs) into corresponding good robot actions, improving zero-shot control. These gains can be distilled with behavioral cloning by training an auxiliary policy to output noises that the generalist maps to good actions -- showing up to 95% absolute task success rate boosts in under a minute of training. Finally, FRS enables policy improvement by bootstrapping reinforcement learning with semantic knowledge, improving on several tasks that standard RL fails to improve on.

</details>


### [404] [Mana: Dexterous Manipulation of Articulated Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13677)
*Zhao-Heng Yin,Guanya Shi,Pieter Abbeel,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mana是一个受计算机动画启发的通用仿真到现实框架，通过粗到细的流水线将程序化生成的关键帧转换为操作轨迹，实现灵巧手对铰接工具的零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接工具操作由于需要协调内部自由度与接触丰富的交互，在灵巧机器人领域仍是一个重大挑战；现有工作多聚焦于刚体物体，对铰接工具的研究不足。

Method: 受计算机动画启发，采用粗到细流水线：首先通过程序化方式生成抓取关键帧（仅需鼠标点击指定功能可供性，<1分钟/工具），然后结合运动规划和强化学习生成操作轨迹。

Result: 在四种不同尺度和关节类型的铰接工具上，Mana实现了抓取和手内操作的零样本仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: Mana展示了灵巧铰接工具操作的可扩展方法，验证了将灵巧操作重新解释为动画问题的有效性。

Abstract: Articulated tool manipulation remains a major challenge in dexterous robotics due to the need to coordinate internal degrees of freedom and contact-rich interactions. While prior work has largely focused on rigid objects, articulated tool use remains underexplored because of its physical complexity and the difficulty of learning functional grasping and manipulation policies. We present Mana (Manipulation Animator), a general sim-to-real framework that reinterprets dexterous manipulation as an animation problem. Inspired by computer animation, Mana employs a coarse-to-fine pipeline that transforms procedurally-generated grasp keyframes into manipulation trajectories through motion planning and reinforcement learning. The data generation process is largely automatic, requiring only a few mouse clicks to specify functional affordances (<1 minute per tool). Across four articulated tools spanning different scales and joint types, Mana achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer for both grasping and in-hand manipulation, demonstrating a scalable approach to dexterous articulated tool use.

</details>


### [405] [Learning Robot Safety from Sparse Human Feedback using Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.04823)
*Aaron O. Feldman,Joseph A. Vincent,Maximilian Adang,JunEn Low,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于人类二元反馈和共形预测的机器人安全保证方法，通过识别潜在不安全状态区域（可能在潜在空间中），构建具有保证漏报率的预警系统，并用于策略改进。在四旋翼飞行器视觉运动策略上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户定义的约束可能遗漏边界情况、即使从安全数据训练的策略也可能变得不安全、安全性具有主观性，因此需要一种能从人类反馈中学习并给出统计保证的安全评估方法。

Method: 向人类展示策略轨迹并收集二元安全反馈，利用共形预测方法（基于最近邻分类）识别包含用户指定比例未来策略错误的状态区域（可在潜在空间中），该方法避免保留数据因此样本高效；通过警报系统实现保证漏报率的预警，并利用避开该区域进行策略改进。

Result: 从视频标注中检测到四旋翼视觉运动策略何时无法穿过指定门；在30次四旋翼飞行、6个导航任务的实验中，策略改进方法提升了模型预测控制器的安全性。

Conclusion: 结合人类反馈和共形预测可以在有限样本下提供有统计保证的机器人安全预警，并可用于下游策略改进以提升实际安全性。

Abstract: Ensuring robot safety can be challenging; user-defined constraints can miss edge cases, policies can become unsafe even when trained from safe data, and safety can be subjective. Thus, we learn about robot safety by showing policy trajectories to a human who flags unsafe behavior. From this binary feedback, we use the statistical method of conformal prediction to identify a region of states, potentially in learned latent space, guaranteed to contain a user-specified fraction of future policy errors. Our method is sample-efficient, as it builds on nearest neighbor classification and avoids withholding data as is common with conformal prediction. By alerting if the robot reaches the suspected unsafe region, we obtain a warning system that mimics the human's safety preferences with guaranteed miss rate. From video labeling, our system can detect when a quadcopter visuomotor policy will fail to steer through a designated gate. We present an approach for policy improvement by avoiding the suspected unsafe region. With it we improve a model predictive controller's safety, as shown in experimental testing with 30 quadcopter flights across 6 navigation tasks. Code and videos are provided.

</details>


### [406] [Data-Driven Soft Robot Control via Adiabatic Spectral Submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10919)
*Roshan S. Kaundinya,John Irvin Alora,Jonas G. Matt,Luis A. Pabon,Marco Pavone,George Haller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于绝热情谱子流形（aSSM）的数据驱动模型预测控制策略，用于解决软机器人因其机械复杂性而难以进行基于模型控制的问题。该策略利用重阻尼机器人内部振动衰减远快于路径运动速度的特性，提取低维吸引不变子流形来捕捉主导动力学。在多种任务（软体躯干机器人、Cosserat杆弹性软臂）中，aSSM降维模型（5-6维）的闭环跟踪性能比其它数据驱动方法提升高达10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软机器人的机械复杂性导致其基于模型的控制极为困难，传统线性数据驱动模型无法有效控制软机器人在复杂空间路径上表现出显著非线性行为的轨迹。现有方法难以平衡模型精度与计算效率之间的矛盾。

Method: 基于绝热情谱子流形（aSSM）理论，利用重阻尼机器人内部振动衰减远快于路径运动速度的特点，从路径上提取低维吸引不变子流形作为降维模型；结合模型预测控制（MPC）框架，构建纯数据驱动的控制方案。

Result: 在软体躯干机器人的高保真有限元模型和Cosserat杆弹性软臂模型上验证，5-6维aSSM降维模型在所有闭环控制任务中的跟踪性能优于其他数据驱动方法最高达10倍，且对实验噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: aSSM-based数据驱动模型预测控制策略能有效处理软机器人的非线性动力学，显著提升复杂路径跟踪控制性能，为软机器人模型预测控制提供了一种高精度、低维度的有效方案。

Abstract: The mechanical complexity of soft robots creates significant challenges for their model-based control. Specifically, linear data-driven models have struggled to control soft robots on complex, spatially extended paths that explore regions with significant nonlinear behavior. To account for these nonlinearities, we develop here a model-predictive control strategy based on the recent theory of adiabatic spectral submanifolds (aSSMs). This theory is applicable because the internal vibrations of heavily overdamped robots decay at a speed that is much faster than the desired speed of the robot along its intended path. In that case, low-dimensional attracting invariant manifolds (aSSMs) emanate from the path and carry the dominant dynamics of the robot. Aided by this recent theory, we devise an aSSM-based model-predictive control scheme purely from data. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our data-driven model in tracking dynamic trajectories across diverse tasks. We validate on high-fidelity, high-dimensional finite-element models of a soft trunk robot and Cosserat-rod-based elastic soft arms, with additional experiments confirming robust performance even in the presence of experimental noise. Notably, we find that five- or six-dimensional aSSM-reduced models outperform the tracking performance of other data-driven modeling methods by a factor up to 10 across all closed-loop control tasks.

</details>


### [407] [Adaptive Model-Predictive Control of a Soft Continuum Robot Using a Physics-Informed Neural Network Based on Cosserat Rod Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.12681)
*Johann Licher,Max Bartholdt,Henrik Krauss,Tim-Lukas Habich,Thomas Seel,Moritz Schappler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于域解耦物理信息神经网络（DD-PINN）的实时非线性模型预测控制（MPC）框架，用于软连续体机器人（SCR）的动态控制。DD-PINN作为Cosserat杆动力学替代模型，速度提升达44,000倍，结合无迹卡尔曼滤波估计状态和弯曲柔顺性，在GPU上以70Hz运行，仿真和实验中实现了高精度轨迹跟踪（末端误差<3mm，加速度达3.55 m/s²）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软连续体机器人的动态控制因其精确动力学模型计算量巨大而极具挑战。现有数据驱动方法（如Koopman算子方法）缺乏适应性且无法重构完整机器人形状，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出域解耦物理信息神经网络（DD-PINN）作为Cosserat杆动力学模型的替代，实现44,000倍加速；结合无迹卡尔曼滤波（UKF）从末端位置测量估计模型状态和弯曲柔顺性；在GPU上实现运行频率70Hz的非线性进化MPC。

Result: 仿真中动态轨迹跟踪和设定点控制的末端位置误差低于3mm（执行器长度的2.3%）；真实实验中达到相似精度，加速度高达3.55 m/s²。

Conclusion: 提出的DD-PINN+MPC框架实现了软连续体机器人的实时、高精度动态控制，兼具模型速度提升、状态估计和适应性，大幅拓展了SCR的实用潜力。

Abstract: Dynamic control of soft continuum robots (SCRs) holds great potential for expanding their applications, but remains a challenging problem due to the high computational demands of accurate dynamic models. While data-driven approaches like Koopman-operator-based methods have been proposed, they typically lack adaptability and cannot reconstruct the full robot shape, limiting their applicability. This work introduces a real-time-capable nonlinear model-predictive control (MPC) framework for SCRs based on a domain-decoupled physics-informed neural network (DD-PINN) with adaptable bending stiffness. The DD-PINN serves as a surrogate for the dynamic Cosserat rod model with a speed-up factor of up to 44,000. It is also used within an unscented Kalman filter for estimating the model states and bending compliance from end-effector position measurements. We implement a nonlinear evolutionary MPC running at 70 Hz on the GPU. In simulation, it demonstrates accurate tracking of dynamic trajectories and setpoint control with end-effector position errors below 3 mm (2.3\% of the actuator's length). In real-world experiments, the controller achieves similar accuracy and accelerations up to 3.55 m/s2.

</details>


### [408] [Active Semantic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05430)
*Huayi Tang,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于主动语义感知的方法，利用紧凑的多层场景图和大语言模型来采样未观测区域的新场景图，并通过计算信息增益实现空间推理，在仿真和真实机器人上验证了其高效性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在复杂室内环境中难以高效地利用语义信息进行探索和空间推理，尤其是在部分观测条件下对未观测区域的理解和推理能力不足。

Method: 构建紧凑的多层场景图表示复杂室内环境（房间、物体、墙壁、窗户等）；利用LLM从未观测区域采样与部分观测一致的 plausible 场景图；通过计算潜在航点的信息增益实现高级空间推理（如预测哪扇门通往厨房或卧室）。

Result: 在3D室内仿真环境和真实Unitree Go 2机器人上的定性和定量分析表明，该方法比现有方法更快、更准确地定位环境中的高层和低层语义信息。

Conclusion: 提出的主动语义感知方法通过多层场景图与LLM的结合，显著提升了部分观测条件下复杂室内环境的语义理解与探索效率。

Abstract: We develop an approach for active semantic perception, which refers to using the semantics of the scene for tasks such as exploration. We build a compact, multi-layer scene graph that can represent large, complex indoor environments at various levels of abstraction, e.g., nodes corresponding to rooms, objects, walls, windows etc., as well as fine-grained details of their geometry. We develop a procedure based on large language models (LLMs) to sample new plausible scene graphs of unobserved regions that are consistent with partial observations of the scene. We develop a procedure to compute the information gain of a potential waypoint upon this scene graph to enable sophisticated spatial reasoning: for example, of the two doors that lead out of the living room, one probably leads to the kitchen and the other to the bedroom. We evaluate our approach in realistic 3D indoor apartments in simulation and also on a Unitree Go 2 robot in the real world. Qualitative and quantitative analysis shows that our approach can pin down high-level and low-level semantic information in the environment quickly and more accurately than existing approaches.

</details>


### [409] [Miniature Testbed for Validating Multi-Agent Cooperative Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11022)
*Hyunchul Bae,Eunjae Lee,Jehyeop Han,Minhee Kang,Jaehyeon Kim,Junggeun Seo,Minkyun Noh,Heejin Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为CIVAT的1:15比例微型测试平台，用于验证协同自动驾驶。该平台集成了智能路侧基础设施（配备感知、边缘计算和通信能力）、自主车辆和V2V/V2I通信（基于Wi-Fi和ROS2的发布-订阅模式），并通过基础设施感知和交叉口管理实验验证了系统功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试平台均未采用具备感知、边缘计算和通信能力的智能基础设施，无法有效支持协同自动驾驶的验证，因此需要构建一个能填补这一空白的测试环境。

Method: 设计并实现了一个1:15比例的小型测试平台CIVAT，包含缩比城市地图、搭载车载传感器的自主车辆和智能基础设施；通过共享Wi-Fi和ROS2框架集成V2V和V2I通信，采用发布-订阅模式实现车辆与基础设施之间的信息交换。

Result: 通过基于基础设施的感知实验和交叉口管理实验，验证了CIVAT系统在协同自动驾驶功能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: CIVAT测试平台成功填补了现有测试平台缺乏智能基础设施支持的空白，为协同自动驾驶的研究和验证提供了有效的实验工具。

Abstract: Cooperative autonomous driving, which extends vehicle autonomy by enabling real-time collaboration between vehicles and smart roadside infrastructure, remains a challenging yet essential problem. However, none of the existing testbeds employ smart infrastructure equipped with sensing, edge computing, and communication capabilities. To address this gap, we design and implement a 1:15-scale miniature testbed, CIVAT, for validating cooperative autonomous driving, consisting of a scaled urban map, autonomous vehicles with onboard sensors, and smart infrastructure. The proposed testbed integrates V2V and V2I communication with the publish-subscribe pattern through a shared Wi-Fi and ROS2 framework, enabling information exchange between vehicles and infrastructure to realize cooperative driving functionality. As a case study, we validate the system through infrastructure-based perception and intersection management experiments.

</details>


### [410] [Learning Visually Interpretable Oscillator Networks for Soft Continuum Robots from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18322)
*Henrik Krauss,Johann Licher,Naoya Takeishi,Annika Raatz,Takehisa Yairi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出注意力广播解码器(ABCD)和视觉振荡器网络(VONs)，用于从视频中学习软体连续机器人动力学，实现可解释的潜在动力学建模，预测误差降低5.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从视频学习软体连续机器人动力学的方法缺乏可解释性或依赖先验假设，基于模型的方法需要先验知识和人工设计。

Method: (1) 注意力广播解码器(ABCD)：即插即用模块，生成像素级注意力图定位每个潜在维度的贡献并过滤静态背景；(2) 视觉振荡器网络(VONs)：2D潜在振荡器网络，耦合ABCD注意力图实现力学量的可视化。

Result: 在两段机器人上，ABCD模型将Koopman算子多步预测误差降低5.8倍，振荡器网络降低3.5倍；VONs自主发现振荡器的链式结构。

Conclusion: 纯数据驱动方法获得紧凑、力学可解释的模型，对未来控制应用具有潜力。

Abstract: Learning soft continuum robot (SCR) dynamics from video offers flexibility but existing methods lack interpretability or rely on prior assumptions. Model-based approaches require prior knowledge and manual design. We bridge this gap by introducing: (1) The Attention Broadcast Decoder (ABCD), a plug-and-play module for autoencoder-based latent dynamics learning that generates pixel-accurate attention maps localizing each latent dimension's contribution while filtering static backgrounds, enabling visual interpretability via spatially grounded latents and on-image overlays. (2) Visual Oscillator Networks (VONs), a 2D latent oscillator network coupled to ABCD attention maps for on-image visualization of learned masses, coupling stiffness, and forces, thereby enabling mechanical interpretability. We validate our approach on single- and double-segment SCRs, demonstrating that ABCD-based models significantly improve multi-step prediction accuracy with 5.8x error reduction for Koopman operators and 3.5x for oscillator networks on a two-segment robot. VONs autonomously discover a chain structure of oscillators. This fully data-driven approach yields compact, mechanically interpretable models with potential relevance for future control applications.

</details>


### [411] [DiskChunGS: Large-Scale 3D Gaussian SLAM Through Chunk-Based Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.23030)
*Casimir Feldmann,Maximum Wilder-Smith,Vaishakh Patil,Michael Oechsle,Michael Niemeyer,Keisuke Tateno,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiskChunGS提出了一种基于磁盘分块的3DGS SLAM系统，通过将场景划分为空间块并仅在GPU内存中维护活动区域，解决了大规模场景重建中的内存瓶颈问题，在多个数据集上实现了无内存故障的高质量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS SLAM系统受限于GPU内存容量，只能重建小规模场景，缺乏处理大规模环境的能力。

Method: 提出out-of-core方法，将场景划分为空间块（spatial chunks），仅将活动区域保留在GPU内存中，非活动区域存储于磁盘，并与现有SLAM框架（位姿估计、回环检测）无缝集成。

Result: 在Replica、TUM-RGBD、KITTI（完整11个序列）及Nvidia Jetson等资源受限平台上均成功运行，无内存故障，视觉质量优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 算法创新（如磁盘分块策略）能够有效克服3DGS SLAM的内存约束，实现大规模场景的全局一致重建。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have demonstrated impressive results for novel view synthesis with real-time rendering capabilities. However, integrating 3DGS with SLAM systems faces a fundamental scalability limitation: methods are constrained by GPU memory capacity, restricting reconstruction to small-scale environments. We present DiskChunGS, a scalable 3DGS SLAM system that overcomes this bottleneck through an out-of-core approach that partitions scenes into spatial chunks and maintains only active regions in GPU memory while storing inactive areas on disk. Our architecture integrates seamlessly with existing SLAM frameworks for pose estimation and loop closure, enabling globally consistent reconstruction at scale. We validate DiskChunGS on indoor scenes (Replica, TUM-RGBD), urban driving scenarios (KITTI), and resource-constrained Nvidia Jetson platforms. Our method uniquely completes all 11 KITTI sequences without memory failures while achieving superior visual quality, demonstrating that algorithmic innovation can overcome the memory constraints that have limited previous 3DGS SLAM methods.

</details>


### [412] [ReactEMG Stroke: Healthy-to-Stroke Few-shot Adaptation for sEMG-Based Intent Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22090)
*Runsheng Wang,Katelyn Lee,Xinyue Zhu,Lauren Winterbottom,Dawn M. Nilsen,Joel Stein,Matei Ciocarlie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种健康到中风迁移适应流程，利用大规模健康人群sEMG预训练模型，仅需少量中风患者数据微调即可实现鲁棒的意图检测。在三人慢性中风数据集上评估多种适应策略，最佳方法将过渡精度从0.42提升至0.61，原始精度从0.69提升至0.78。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面肌电信号（sEMG）用于中风后手部康复的按需辅助控制时，从患侧肌肉检测运动意图通常需要冗长的受试者特定校准，且对信号变异性鲁棒性差。为降低校准负担并提升鲁棒性，作者探索利用健康人群预训练模型迁移至中风场景。

Method: 构建健康→中风迁移适应流程：先在大型健康人群sEMG数据集上预训练意图检测模型，然后针对每位中风参与者仅使用少量受试者特定数据进行微调。比较三种适应策略：仅头部调优、参数高效LoRA适配器、全端到端微调。在包含会话内漂移、姿势变化、臂带重定位等分布偏移的测试集上评估。

Result: 健康预训练迁移适应在所有条件下均一致优于零样本迁移和仅中风数据训练（相同数据预算下）。最佳适应策略将平均过渡精度从0.42提升至0.61，原始精度从0.69提升至0.78。

Conclusion: 迁移健康域sEMG表征可降低校准负担并提升鲁棒性，适用于实时中风后意图检测。项目网站、视频、代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) is a promising control signal for assist-as-needed hand rehabilitation after stroke, but detecting intent from paretic muscles often requires lengthy, subject-specific calibration and remains brittle to variability. We propose a healthy-to-stroke adaptation pipeline that initializes an intent detector from a model pretrained on large-scale able-bodied sEMG, then fine-tunes it for each stroke participant using only a small amount of subject-specific data. Using a newly collected dataset from three individuals with chronic stroke, we compare adaptation strategies (head-only tuning, parameter-efficient LoRA adapters, and full end-to-end fine-tuning) and evaluate on held-out test sets that include realistic distribution shifts such as within-session drift, posture changes, and armband repositioning. Across conditions, healthy-pretrained adaptation consistently improves stroke intent detection relative to both zero-shot transfer and stroke-only training under the same data budget; the best adaptation methods improve average transition accuracy from 0.42 to 0.61 and raw accuracy from 0.69 to 0.78. These results suggest that transferring a reusable healthy-domain EMG representation can reduce calibration burden while improving robustness for real-time post-stroke intent detection. Our project website, video, code, and dataset are available at: https://roamlab.github.io/reactemg-stroke/.

</details>


### [413] [Extending the Law of Intersegmental Coordination: Implications for Powered Prosthetic Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02181)
*Elad Siman Tov,Nili E. Krausz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分析下肢截肢者步态中节段间协调性（ISC）的方法，并扩展至力矩协调性（ESM），开发了基于ISC的假肢控制框架及开源工具箱ISC3d。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力假肢虽能提供正功，但降低截肢者步行代谢能耗仍是难题。ISC已被观察到与步态能耗相关，却鲜少应用于下肢截肢者步态分析。

Method: 开发了三维运动学ISC分析方法；将其扩展至力矩协调性（Elevation Space Moments, ESM）；提出ISC驱动的动力假肢控制框架，以健康协调性为约束预测小腿角度/力矩；开发ISC3d工具箱。

Result: 健康步态中ESM存在平面协调性；截肢者使用动力/被动假肢时，抬升角仍保持平面性，但ESM缺乏平面协调性。

Conclusion: ISC方法可揭示截肢者步态协调缺陷，基于ISC的控制框架有望补偿被动足部带来的步态改变，ISC3d工具箱为研究步态协调和神经控制提供了工具。

Abstract: Powered prostheses are capable of providing net positive work to amputees and have advanced in the past two decades. However, reducing amputee metabolic cost of walking remains an open problem. The Law of Intersegmental Coordination (ISC) has been observed across gaits and previously implicated in energy expenditure of walking, yet it has rarely been analyzed or applied within the context of lower-limb amputee gait. This law states that the elevation angles of the thigh, shank and foot over the gait cycle covary. In this work, we developed a method to analyze intersegmental coordination for lower-limb 3D kinematic data, to simplify ISC analysis. Moreover, inspired by motor control, biomechanics and robotics literature, we used our method to extend ISC to a new law of coordination of moments. We find these Elevation Space Moments (ESM), and present results showing a moment-based coordination for able bodied gait. We also analyzed ISC for amputee gait with powered and passive prostheses, and found that while elevation angles remained planar, the ESM lacked planar coordination. We present an ISC-driven powered prosthetic control framework, using healthy coordination as a constraint to predict the shank angles/moments to compensate for alterations due to a passive foot. We developed the ISC3d toolbox that is freely available online, which may be used to compute kinematic and kinetic ISC in 3D. This provides a means to further study the role of coordination in gait and may help address fundamental questions of the neural control of human movement.

</details>


### [414] [SCALE: Self-uncertainty Conditioned Adaptive Looking and Execution for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04208)
*Hyeonbeom Choi,Daechul Ahn,Youhan Lee,Taewook Kang,Seongwon Cho,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCALE是一种无需额外训练、验证器或多轮前向传播的轻量级推理策略，通过自不确定性联合调节视觉感知和动作输出，在单次前向传播中实现自适应探索-利用平衡，提升了VLA模型的鲁棒性和表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的测试时缩放方法需要额外训练、验证器和多次前向传播，部署成本高；且仅干预动作解码而保持视觉表征固定，无法应对感知模糊场景。需要一种轻量级、单次前向传播且能同时调节感知与动作的推理策略。

Method: 提出SCALE推理策略，基于Active Inference理论中的不确定性驱动探索思想，利用模型自身的"自不确定性"同时调节视觉感知和动作生成：高不确定性时扩大感知与动作的探索范围，低不确定性时聚焦于利用，无需额外训练、验证器，仅需单次前向传播。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界基准测试中，SCALE提升了最先进VLA模型的性能，并优于现有TTS方法，同时保持了单次前向传播的效率。

Conclusion: SCALE为VLA模型提供了一种实用且高效的测试时自适应推理方案，通过不确定性驱动的联合感知-动作调节，在不增加计算负担的前提下提升了鲁棒性和任务完成能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose robotic control, with test-time scaling (TTS) gaining attention to enhance robustness beyond training. However, existing TTS methods for VLAs require additional training, verifiers, and multiple forward passes, making them impractical for deployment. Moreover, they intervene only at action decoding while keeping visual representations fixed-insufficient under perceptual ambiguity, where reconsidering how to perceive is as important as deciding what to do. To address these limitations, we propose SCALE, a simple inference strategy that jointly modulates visual perception and action based on 'self-uncertainty', inspired by uncertainty-driven exploration in Active Inference theory-requiring no additional training, no verifier, and only a single forward pass. SCALE broadens exploration in both perception and action under high uncertainty, while focusing on exploitation when confident-enabling adaptive execution across varying conditions. Experiments on simulated and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that SCALE improves state-of-the-art VLAs and outperforms existing TTS methods while maintaining single-pass efficiency.

</details>


### [415] [Adaptive-Horizon Conflict-Based Search for Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12024)
*Jiarui Li,Federico Pecora,Runyu Zhang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ACCBS算法，结合有限时域CBS和MPC的迭代加深机制，通过动态调整规划时域并重用约束树，在计算预算内快速生成高质量可行解，兼具渐近最优性和随时性能，弥合了理论最优性与实际鲁棒性之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPF方法存在两难：开环规划器生成固定轨迹，难以应对干扰；闭环启发式方法缺乏可靠性能保证，限制了在安全关键场景下的部署。需要一种兼具理论最优性与实际鲁棒性的方法。

Method: ACCBS算法，基于有限时域CBS（Conflict-Based Search）变体，引入受MPC中迭代加深启发的时域变化机制。动态根据可用计算预算调整规划时域，并复用单个约束树实现不同时域间的无缝切换。

Result: ACCBS能在计算预算内快速生成高质量可行解，随预算增加渐近最优，展现随时行为（anytime behavior）。广泛案例研究表明其兼具应对干扰的灵活性和强性能保证。

Conclusion: ACCBS有效桥接了理论最优性与实践鲁棒性之间的差距，适用于大规模机器人安全关键部署场景。

Abstract: MAPF is a core coordination problem for large robot fleets in automated warehouses and logistics. Existing approaches are typically either open-loop planners, which generate fixed trajectories and struggle to handle disturbances, or closed-loop heuristics without reliable performance guarantees, limiting their use in safety-critical deployments. This paper presents ACCBS, a closed-loop algorithm built on a finite-horizon variant of CBS with a horizon-changing mechanism inspired by iterative deepening in MPC. ACCBS dynamically adjusts the planning horizon based on the available computational budget, and reuses a single constraint tree to enable seamless transitions between horizons. As a result, it produces high-quality feasible solutions quickly while being asymptotically optimal as the budget increases, exhibiting anytime behavior. Extensive case studies demonstrate that ACCBS combines flexibility to disturbances with strong performance guarantees, effectively bridging the gap between theoretical optimality and practical robustness for large-scale robot deployment.

</details>


### [416] [Lyapunov-Based PI-Like Control for Robust Trajectory Tracking of a Four-Wheel Independently Driven and Steered Robot: Design and Experimental Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15424)
*Branimir Ćaran,Vladimir Milić,Marko Švaco,Bojan Jerbić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于李雅普诺夫方法的PI类控制器，用于四轮独立驱动与转向移动机器人的鲁棒轨迹跟踪，通过增广李雅普诺夫稳定性分析为速度-误差和积分-误差联合动力学提供了实用稳定性保证，并在实验平台上验证了其优于传统PI和滑模控制器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对四轮独立驱动与独立转向移动机器人（4WIS-4WID）的轨迹跟踪问题，现有方法在建模不精确、未建模动态和构型相关残余动力学影响下鲁棒性不足，需要一种兼具严格稳定性保证和实际可部署性的控制方案。

Method: 采用显式结构验证的数学模型；基于李雅普诺夫方法设计PI类控制器；对速度误差和积分误差的联合动力学进行增广李雅普诺夫实用稳定性分析，推导出实用稳定性和一致最终有界的显式界值与充分条件；控制律保留PI结构并加入基于模型的前馈补偿。

Result: 在四轮独立驱动与独立转向移动机器人平台上进行了水平与垂直工况下的实验验证，并与PI控制器和滑模控制器进行了基准对比，验证了所提设计的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于李雅普诺夫方法的PI类控制器在保证严格稳定性的同时，兼具PI控制的简洁性，适用于嵌入式实时实现，并在鲁棒性方面优于传统PI和滑模控制方法。

Abstract: In this paper, a Lyapunov-based synthesis of a PI-like controller is proposed for robust trajectory tracking of an independently driven and steered four-wheel mobile robot. For the robot considered in this work, an explicit structurally verified mathematical model is used to enable systematic controller design with rigorous stability guarantees suitable for real time implementation. An augmented Lyapunov-based practical stability analysis is developed for the combined velocity-error and integral-error dynamics of the inner loop, yielding explicit bounds and sufficient conditions for practical stability and uniform ultimate boundedness of the combined velocity-error and integral-error state. The resulting control law retains a PI-like structure with model-based feedforward compensation, making it suitable for implementation on standard embedded platforms while improving robustness against configuration dependent residual dynamics and unmodelled effects. The effectiveness and robustness of the proposed design are demonstrated experimentally on a four-wheel independently steered and independently driven mobile robot platform, under both horizontal and vertical operating conditions and benchmarked against a PI controller and a sliding-mode controller.

</details>


### [417] [EgoMoD: Predicting Global Maps of Dynamics from Local Egocentric Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00167)
*Iacopo Catalano,David Morilla-Cabello,Jorge Pena-Queralta,Eduardo Montijano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoMoD通过仅利用机器人运行过程中采集的短时自我中心视频片段，学习预测全局环境下的运动动力学图（MoDs），替代传统方法所需的长期全局观测，实现零样本迁移到真实系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoDs构建需要长时间全局环境观测，限制了其在动态场景中的实用性和部署效率。现有方法无法从机器人自身的局部感知中推断全局运动模式，导致规划缺乏前瞻性。

Method: 提出视频与位姿条件化的架构，以外部观测计算得到的MoDs作为特权监督信号，训练模型从局部观测中推断全局运动倾向，实现从局部动态线索到全局运动结构的预测。

Result: 在大型仿真环境中验证了EgoMoD在有限观测条件下对未来MoDs的预测能力，并在真实图像上展示了零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: EgoMoD使得机器人仅凭机载传感器即可获得全局运动动态先验，摆脱对外部全局感知基础设施的依赖，为动态环境下的前瞻性规划提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient navigation in dynamic environments requires anticipating how motion patterns evolve beyond the robot's immediate perceptual range, enabling preemptive rather than purely reactive planning in crowded scenes. Maps of Dynamics (MoDs) offer a structured representation of motion tendencies in space useful for long-term global planning, but constructing them traditionally requires global environment observations over extended periods of time. We introduce EgoMoD, the first approach that learns to predict future MoDs directly from short egocentric video clips collected during robot operation. Our method learns to infer environment-wide motion tendencies from local dynamic cues using a video- and pose-conditioned architecture trained with MoDs computed from external observations as privileged supervision, allowing local observations to serve as predictive signals of global motion structure. Thanks to this, we offer the capacity to forecast future motion dynamics over the whole environment rather than merely extend past patterns in the robot's field of view. As a site-specific dynamic prior, EgoMoD replaces the external global sensing infrastructure required by prior MoD methods at inference time with standard onboard sensors. Experiments in large simulated environments show that EgoMoD predicts future MoDs under limited observability, while evaluation with real images showcases its zero-shot transferability to real systems.

</details>


### [418] [PROBE: Probabilistic Occupancy BEV Encoding with Analytical Translation Robustness for 3D Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05965)
*Jinseop Lee,Byoungho Lee,Gichul Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PROBE是一种无学习的LiDAR地点识别描述子，将BEV网格的占据概率建模为伯努利随机变量，通过极坐标雅可比解析边缘化连续平移不确定性，在O(R·S)时间内实现距离自适应角不确定性σ_θ=σ_t/r。主要参数σ_t是传感器无关的物理量，提升了跨传感器泛化能力。在四个数据集上，PROBE在手工地标描述子中达到最高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR地点识别方法依赖离散点云扰动或监督学习，缺乏对连续平移不确定性的解析建模，且跨传感器泛化能力有限，需要大量逐数据集调参。

Method: 将BEV每个单元的占据状态建模为伯努利随机变量；通过极坐标雅可比解析边缘化连续笛卡尔平移，得到距离自适应角不确定性σ_θ=σ_t/r；使用伯努利KL Jaccard结合指数不确定性门控计算相似度，并引入FFT高度余弦相似度进行旋转对齐。

Result: 在四个不同LiDAR类型的数据集上，PROBE在多会话评估中达到手工地标描述子最高精度，在单会话评估中与手工地标和监督方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: PROBE通过概率建模和解析边缘化，实现了高效、高精度且具有跨传感器泛化能力的无学习LiDAR地点识别。

Abstract: We present PROBE (PRobabilistic Occupancy BEV Encoding), a learning-free LiDAR place recognition descriptor that models each BEV cell's occupancy as a Bernoulli random variable. Rather than relying on discrete point-cloud perturbations, PROBE analytically marginalizes over continuous Cartesian translations via the polar Jacobian, yielding a distance-adaptive angular uncertainty $σ_θ= σ_t / r$ in $\mathcal{O}(R{\cdot}S)$ time. The primary parameter $σ_t$ represents the expected translational uncertainty in meters, a sensor-independent physical quantity that enhances cross-sensor generalization while reducing the need for extensive per-dataset tuning. Pairwise similarity combines a Bernoulli-KL Jaccard with exponential uncertainty gating and FFT-based height cosine similarity for rotation alignment. Evaluated on four datasets spanning four diverse LiDAR types, PROBE achieves the highest accuracy among handcrafted descriptors in multi-session evaluation and competitive single-session performance relative to both handcrafted and supervised baselines. The source code and supplementary materials are available at https://sites.google.com/view/probe-pr.

</details>


### [419] [Safety Case Patterns for VLA-based driving systems: Insights from SimLingo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16013)
*Gerhard Yu,Fuyuki Ishikawa,Oluwafemi Odu,Alvine Boaye Belle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对基于VLA的自动驾驶系统可能因自然语言指令集成而产生新危险行为的问题，提出了一种名为RAISE的安全案例设计方法，包括针对指令驱动驾驶系统的模式、扩展的危险分析与风险评估（HARA）以及安全案例设计技术，并通过SimLingo案例研究验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA驾驶系统虽能结合场景理解、语言解释和动作生成实现灵活自适应驾驶，但开放自然语言输入可能带来不可预测的不安全行为，威胁乘客和行人安全，亟需建立系统化的安全保证方法以建立信任。

Method: 提出了RAISE方法：(1) 针对基于指令的驾驶系统（如VLA系统）的新型安全案例模式；(2) 扩展的HARA（危险分析与风险评估），细化安全场景及其结果；(3) 用于构建VLA驾驶系统安全案例的设计技术。

Result: 通过SimLingo案例研究，展示了如何利用RAISE方法为这类新兴自动驾驶系统构建严谨的、基于证据的安全声明。

Conclusion: RAISE方法能够有效支持VLA自动驾驶系统的安全保证，为构建可信的、对社会负责任的自动驾驶系统提供系统性安全案例设计框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA)-based driving systems represent a significant paradigm shift in autonomous driving since, by combining traffic scene understanding, linguistic interpretation, and action generation, these systems enable more flexible, adaptive, and instruction-responsive driving behaviors. However, despite their growing adoption and potential to support socially responsible autonomous driving as well as understanding high-level human instructions, VLA-based driving systems may exhibit new types of hazardous behaviors. For instance, the integration of open-ended natural language inputs (e.g., user or navigation instructions) into the multimodal control loop may lead to unpredictable and unsafe behaviors that could endanger vehicle occupants and pedestrians. Hence, assuring the safety of these systems is crucial to help build trust in their operations. To support this, we propose a novel safety case design approach called RAISE. Our approach introduces novel patterns tailored to instruction-based driving systems such as VLA-based driving systems, an extension of Hazard Analysis and Risk Assessment (HARA) detailing safe scenarios and their outcomes, and a design technique to create the safety cases of VLA-based driving systems. A case study on SimLingo illustrates how our approach can be used to construct rigorous, evidence-based safety claims for this emerging class of autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [420] [AssemLM: A Spatial Reasoning Multimodal Large Language Model for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08983)
*Zhi Jing,Jinbin Qiao,Ouyang Lu,Jicong Ao,Shuang Qiu,Huazhe Xu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AssemLM，一种面向机器人装配的空间多模态大语言模型，通过集成装配手册、点云和文本指令来预测6D装配姿态；同时构建了包含超90万样本的AssemBench基准数据集，在精细装配任务上实现了最先进的6D空间推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的方法依赖粗糙的2D感知，难以对复杂3D几何进行精确推理，无法满足机器人装配等精细操作任务的需求。

Method: AssemLM设计了专用点云编码器，从原始3D点云中提取细粒度几何和旋转特征，融合装配手册、点云和文本指令等多模态信息，预测任务关键6D装配姿态，实现3D空间推理与高层语言推理的桥接。

Result: 在AssemBench基准和真实机器人评估中，AssemLM在6D姿态推理任务上达到最先进水平，有效支持真实环境下的精细多步装配任务。

Conclusion: 融合3D几何感知的多模态大语言模型是提升机器人精细操作空间推理能力的有效路径，AssemBench为3D空间推理提供了大规模标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability for embodied intelligence, especially for fine-grained manipulation tasks such as robotic assembly. Recent methods based on vision-language models (VLMs) largely rely on coarse 2D perception and struggle to perform accurate reasoning over complex 3D geometry. To address this limitation, we propose AssemLM, a spatial multimodal large language model for robotic assembly that integrates assembly manuals, point clouds, and textual instructions to predict task-critical 6D assembly poses with explicit geometric understanding. To bridge raw 3D perception and high-level linguistic reasoning, AssemLM employs a specialized point cloud encoder to extract fine-grained geometric and rotational features for accurate 3D spatial reasoning in assembly tasks. In addition, we introduce AssemBench, a large-scale benchmark for assembly-oriented spatial reasoning with over 900K multimodal samples and precise 6D pose annotations, extending evaluation from 2D grounding to full 3D geometric inference. Extensive experiments and real-robot evaluations demonstrate that AssemLM achieves state-of-the-art 6D pose reasoning performance and effectively supports fine-grained, multi-step assembly tasks in real-world settings. Code, models, and the AssemBench dataset will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [421] [Lexicographic Minimum-Violation Motion Planning using Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20428)
*Patrick Halder,Lothar Kiltz,Hannes Homburger,Johannes Reuter,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将字典序STL最小违规运动规划转化为单目标标量优化问题的方法，通过非均匀量化和位移技术扩展了MPPI求解器，并引入了结合空间与时间违规的新型谓词鲁棒性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶运动规划常需满足多个条件冲突的规约，当无法同时满足所有规约时，需要最小违规规划。然而，规约的字典序排序导致字典序优化问题，标准方法计算代价高昂。

Method: 将多目标字典序优化问题通过非均匀量化和位移转化为单目标标量优化问题；扩展确定性模型预测路径积分（MPPI）求解器以高效求解无二次输入成本的优化问题；引入结合空间和时间违规的新型谓词鲁棒性度量。

Result: 所提方法在单目标求解器框架内提供了可解释且可扩展的字典序STL最小违规运动规划解决方案。

Conclusion: 该方法为自动驾驶中多约束条件下的最小违规运动规划提供了一种高效、可解释且可扩展的求解方案。

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous vehicles often requires satisfying multiple conditionally conflicting specifications. In situations where not all specifications can be met simultaneously, minimum-violation motion planning maintains system operation by minimizing violations of specifications in accordance with their priorities. Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for rigorously defining these specifications and enables the quantitative evaluation of their violations. However, a total ordering of specifications yields a lexicographic optimization problem, which is typically computationally expensive to solve using standard methods. We address this problem by transforming the multi-objective lexicographic optimization problem into a single-objective scalar optimization problem using non-uniform quantization and bit-shifting. Specifically, we extend a deterministic model predictive path integral (MPPI) solver to efficiently solve optimization problems without quadratic input cost. Additionally, a novel predicate-robustness measure that combines spatial and temporal violations is introduced. Our results show that the proposed method offers an interpretable and scalable solution for lexicographic STL minimum-violation motion planning within a single-objective solver framework.

</details>


### [422] [RGB-S: Image-Aligned Tactile Saliency for Robust Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08765)
*Shengcheng Luo,Kefei Wu,Xiaoying Zhou,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种显式将触觉信号投影到RGB图像平面的框架，通过力调制高斯显著性图建模空间不确定性，利用零初始化条件架构将物理接触先验注入视觉骨干网络，在六项灵巧操作任务中显著提升了遮挡条件下的操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视触觉融合方法需要从有限演示中隐式学习跨模态对应关系，缺乏几何先验，导致数据效率低且在视觉观测退化时泛化能力差。本文旨在解决稀疏异构触觉测量与稠密视觉表示之间的鲁棒对齐问题。

Method: 1) 利用机器人正向运动学和相机标定，将触觉传感器位置直接投影到RGB图像平面；2) 渲染力调制的高斯显著性图，建模由运动学和标定误差引起的空间不确定性；3) 通过零初始化条件架构将2D空间锚点注入标准视觉骨干网络，同时保留预训练的视觉表示。

Result: 在六项灵巧操作任务（仿真和真实世界）中，在严重视觉遮挡条件下，真实世界实验表明显式RGB-S图像域对齐相比最强的隐式视触觉基线，遮挡操作成功率提升了26.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式将触觉信号空间锚定到图像域能够提升空间推理能力和对遮挡的鲁棒性，为解决视触觉融合中的数据效率和泛化问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Effective visuo-tactile integration is critical for robotic dexterous manipulation, especially when visual observations are unreliable or occluded. However, robustly aligning sparse, heterogeneous tactile measurements with dense visual representations remains a fundamental challenge. Most existing approaches require policies to learn cross-modal correspondences implicitly from limited demonstrations, without leveraging geometric priors. As a result, they are often data-inefficient and generalize poorly when visual observations are degraded. To address this limitation, we propose a framework that explicitly grounds physical contacts in the image domain. Using robot forward kinematics and camera calibration, we project tactile sensor locations directly onto the RGB image plane. We then render force-modulated Gaussian saliency maps to model spatial uncertainty arising from kinematic and calibration errors. By integrating these 2D spatial anchors through a zero-initialized conditioning architecture, our method injects physical contact priors into standard visual backbones while preserving pre-trained visual representations. We evaluate our method on six dexterous manipulation tasks in both simulation and the real world under severe visual occlusions. Real-world experiments show that explicit RGB-S grounding in the image domain improves real-world occluded manipulation success rates by $26.7$ percentage points over the strongest implicit visuo-tactile baseline, suggesting its improved spatial reasoning and robustness to occlusion. Project page: touch-as-saliency.github.io

</details>


### [423] [UniDexTok: A Unified Dexterous Hand Tokenizer from Real Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10683)
*Dong Fang,Youjun Wu,Yuanxin Zhong,Rui Zhang,Yunlong Wang,Xiaosong Jia,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出统一灵巧手模型（UDHM）和基于其的状态分词器UniDexTok，将异构灵巧手状态映射到共享的22自由度假肢语义接口，实现跨形态统一表征，无需重定位或仿真数据，将关节角度误差降低98.98%、位置误差降低99.03%，达到亚毫米级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手硬件设计差异大（运动学、关节定义、自由度不同），导致数据碎片化，难以像平行夹爪那样定义共享状态表示进行联合训练。

Method: 提出UDHM将人/机器手状态映射到22-DoF共享语义接口；基于UDHM设计UniDexTok，学习受形态条件约束的离散token，直接从标准化的真实关节状态进行无重定位分词。

Result: 相比UniHM基线，MPJAE从15.63°降至0.16°（降98.98%），MPJPE从18.51mm降至0.18mm（降99.03%），从厘米级提升至亚毫米级。跨形态数据能提升目标形态重建精度，且具备零样本和少样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniDexTok实现了异构灵巧手的高精度统一状态表征，验证了跨形态分词的有效性，为灵巧手数据的联合训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dexterous hands are essential for fine-grained manipulation, but their hardware designs vary substantially across embodiments. Differences in kinematics, joint definitions, and degrees of freedom make it difficult to define a shared state representation compared with parallel grippers. As a result, dexterous-hand data remains fragmented and difficult to use for joint training. In this work, we propose the Unified Dexterous Hand Model (UDHM), which maps human and robot hand states into a shared 22-DoF semantic interface. Based on UDHM, we introduce UniDexTok, a retargeting-free state tokenizer that learns embodiment-conditioned discrete tokens from standardized real joint states. UniDexTok provides a unified representation for heterogeneous dexterous hands without relying on retargeting or simulation data. Compared with the recent baseline UniHM, UniDexTok reduces MPJAE from 15.63 degrees to 0.16 degrees and MPJPE from 18.51 mm to 0.18 mm, corresponding to error reductions of 98.98% and 99.03%, respectively. These results improve reconstruction from centimeter-scale to sub-millimeter accuracy. Experiments further show that data from other embodiments improves target-embodiment reconstruction accuracy, demonstrating the benefit of cross-embodiment tokenization. UniDexTok also shows strong zero-shot and few-shot reconstruction ability when new dexterous hands are introduced.

</details>


### [424] [RoboNaldo: Accurate, Stable and Powerful Humanoid Soccer Shooting via Motion-Guided Curriculum Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11092)
*Yichao Zhong,Yidan Lu,Yuhang Lu,Tianyang Tang,Haoguang Mai,Yixuan Pan,Tianyu Li,Li Chen,Jingbo Wang,Zhongyu Li,Peng Lu,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboNaldo，一个三阶段运动引导课程强化学习框架，用于高冲击力人形机器人踢球。通过单一人体踢球参考动作，逐步从学习全身稳定踢球先验，过渡到定点任意位置任意球，最后扩展到运动球射门。在仿真中，任意球误差降低48.6%，射门速度提升2.96倍；在真实Unitree G1机器人上，3米距离平均射门误差为0.73米（任意球）和0.86米（运动球），触球后速度达13.10 m/s，达到职业球员射门速度的59-71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两难困境：基于运动跟踪的强化学习能提供全身稳定性，但固定参考动作难以适应不同球位和击球时机；而纯任务奖励驱动的强化学习则难以从零开始探索发现有效的踢球动作。需要一种能兼顾稳定性与适应性的方法。

Method: 提出三阶段课程强化学习框架RoboNaldo：第一阶段学习全身踢球先验（基于单人踢球参考），第二阶段适应定点任意球（球随机位置），第三阶段扩展到运动球射门（通过运动指令+踢球触发接口）。训练时高层启发式规划器控制接口，推理时可用替代高层控制器驱动同一底层策略。

Result: 仿真：任意球射门误差降低48.6%，射门速度提升2.96倍。真实世界（Unitree G1）：3米距离，任意球平均误差0.73米，运动球平均误差0.86米，触球后球速13.10 m/s，达到职业球员射门速度的59-71%。

Conclusion: RoboNaldo通过运动引导的课程学习，有效解决了人形机器人高冲击力踢球中稳定性与适应性的权衡问题，在仿真和真实环境中均取得了显著优于基线方法的表现。

Abstract: Elite humanoid soccer shooting requires whole-body stability, high-impulse whole-body interactions, and accuracy to targets. Motion tracking-driven reinforcement learning (RL) provides stability in whole-body movement coordination, but a fixed reference makes it hard to adapt to varied ball positions and strike timings; in contrast, task reward-driven RL struggles to explore and discover valid kicks from scratch. We therefore introduce RoboNaldo, a three-stage motion-guided curriculum RL framework for high-impulse humanoid interaction. A single human-kick reference is used as a scaffold and progressively shifts optimization towards shooting performance. The curriculum first learns a stable whole-body kicking prior, then adapts the kick to free-kick settings where the ball is stationary at random positions, and finally extends it to moving-ball shooting through a locomotion-command and kick-trigger interface. A high-level heuristic planner controls this interface during training, while alternative high-level controllers can drive the same low-level policy at inference. In simulation, RoboNaldo demonstrates free-kick shot error 48.6% lower and shoot velocity 2.96x than prior work baselines. In real world on a Unitree G1 with onboard perception, RoboNaldo attains 0.73 m and 0.86 m average target shooting error from 3 m away in free-kick and moving-ball cases, accordingly. And the post-contact ball velocity reaches 13.10 m/s, which is 59-71% of reported professional open-play shot speed. Project page: https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo.

</details>


### [425] [Blind Dexterous Grasping via Real2Sim2Real Tactile Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11767)
*Shengcheng Luo,Xiyan Huang,Zhe Xu,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多指灵巧手的触觉盲抓取框架，通过Real2Sim触觉标定、布局感知触觉编码器和触觉条件扩散策略，在真实机器人手上实现了仅依赖触觉的抓取，无需视觉输入或真实抓取演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手的盲抓取是重要的操纵能力，但真实机器人上学习纯触觉策略面临触觉仿真到真实的差距以及稀疏触觉信号表达能力有限的挑战。

Method: 提出三组件框架：1) Real2Sim触觉标定管道，构建接触标定的数字孪生仿真器；2) 布局感知触觉编码器，通过自监督预训练融入传感器几何先验，增强稀疏触觉观测的表达能力；3) 在标定仿真器中训练物体特定的强化学习专家，聚合成功抓取轨迹形成触觉条件扩散策略。

Result: 在配备分布式触觉传感的LEAP Hand上，对10个见过和10个未见过的物体实现27%的真实世界抓取成功率；仿真消融实验证明布局感知触觉预训练提升抓取性能，Real2Sim标定增强触觉接触事件的一致性。

Conclusion: 接触事件标定、几何感知触觉表示学习和基于扩散的策略聚合为真实灵巧手上的触觉盲抓取提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Blind grasping with a dexterous hand is a crucial manipulation capability. Nevertheless, learning such tactile-only policies for real robots remains challenging due to the tactile sim-to-real gap and the limited expressiveness of sparse tactile signals. To bridge this gap, we propose a framework for tactile-only blind grasping that is deployable on a physical multi-fingered robotic hand. Our approach combines three key components. First, we introduce a Real2Sim tactile calibration pipeline that constructs a contact-calibrated digital-twin simulator capable of reproducing real tactile signals. Second, we improve the expressiveness of sparse tactile observations using a layout-aware tactile encoder, which incorporates sensor-geometry priors through self-supervised pretraining. Third, to improve generalization to unseen objects, we train object-specific reinforcement-learning experts in the calibrated simulator and aggregate their successful grasp trajectories into a tactile-conditioned Diffusion Policy. We evaluate our method on a physical LEAP Hand equipped with distributed tactile sensing across 10 seen and 10 unseen objects. The deployed policy achieves a 27\% real-world grasp success rate across all 20 objects, without real-world grasping demonstrations or visual input. Simulation ablations show that layout-aware tactile pretraining improves grasping performance, while sensing-level evaluations confirm that Real2Sim calibration increases the consistency of tactile contact events between simulation and hardware. Together, these results suggest that contact-event calibration, geometry-aware tactile representation learning, and diffusion-based policy aggregation provide an effective path toward tactile-only blind grasping on real dexterous robotic hands. Project page:Dex-Blind-Grasp.github.io.

</details>


### [426] [DrivingAgent: Design and Scheduling Agents for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12236)
*Zhongyu Xia,Wenhao Chen,Yongtao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DrivingAgent是一个面向自动驾驶的双阶段智能体框架：在设计阶段自动生成和验证系统模块，在调度阶段通过强化学习训练的轻量级LLM实现实时动态调度，在nuScenes和Bench2Drive基准上取得了更优的速度-精度平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶系统引入基础模型时面临两大挑战：手动设计和集成新模型成本高昂，且缺乏满足实时约束的动态调度机制。现有基于LLM的智能体框架不适用于自动驾驶，无法区分系统设计与实时调度的不同需求，将模块视为黑盒，且不支持持续运行。

Method: DrivingAgent包含两阶段：设计阶段通过解释系统架构、生成代码和超网络训练来自动化模块开发与验证；调度阶段使用经强化学习训练的轻量级LLM，结合长时存储与带时间戳的短时上下文的结构化记忆，实时动态编排系统模块。

Result: 在nuScenes和Bench2Drive两个基准测试上，DrivingAgent在速度与精度之间取得了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: DrivingAgent通过将系统设计自动化与实时智能调度解耦，有效解决了自动驾驶系统集成基础模型时的两大核心挑战，验证了LLM智能体在该领域的实用性。

Abstract: Many autonomous driving systems are increasingly incorporating foundation models to improve generalization and handle long-tail scenarios. However, this trend introduces two key challenges: (i) the manual and labor-intensive process of designing and integrating new models, and (ii) the lack of intelligent, dynamic scheduling mechanisms to meet strict real-time constraints. While Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents offer a promising avenue for automation, existing frameworks are ill-suited for autonomous driving. Specifically, they fail to distinguish between the fundamentally different requirements of system design and real-time scheduling, treat modules as opaque black boxes, and are not designed for continuous operation. To address these limitations, we propose DrivingAgent, a novel agent framework tailored to the dual challenges of autonomous driving system design and scheduling. In the design phase, DrivingAgent automates module development by interpreting system architecture, generating code, and validating modules via super-network training. In the scheduling phase, it employs a lightweight LLM trained with reinforcement learning to dynamically orchestrate system modules in real time, supported by a structured memory that integrates long-term storage with timestamped short-term context. Experimental results demonstrate that DrivingAgent achieves a superior speed--accuracy trade-off on both the nuScenes and Bench2Drive benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [427] [Graph Reduction in Multirelational Networks: A Spreading-Oriented Reduction Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12581)
*Mateusz Stolarski,Michał Czuba,Piotr Bielak,Piotr Bródka*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出SORB基准框架，系统评估图缩减（稀疏化与粗化）对影响力最大化任务的影响，发现缩减效果高度依赖于网络类型（单层vs多层）和评估指标（Gain@k vs AUC_cutoff）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实网络存在不完整、噪声和动态演化特性，大规模网络直接分析计算代价高。尽管影响力最大化（IM）已被广泛研究，但图缩减作为预处理步骤对IM精度的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 开发SORB（Spreading-Oriented Reduction Benchmark）开源标准化框架，集成多种真实网络（单层和多层），将图缩减直接纳入评估流程，系统比较稀疏化和粗化策略在不同IM场景下的效果。

Result: 稀疏化在单层网络上能保持种子集质量；而扁平化多层网络无论采用何种缩减策略，均表现出系统性排序退化。

Conclusion: 图缩减的影响强烈依赖于网络类型和下游任务指标，研究复杂网络传播过程时需采用缩减感知的、多任务评估方式。

Abstract: Real-world networks are inherently incomplete, noisy, and dynamically evolving, making it difficult to capture all actors and their relationships. Their scale often renders direct analysis computationally demanding. While influence maximisation (IM) has been widely studied, the role of graph reduction as a preprocessing step, and its impact on IM accuracy, remains underexplored. In this work, we introduce the Spreading-Oriented Reduction Benchmark (SORB), an open-source, standardised framework for systematically evaluating IM models across diverse task settings. SORB provides an extensible pipeline operating on a representative collection of real-world networks, including single- and multilayer structures, and accounts for graph reduction directly into the evaluation process. This design shifts the focus from analysing IM algorithms in isolation to quantifying how graph reduction alters predictive performance. Using SORB, we study the effects of sparsification and coarsening across multiple IM scenarios. Our results show that the impact of reduction is strongly dependent on both the network type (single-layer vs. multirelational) and the downstream task ($Gain@k$ vs. $\mathrm{AUC}_{\mathrm{cutoff}}$): sparsification preserves seed set quality on single-layer networks, whereas flattened multilayer networks exhibit systematic ranking degradation regardless of reduction strategy. These findings highlight the importance of reduction-aware, multi-task evaluation when studying spreading processes in complex networks.

</details>


### [428] [Orchestrating the Twin Transition in Multinational Corporations: Technology Roadmapping for Green and Digital Global Business Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12787)
*Han-Teng Liao,Karen Ang*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文通过将技术路线图（TRM）与国际电信联盟（ITU）ICT创新生态系统工具包相结合，构建了一个社会技术框架，研究全球商业服务（GBS）如何作为绿色与数字化转型的“活实验室”。文献计量分析揭示了从流程自动化到“可持续智能”的演进，并识别出GBS单元作为连接宏观压力与利基创新的“操作气闸”。研究还发现波兰、葡萄牙和马来西亚等“中等强国”枢纽正绕过中等收入陷阱，在分裂的地缘政治格局中为全球价值链提供“第三条道路”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨国企业在数字化转型与绿色转型的双重压力下，需要协调数字效率与环境责任，而GBS作为“活实验室”为这一双重转型提供了独特的研究场景。现有研究缺乏将技术路线图与ICT创新生态系统整合的社会技术框架，且对GBS在双转型中的中介角色认识不足。

Method: 采用技术路线图（TRM）与国际电信联盟（ITU）ICT创新生态系统工具包的综合方法，通过文献计量分析识别研究集群，并将集群映射到利益相关者参与画布上。

Result: 识别出从基本流程自动化向“可持续智能”的演进转变；发现GBS单元作为“操作气闸”在连接欧盟双重指令、碳边境调节机制等宏观压力与AI原生工作流程等利基创新之间的中介作用；识别出波兰、葡萄牙、马来西亚等“中等强国”枢纽正绕过中等收入陷阱，为全球价值链提供替代路径。

Conclusion: 该研究为领导者和创业支持网络提供了数据驱动的设计方法，用于协调人才和供应链流动，丰富了工业5.0的概念理解，并明确了GBS在应对多变、多极数字经济中的主要机制角色。

Abstract: Global Business Services (GBS) have emerged as a "living laboratory" for the Twin Transition of Green and Digital Transformation, as multinational corporations (MNCs) face increasing pressure to harmonize digital efficiency with environmental stewardship. Aiming to derive a socio-technical framework, this paper synthesizes Technology Roadmapping (TRM) with the International Telecommunication Union (ITU) ICT-centric innovation ecosystem toolkit. A bibliometric analysis of research clusters reveals an evolutionary shift from basic process automation toward "Sustainable Intelligence," identifying the GBS unit as a central "operational airlock" that mediates between landscape pressures -- such as the EU's dual mandate and Carbon Border Adjustment Mechanisms -- and niche innovations in AI-native workflows. The study further maps these clusters onto a stakeholder engagement canvas, highlighting how resilient "Middle Power" hubs in Poland, Portugal, and Malaysia are bypassing the middle-income trap to provide a "third way" for global value chains amidst a bifurcated geopolitical cloud. The results offer a data-driven design approach for leaders and entrepreneurial support networks to orchestrate talent and supply chain flows, thereby enriching the conceptual understanding of Industry 5.0 and the role of GBS as a primary mechanism for navigating a volatile, multipolar digital economy.

</details>


### [429] [To Share or Not to Share: Orchestrating Trustworthy Data in Global Value Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12788)
*Han-Teng Liao,Chang-Yi Kao*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于国际数据空间（IDSA）框架的RegTech参考架构，用于协调半导体-石化产业链的环境遥测，通过数字产品护照（DPP）和主权数据交换实现CBAM合规、SBTi自愿框架及SSbD安全可持续设计，并在台北-槟城技术走廊验证了该架构对全球业务服务（GBS）能力需求的驱动作用，最后探讨了Agentic AI自主合规与FinTech绿色融资的整合路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟CBAM机制即将实施，全球半导体价值链面临监管透明度与数据主权之间的结构性张力，现有技术方案无法同时满足强制性CBAM要求、自愿性SBTi框架以及SSbD安全可持续设计框架的叠加复杂性。

Method: 采用IDSA国际数据空间框架构建RegTech参考架构；引入前瞻性路线图方法将上游物理脆弱性转化为循环负反馈回路；以台北-槟城技术走廊为实证场景，通过主权数据交换实现DPP数字产品护照对GBS全球业务服务能力需求的驱动；集成Agentic AI自主合规与FinTech绿色金融机制。

Result: 提出了可区分CBAM强制要求与SBTi自愿框架的RegTech架构，解决了SSbD框架的附加复杂性，在台北-槟城走廊验证了主权数据交换驱动DPP及GBS能力需求的有效性，并形成了Agentic AI与FinTech整合的可扩展蓝图。

Conclusion: 该架构为全球工业集群实现主权化、可持续化、透明化的价值链提供了可扩展蓝图，有效解决了监管透明度与数据主权之间的结构性矛盾。

Abstract: As the EU Carbon Border Adjustment Mechanism (CBAM) approaches, the global semiconductor value chain faces growing structural tensions between regulatory transparency and data sovereignty. This article proposes a RegTech reference architecture using the International Data Spaces (IDSA) framework to orchestrate trustworthy environmental telemetry across the semiconductor-petrochemical nexus. The framework distinguishes the mandatory CBAM requirements from voluntary Science Based Targets initiative (SBTi) frameworks, while addressing the additive complexities of the Safe-and-Sustainable-by-Design (SSbD) framework. Moving beyond standard linear technology stacks, we introduce a prospective roadmapping methodology that transforms upstream physical vulnerabilities into circular, negative feedback loops. Focusing on the Taipei and Penang technology corridor, the article details how sovereign data exchange enables Digital Product Passports (DPPs) to drive Global Business Services (GBSs) capability demands. Finally, we discuss the integration of Agentic AI for autonomous compliance and FinTech green financing, providing a scalable blueprint for global industrial clusters to achieve sovereign, sustainable, and transparent value chains.

</details>


### [430] [MIDSim: Simulating Multi-Channel Information Diffusion in Social Media with LLM-Powered Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13140)
*Lexi Liu,Qi Cao,Yuanhao Liu,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的多智能体系统，用于模拟社交媒体中多通道信息扩散（同时考虑社交链接和算法推荐），并在三个真实平台数据集上验证了其优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息扩散模型（如流行病模型、级联模型、点过程模型）主要关注社交链接传播，忽视了平台算法（如推荐系统）带来的其他扩散通道，且未能捕捉用户行为的复杂性。

Method: 构建LLM驱动的多智能体系统：用大语言模型实例化个性化用户智能体，扩散过程联合建模社交曝光流和算法曝光流；并在微博、小红书、Twitter三个平台构建包含扩散记录、用户画像、历史帖子、社交关系的真实数据集。

Result: 在真实扩散事件上，该方法在宏观扩散现象模拟和多样化评论内容生成方面显著优于基线模型。

Conclusion: LLM多智能体系统能够更逼真地模拟多通道信息扩散，弥补了传统模型忽视算法推荐通道和用户行为复杂性的不足。

Abstract: Information diffusion in social media shapes public opinion and collective behavior, making its modeling and simulation an important research problem. Existing studies have investigated information diffusion through epidemic-based, cascade-based, and point process models. However, they predominantly focus on diffusion through social links, overlooking other diffusion channels enabled by platform algorithms (e.g., recommender systems) and failing to capture user behavioral complexity. To address these limitations, we propose an LLM-powered multi-agent system for simulating multi-channel information diffusion, where large language models instantiate personalized user agents and the diffusion process jointly models social and algorithmic exposure streams. We further construct three real-world diffusion dataset spanning Sina Weibo, RedNote, and Twitter, containing diffusion records, user profiles, historical posts, and social relationships. Experimental results on real diffusion events show that our proposed framework realistically simulate macro diffusion phenomenon and generate diverse comment content, significantly outperforming baselines.

</details>


### [431] [Ollivier-Ricci curvature in cycle overlap mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03317)
*Zexian Zhou,Bo Jiao*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最优传输原理的Ollivier-Ricci曲率近似计算方法CCOM（Cycle Overlap Mode），通过利用包含边(x,y)的3,4,5-环来最小化传输距离，并采用贪心剪枝算法进行近似，在保持低时间复杂度的同时显著提升了曲率计算精度，并在社区检测任务中验证了其在大型无标度图上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Ollivier-Ricci曲率计算方法存在计算复杂度高或误差大的问题，限制了其在大型无标度图中的应用，因此需要一种兼顾精度和效率的曲率近似方法。

Method: 提出最优传输原则，通过利用包含目标边(x,y)的3,4,5-环来最小化传输距离；设计了CCOM曲率计算方法，并采用贪心与剪枝算法来近似最优传输原则。

Result: 理论上和实验上验证了CCOM方法在真实网络中以低时间消耗显著提高了曲率精度；在基于曲率的社区检测任务中，与基线近似方法相比，CCOM在大型无标度图上表现更优。

Conclusion: CCOM方法能有效平衡Ollivier-Ricci曲率计算的精度与效率，适用于大规模无标度图的社区检测等下游任务。

Abstract: Ollivier-Ricci curvature of an edge (x,y) is defined by comparing the distance taken to transport from neighbors of x to neighbors of y. It is a structural measure that has been studied in many fields such as community detection and deep neural networks. However, high computational complexity or error limits its application in large scale-free graphs. This paper proposes an optimal transport principle to minimize the distance by 3,4,5-cycles that include the edge (x,y), and designs a curvature calculation approach named Curvature in Cycle Overlap Mode (CCOM). In this approach, a greedy and pruning algorithm is proposed to approximate the optimal transport principle. We theoretically and experimentally verified that our approach CCOM can significantly improve the accuracy of the curvature on real-world networks with low time consumption. In addition, we compared CCOM with baseline approximation approaches in community detection tasks using the same curvature-based framework, and experimentally confirmed the effectiveness of CCOM on large scale-free graphs.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [432] [A quantum-like benchmark for context-sensitive associative memory with adaptive plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12449)
*Yashine H. Goolam Hossen,Lea Gassab,Travis J. A. Craddock*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个顺序敏感的自适应可塑性基准测试，用于评估联想记忆模型在弱结构支持下的表现。研究发现，量子类模型在保持顺序敏感性和阶段依赖组织方面更稳定，但马尔可夫率控制在原始回忆强度上更强。模型类别的优劣应通过多目标概况（结合回忆、时间组织和上下文敏感性）而非单一回忆分数来区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联想记忆模型难以区分真正的回忆动态与固定背景连接提供的结构性辅助，需要更严格的基准来测试模型在弱支持条件下的真实学习能力。

Method: 设计了一个顺序敏感的自适应可塑性基准，比较量子类联想记忆模型与匹配的实数无相位和马尔可夫率控制模型，在相同任务调度、扰动剖面、弱支持条件和可塑性设置下的表现。

Result: 弱支持区间狭窄且非单调；无可塑性消融实验中弱结构无法挽救回忆；大多数有用的回忆提升来自自适应可塑性，尤其是稳态稳定化；马尔可夫率控制模型在原始回忆强度上更强，但量子类模型在顺序敏感性和阶段依赖性组织方面更一致。

Conclusion: 没有证据支持量子类模型的普遍优势；模型类别应通过结合回忆、时间组织和上下文敏感性的多目标概况来区分，而非单一回忆分数；该基准为研究上下文敏感记忆动力学提供了可控框架。

Abstract: Learning and memory require a balance between plasticity and stability: synaptic connections must encode new information without collapsing, saturating, or erasing previously useful structure. Associative-memory models can appear to learn successfully when fixed background connectivity already carries part of the task, making it difficult to distinguish genuine recall dynamics from structural assistance. We test this issue using an order-sensitive adaptive-plasticity benchmark for staged associative recall. The benchmark compares a quantum-like associative-memory model with matched real-valued no-phase and Markov-rate controls under the same task schedule, perturbation profiles, weak-support conditions, and plasticity settings. Here, "quantum-like" refers to the modeling formalism, not to a biological claim about quantum computation. We first screen weak structural support and then fix a conservative operating point for factorial comparisons across model families and plasticity mechanisms. The useful weak-support regime is narrow and non-monotonic. Weak structure alone does not rescue recall in the no-plasticity ablation, whereas most useful recall gains arise from adaptive plasticity, especially homeostatic stabilization. The Markov-rate control often achieves stronger raw recall, but the quantum-like model more consistently preserves order sensitivity and stage-dependent organization. These results do not support a universal quantum-like advantage. Instead, they show that model classes are better distinguished by a multi-objective profile combining recall, temporal organization, and context sensitivity than by any single recall score. The benchmark therefore provides a controlled framework for studying context-sensitive memory dynamics under weak support, regulated plasticity, and matched classical comparison.

</details>


### [433] [Multifractal human signals at the edge of life reveal a heart-brain anti-correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12600)
*Yago Emanoel Ramos,Maria Eloá do Ó,Henrique Ferraz de Arruda,Mauro Copelli,G. Camelo-Neto,Pedro V. Carelli*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过MF-DFA分析临终患者同步脑电图和心电图时间序列，发现神经活动多重分形谱收窄（复杂度降低），而心脏信号谱异常展宽（波动不稳定性增加），两者呈负相关，提示神经-心脏系统功能性解耦，死亡过程表现为层级协调崩溃的跨系统解体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人体生理功能在终末阶段的崩溃机制，理解死亡过程中神经与心脏系统动力学的非线性演化规律及系统间协调关系的瓦解过程。

Method: 采用多重分形去趋势波动分析（MF-DFA），量化临终患者同步EEG和ECG时间序列中复杂性的时间演化，计算多重分形谱宽度并分析神经与心脏信号谱宽度的相关性。

Result: 神经活动多重分形谱收窄（向更受限状态塌缩），心脏信号谱异常展宽（非线性波动增加、动力学不稳定性增强）；两者谱宽度呈负相关，表明功能性解耦和反相关动力学出现。

Conclusion: 临终过程并非均匀的生理衰退，而是体-脑崩解（body-to-brain breakdown）过程，外周功能障碍逐步压倒中枢调控机制；死亡是跨系统整合解体的极端形式，表现为维持整体生理功能的层级协调的崩溃。

Abstract: This study investigates the terminal breakdown of human neurophysiological function through the lens of non-linear dynamics by analyzing the multifractal spectrum. Using Multifractal Detrended Fluctuation Analysis (MF-DFA), we quantify the temporal evolution of complexity in synchronized electroencephalogram (EEG) and electrocardiogram (ECG) time series from patients in the terminal stage. Our results reveal a marked divergence in multifractal spectrum width: while neural activity exhibits a collapse of multifractality toward a more constrained state, cardiac signals undergo anomalous spectral broadening, indicating increased non-linear fluctuations and dynamical instability. A negative correlation between these spectral widths suggests effective functional decoupling and the emergence of anti-correlated dynamics between neural and cardiac systems. Rather than reflecting a uniform physiological decline, this divergence is consistent with a body-to-brain breakdown in which peripheral dysfunction progressively overwhelms central regulatory processes. In a broader context, the observed opposing trends resemble patterns reported in other body-driven adaptive processes, suggesting that inverse dynamics across coupled systems may emerge when constraints originate from peripheral rather than central mechanisms. Ultimately, the dying process appears to represent an extreme form of cross-system disintegration, marked by the collapse of the hierarchical coordination that normally sustains integrated physiological function.

</details>


### [434] [Phase model analysis of the effect of M-current on neural synchrony in hippocampal networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12684)
*Megha Manoj,Sue Ann Campbell*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过构建包含M电流的锥体神经元网络模型，分析了乙酰胆碱（ACh）水平对神经集群形成的影响。在低ACh条件下网络可完全同步（支持记忆巩固），高ACh条件下网络解同步为多个稳定对称集群（支持记忆编码），揭示了ACh双向调控记忆编码与巩固的神经机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索乙酰胆碱如何通过调节神经元同步性，在海马体中双向调控记忆编码（高ACh）与记忆巩固（低ACh）的神经机制。

Method: 构建含M电流（受ACh下调）的锥体神经元网络模型，通过弱耦合相模型降维分析，在全局均匀、对称距离依赖和最近邻三种耦合架构下预测对称集群解的存在性。

Result: 低ACh条件下网络可实现完全同步；高ACh条件下网络解同步为多个稳定对称集群，代表不同的神经集群。

Conclusion: ACh通过调节M电流调控神经元同步性，高ACh促进神经集群分化（记忆编码），低ACh促进网络同步（记忆巩固），为ACh双向调控记忆的假说提供了计算层面的支持。

Abstract: Neural assemblies, transiently coordinated groups of neurons, observed in the hippocampus are thought to underlie the formation of episodic memories. Acetylcholine (ACh), a neuromodulator, that is received by the hippocampus, plays a critical role in memory and learning. A well supported hypothesis suggests that high levels of ACh during active exploration and rapid eye movement (REM) sleep promote memory encoding, while low levels during quiet waking and slow-wave sleep (SWS) support memory consolidation.
